### [1] [EmbodiedMidtrain: Bridging the Gap between Vision-Language Models and Vision-Language-Action Models via Mid-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20012)
*Du, Yiyang,Guo, Zhanqiu,Zhang, Haochen,Macaluso, Annabella,Ye, Xin,Ren, Liu,Bisk, Yonatan,Xiong, Chenyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EmbodiedMidtrain：先刻画 VLM 与 VLA 数据分布差异，再用可学习的近邻估计器从大规模 VLM 池中筛选与 VLA 对齐的样本，构建 EmbodiedMidtrain-1M 用于 VLM 中间训练，再接入 VLA 微调，在仿真与真机上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 多直接复用通用 VLM 权重，未针对具身域适配，VLM 与 VLA 数据分布存在明显鸿沟，导致下游性能受限。

Method: 分析 VLM/VLA 数据分布差异（VLA 数据集中于紧凑区域，跨数据源及源内对齐程度差异大）；构建中间训练数据引擎，用轻量可学习"近邻/对齐度估计器"从大 VLM 池筛出最贴近 VLA 分布的候选，得到 1M 规模数据集 EmbodiedMidtrain-1M；在 VLA 微调前对 VLM 做中期训练，跨多种 VLM 主干与动作头验证。

Result: 在仿真机器人操作基准与真实任务上，相对 off-the-shelf 检查点均获得一致提升，且泛化到未参与数据选择的下游任务；增益从训练极早期即出现并随训练扩大；数据引擎同时捕捉数据集级与样本级对齐信号，偏好空间推理任务而非纯文本任务，并保持 VLM 数据多样性。

Conclusion: VLM 与 VLA 之间的分布鸿沟可通过数据筛选式中期训练有效弥合；该中期训练提供了更强的 VLA 微调初始化，且代码、数据与模型将开源，具可复现性与工程价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) inherit their visual and linguistic capabilities from Vision-Language Models (VLMs), yet most VLAs are built from off-the-shelf VLMs that are not adapted to the embodied domain, limiting their downstream performance. In this work, we propose EmbodiedMidtrain to bridge the gap between VLMs and VLAs. We first characterize the data distribution gap between them, showing that VLA data occupy compact regions that are largely separated from the broader VLM distribution, while the degree of alignment varies substantially both across and within VLM data sources. Then, we build a mid-training data engine that leverages a lightweight learnable proximity estimator to select the most VLA-aligned candidates from a large VLM pool, and produce EmbodiedMidtrain-1M, a VLM dataset that we further use to mid-train the VLM before downstream VLA fine-tuning. Experiments on both simulated robot manipulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show consistent VLA performance gains over off-the-shelf checkpoints across VLM backbones and VLA action heads. These gains extend to downstream tasks not used to guide data selection. Further analysis reveals that mid-training provides a stronger initialization for VLA fine-tuning, with gains emerging from the earliest steps and widening throughout training. Moreover, the data engine captures both dataset-level and sample-level alignment signals, favoring spatial reasoning over text-centric tasks while preserving the diversity of the VLM data. We will release all code, data and models for future research.

</details>


### [2] [Event-STVG: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding for Long-Form Event Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22444)
*Zhang, Yizhe,Wang, Xujia,Tang, Songqi,Tang, Xujie,Jiang, Leo Jiahao,Wang, Jiacheng,Qu, Bowen,Li, JinBo,Wang, Qin,Qi, Ji,Hou, Lei,Xu, Bin,Li, Juanzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Event-STVG 任务与基准，将时空视频定位从"定位所描述实例"转向事件级推理，统一长上下文、重复事件推理、事件定位、角色推断与多目标定位；构建 4,016 个事件标注、跨足球等 8 个领域的基准，最强 MLLM 在 10 分钟时域上 m-vIoU 仅 5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 STVG 基准主要依赖实例特定描述定位目标，与 MLLM 日益强调的长文本推理相脱节；真实场景需要从长视频中推理复杂事件、区分同类重复事件、推断角色并同时定位多目标。

Method: 定义 Event-STVG 任务，将长上下文理解、重复事件区分、事件级时空定位、角色推断与多目标 grounding 统一为一个任务；构建覆盖足球及另外七个领域、共 4,016 个事件发生实例的大规模标注基准，并对当前 SOTA MLLM 进行系统性评测与错误分析。

Result: 现有 MLLM 表现显著不足，最强模型在 10 分钟时域下 m-vIoU 仅 5.4%；跨领域一致出现同类重复事件混淆、空间定位瓶颈，以及难以同时定位全部所需角色的问题。

Conclusion: Event-STVG 为多模态大模型视觉理解建立了新的挑战，指向需要跨时空联合推理复杂事件的未来模型；论文本身以基准与评测诊断为主，未提供可迁移的方法创新。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG) aims to jointly localize a target in space and time given a natural-language query. Despite continued advances, existing STVG benchmarks remain largely centered on localizing targets through instance-specific descriptions, leaving them largely decoupled from long-form reasoning increasingly emphasized in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In this paper, we introduce Event-STVG, a task that shifts STVG from locating what is described toward event-level reasoning by unifying long-form context, repeated-event reasoning, event localization, role inference, and multi-target grounding. To facilitate research on Event-STVG, we construct a large-scale benchmark comprising 4,016 annotated event occurrences across soccer and seven additional domains. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal substantial difficulty, with the strongest model achieving only 5.4% m-vIoU at the 10-minute horizon. Across domains, we consistently observe confusion among repeated same-type events, spatial grounding bottlenecks, and rare success in jointly grounding all required roles. Event-STVG establishes a new challenge for visual understanding in MLLMs, calling for future models that can jointly reason about complex events across space and time. Code and annotations are available at https://github.com/THU-KEG/EventSTVG

</details>


### [3] [Star Light, Star Bright: Can Multimodal Models Parse an Astronomy Visual Question Answering Dataset Right?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22474)
*Naiman, J. P.*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建天文天空图像领域专用VQA数据集（2001张图：等高线图、NASA SkyView真实望远镜图、高斯混合模型生成的"假"天空图，含47357问），并测试三款低成本专有LMM，发现其在领域问题上准确率普遍偏低，中位角度尺度误差与人类验证历史文献图相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像是科学交流的核心载体，需评估大型多模态模型（LMM）对科学图形的理解能力，尤其在天文学等专业领域，低成本模型的实际可用性缺乏系统量化。

Method: 构建领域VQA数据集：2001张图三类均分（等高线图、SkyView真实望远镜图、共享坐标轴与角尺度但色彩来自高斯混合模型的伪造天空图），共47357问；每图提供原始版本与模拟历史文献页面老化退化的版本。对3个低成本专有LMM评测，并与104张人工核验、含天体测量标注的历史期刊图做中位角尺度误差对比。

Result: 低成本LMM在领域特定问题上频繁失败，难以达到高准确率；其角尺度估计中位误差与人类/历史标注基线相近，说明模型在精细空间度量上并不优于传统参照。

Conclusion: 科学图像理解对当前低成本LMM仍是显著短板，需领域专用数据与评测；代码与数据集已开源，可作为天文VQA与文档退化鲁棒性的基准。

Abstract: Figures are essential for scientific communication, and the field of Visual Question Answering (VQA) aims to test the figure understanding capabilities of large multimodal models (LMMs). Here, we investigate the accuracy of several low-cost LMMs on domain-specific VQA tasks for astronomical images of the sky. As instrument for this task we build a domain-specific VQA dataset comprising 2001 partially-synthetic figures divided evenly between contour plots, real telescope images drawn from NASA's SkyView, and "fake" sky images which share the axes and angular scales of the real images but draw their colors from a gaussian mixture model, alongside 47357 questions. Each figure is released as pristine and with realistic page-aging degradations mimicking the historical astronomical corpus. Testing three low-cost proprietary LMMs, we find models frequently fail to answer domain-specific questions at high accuracy. An initial comparison with 104 human-verified, astrometrically tagged figures from historical journal publications shows similar median errors in angular extent. All code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/jnaiman/SkyImagesWASP2026 and dataset is available on HuggingFace: https://huggingface.co/datasets/ReadingTimeMachine/astro_sky_image_vqa.

</details>


### [4] [JAVIS: Joint Audio-Visual Interaction and Streaming for Real-Time Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22499)
*Ahn, Junseok,Park, Jaehyun,Choi, Jeongsoo,Chung, Joon Son,Kim, Ji-Hoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JAVIS: 端到端流式音视频对话智能体框架，通过共享LLM上的Joint Refined Head生成统一音视频表征，再分叉到语音与面部流式分支，实现实时同步的音画生成；并提出334小时合成音视频对话语料JAVIS-Syn。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话系统仅覆盖文本或语音，缺乏自然交互所需的视觉反馈；且缺少富表现力的音视频对话数据。多模态人类交流需要语言、声音与视觉信号协同。

Method: 基于共享LLM主干，加入Joint Refined Head输出统一音视频表征，分别送入语音生成与面部生成两个专用流式分支；端到端训练避免中间表征瓶颈，保持音画紧同步；构建334小时合成语料JAVIS-Syn（含情感丰富多轮对话与助手式交流）。

Result: 初始延迟较强基线降低约200倍；在唇形同步与面部表情恰当性上优于基线。

Conclusion: 统一音视频表征+端到端流式架构可支撑实时、同步的音视频对话智能体，合成语料可缓解数据稀缺。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, conveying intent not only through linguistic and vocal cues but also through visual signals. However, existing conversational systems have mainly focused on text or speech, leaving out the visual feedback that is essential for natural interaction. In this paper, we propose JAVIS, an end-to-end streaming framework for real-time audio-visual conversational agents. Our key idea is to let the voice and the face speak from a single representation. On top of a shared LLM, a Joint Refined Head produces a unified audio-visual representation, which is fed into dedicated streaming branches for speech and face generation. This end-to-end design avoids intermediate bottlenecks, enabling real-time generation while keeping speech and face tightly synchronized. In addition, we present JAVIS-Syn, a 334-hour synthetic corpus designed to address the lack of expressive audio-visual dialogue data, covering emotionally rich multi-turn conversations and assistant-style exchanges. Experiments show that JAVIS achieves approximately 200$\times$ lower initial latency than a strong baseline, while outperforming baselines in lip synchronization and facial expression appropriateness.

</details>


### [5] [KFS$^4$LAM: Adaptive KeyFrame Selection with Structure-Aware Mapping for Endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22505)
*Ju, Xinwei,Mao, Zhehua,Song, Tianyi,Stoyanov, Danail,Bano, Sophia,Vasconcelos, Francisco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KFS$^4$LAM：面向内窥镜高斯泼溅SLAM的运动引导自适应关键帧选择＋结构监督建图框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯SLAM的定位、重建与渲染质量高度依赖保留哪些观测用于建图。现有系统多用固定时间间隔或简单位姿/重叠度准则选关键帧；在内窥镜场景中，非均匀相机运动、重复解剖结构、高光与视觉伪影使这些准则失效，导致冗余关键帧或漏掉有信息量的视角。

Method: 累积运动量引导自适应关键帧选择，并用互补线索抑制冗余与不可靠候选；密度感知建图配合解剖轮廓（结肠皱褶）监督，强化结构信息区域；引入受限时序形变以容纳局部组织动态并避免无约束几何漂移。

Result: 在C3VD与C3VD-v2上与Gaussian-SLAM基线及跨主干、组件消融对比；在更难的C3VD-v2上与SplaTAM同条件比较，旋转相对位姿误差降低20.3%、深度RMSE降低38.4%、PSNR提升0.78 dB；跨主干实验表明框架不绑定单一SLAM架构。

Conclusion: 关键帧选择是高斯SLAM在内窥镜场景下性能的关键瓶颈，运动驱动选择＋结构先验监督＋受限形变可在轨迹、渲染和深度上同时取得增益，且具架构通用性。

Abstract: Gaussian-splatting Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) augments conventional SLAM with a dense, renderable 3D scene representation, enabling high-quality reconstruction and novel-view synthesis. However, its localization, reconstruction, and rendering performance depends strongly on which observations are retained for mapping. Existing systems commonly rely on fixed temporal intervals or simple pose- and overlap-based criteria for keyframe selection. In colonoscopy, non-uniform camera motion, repetitive anatomy, specularities, and visual artifacts can make these criteria unreliable, leading to either redundant keyframes or missed informative viewpoints. We present KFS$^4$LAM (KeyFrame Selection with Structure-aware mapping for endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM), which couples motion-guided adaptive keyframe selection with structurally supervised mapping and restricted temporal deformation. Accumulated motion guides adaptive keyframe selection, with complementary cues suppressing redundant or unreliable candidates. Density-aware mapping with anatomical contour supervision (colon folds) emphasizes structurally informative regions, while restricted temporal deformation accommodates local tissue dynamics without unconstrained geometric drift. We evaluate KFS$^4$LAM against Gaussian-SLAM baselines on C3VD and C3VD-v2, together with cross-backbone and component ablations. KFS$^4$LAM achieves strong overall performance across trajectory, rendering, and depth metrics, with pronounced gains on more challenging C3VD-v2 dataset. In the matched comparison with SplaTAM on C3VD-v2, it reduces rotational relative pose error by 20.3% and depth RMSE by 38.4%, while improving PSNR by 0.78 dB. Cross-backbone experiments further show that the framework is not tied to a single underlying SLAM architecture. Code and visualization results are available at https://xinweiju.github.io/KFSLAM/.

</details>


### [6] [TriSAR-3D and PaintFusion: A Novel Trimodal LiDAR-RGB-Thermal Dataset and Method for 3D Person Detection in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22509)
*Kramer, Markus,Wachenfeld, Jan,von Stryk, Oskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态救援场景人物检测数据集TriSAR-3D（128线LiDAR+RGB+热成像，涵盖躺卧、遮挡、室内外、昼夜等姿态差异），并提出PaintFusion三模态融合检测器，以MLP融合PointPainting证据，取得65.4 AP@50并显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D检测研究多面向车载场景，行人多为直立、近路姿态；救援机器人场景中人员可能躺卧、部分遮挡、处于非结构化室内外环境，现有方法的性能可迁移性未知。

Method: 构建TriSAR-3D多模态数据集（高分辨率128通道LiDAR、RGB相机、热成像相机，覆盖室内外、不同时段与多种姿态）；提出PaintFusion：基于PointPainting将图像/热成像语义证据绘至点云，再用简单MLP进行三模态融合的3D检测流程。

Result: PaintFusion达65.4 AP@50，超过LiDAR-only CenterPoint（53.4）、RGB+LiDAR BEVFusion（60.5）及适配的三模态Tri-BEVFusion（60.7）；单帧延迟51.6 ms对比BEVFusion的101 ms。训练集去除躺卧实例后，所有检测器躺卧姿态AP@50近乎归零。

Conclusion: 三模态融合在救援场景人物3D检测中兼具精度与效率优势；数据集多样性（尤其姿态多样性）对3D检测性能影响巨大，缺乏特定姿态训练数据会导致该姿态检测完全失效。

Abstract: Reliable 3D person detection is important for rescue robotics to give first responders situational awareness. Yet most multimodal 3D detection research targets automotive scenarios in which pedestrians mostly appear in a narrow range of upright, near-road poses. It is therefore unclear how much of the reported performance transfers to rescue settings, as a person may be lying, partially occluded, or in unstructured indoor and outdoor environments. To investigate this gap, we introduce TriSAR-3D, a multimodal person detection dataset recorded with a high-resolution 128-channel LiDAR, an RGB camera, and a thermal camera. It captures varied poses in indoor as well as outdoor locations at different times of day to study their influence on 3D object detector performance. Furthermore, we propose a novel trimodal 3D detection pipeline, PaintFusion, that fuses PointPainting-based evidence using a simple MLP. We show that the derived method reaches 65.4 AP@50 and thereby outperforms strong LiDAR-only (CenterPoint, 53.4), RGB+LiDAR (BEVFusion, 60.5), and adapted trimodal (Tri-BEVFusion, 60.7) baselines on the dataset. At the same time, it is substantially faster than other fusion-based methods such as BEVFusion, with a per-frame latency of 51.6 ms compared to 101 ms. Additionally, we test how the performance of different 3D object detectors generalizes to unseen pose categories. Removing lying instances from training data collapses the lying-pose AP@50 to near zero across all evaluated detectors. The results indicate that dataset diversity exerts a large effect on 3D object detection performance. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/MarkusKramer1/3D-Trimodal-People-Detection.

</details>


### [7] [Towards Reconstructing Geographically Diverse Architecture with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22514)
*Kriplani, Nicky,Zhang, Yiwen,Wang, Angelina,Averbuch-Elor, Hadar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArchWorld：面向地理覆盖与元数据丰富的3D建筑地标基准，用于系统分析前馈式3D基础模型（3DFM）的误差来源，揭示地理区域性能差异并给出改进的干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的真实地标图像虽推动了3D视觉进展，但单次前馈估计场景结构的3DFM仍在此类场景上表现困难；同时模型误差在不同地理区域间存在差异，其成因尚不清楚，缺乏系统性诊断与公平性分析。

Method: 构建ArchWorld基准（高地理覆盖、含丰富元数据的3D建筑地标）；在该基准上对当前3DFM做详尽误差分析，考察模型误差与地理位置的相关性，并将区域差异归因于数据表示、场景尺度、区域特有建筑特征与背景干扰物等因素；基于分析所得失效模式，提出一种干预方案以提升性能并缩小地理差距。

Result: 发现模型性能随地理区域变化，且趋势仅部分与训练数据表示一致；地理差异不仅源于数据表示与场景规模，也源于区域特有建筑特征和背景干扰物导致的内在难度差异；所提干预方案同时提升3DFM性能并降低地理差异。

Conclusion: 地标级前馈3D重建的误差具有可归因的结构性来源，基准驱动的误差分析与针对性干预可同时改善精度与地理公平性，为3DFM的稳健化与数据/训练改进提供方向。

Abstract: Internet-scale photo collections of real-world landmarks have driven progress in 3D computer vision yet remain highly challenging for modern 3D foundation models (3DFMs) that estimate scene structure in a single feed-forward pass. In this work, we introduce ArchWorld, a benchmark of 3D architectural landmarks with high geographic coverage and rich metadata. We use this benchmark to conduct a thorough error analysis of current 3DFMs to understand where they fall short and how they can be improved. We examine socially relevant disparities in model error and find that model performance varies by geographic region; however, unlike typical fairness regimes, the trends only partially match training data representation. Our investigation reveals that the observed geographic disparities reflect not only differences in data representation and scene size but also differences in region-specific architectural features and background distractors that make scenes from different regions inherently vary in difficulty. Leveraging insights from these model failings, we introduce an intervention scheme that improves 3DFM performance while simultaneously reducing geographic disparities.

</details>


### [8] [Learning the Shared Latent Structure of Cryo-EM Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22523)
*Chalapathi, Nithin,Sternlieb, Theo,Han, Ethan,Hanson, Sonya M.,Cossio, Pilar,Krishnapriyan, Aditi S.,Gupta, Ritwik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EMber系列ViT，利用全局+掩码局部补丁的自蒸馏目标在5000万+实验颗粒图像与模拟投影上预训练，作为跨cryo-EM多个逆问题（位姿估计、异质性与重构）的统一表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cryo-EM颗粒图像中分子结构、位姿与成像条件纠缠编码，位姿估计、异质性分析与重构通常被独立求解，缺乏共享的表示；且生物上有意义的差异常是空间局部的，需密集空间特征而非仅全局判别。

Method: 自蒸馏框架下组合全局与掩码补丁（masked patch）互补目标；构建受控合成与实验评测以排除数据采集特异性线索的干扰；ViT在214个EMPIAR数据集的>50M实验颗粒图像及268,000个分子组装体的模拟投影上预训练。

Result: 构象与组分判别分别较现有方法提升14.85与14.26个百分点；线性探针估计构象群组比例误差1.59pp以内；粒子分配可在更少epoch内达到参考质量重构。

Conclusion: 通过密集空间特征驱动的自蒸馏预训练可获得跨多个cryo-EM逆问题复用的生物信息表示，且需受控评测以验证真实结构判别能力而非采集伪线索。

Abstract: Understanding biomolecular function requires characterizing three-dimensional structure and conformational variability, for which cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) is a powerful tool. Cryo-EM produces noisy two-dimensional particle images in which molecular structure, particle pose, and imaging conditions are jointly encoded. Recovering biological information from these observations spans inverse problems including pose estimation, heterogeneity analysis, and reconstruction. These tasks are usually addressed independently despite shared protein structure and image formation, motivating a common representation across inverse problems. Since biologically meaningful differences may be spatially localized, we study self-distillation with complementary global and masked patch objectives. Because experimental dataset discrimination can reflect acquisition-specific cues, we develop controlled synthetic and experimental evaluations and find that structural discrimination benefits from dense spatial features. We introduce the EMber family of vision transformers, pre-trained on >50 million experimental particle images from 214 EMPIAR datasets together with simulated projections from 268,000 molecular assemblies. EMber improves upon existing methods at conformational and compositional discrimination by 14.85 and 14.26 percentage points, estimates conformational populations to within 1.59pp error with a linear probe, and produces particle assignments yielding reference-quality reconstructions in fewer epochs. We demonstrate how to learn representations that capture biological information for use across multiple cryo-EM inverse problems.

</details>


### [9] [FlashControl: One-Step Controllable Generator via Distillation-Friendly Single-Stream Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22537)
*Nguyen, Ngan,Nguyen, Dung,Dao, Quan,Metaxas, Dimitris N.,Hoai, Minh,Pham, Cuong,Tran, Anh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaControl通过微调文生图扩散模型的少数层实现空间可控生成，再用步蒸馏得到单步可控模型FlashControl。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理慢需多步采样；已有步蒸馏可实现单步文生图，但ControlNet等双分支可控模型难以蒸馏。作者希望用更简单路径同时解决可控性与推理效率问题。

Method: 不新增条件分支，而是在原文生图扩散模型中选取部分层做微调，注入空间条件能力，得到单流多步教师模型DeltaControl；随后对其施加步蒸馏（step distillation），得到单前向的单步空间可控模型FlashControl。

Result: DeltaControl在单流架构下达到与双分支方法相当的空间条件生成性能；FlashControl实现单次前向的空间可控生成。

Conclusion: 部分层微调＋步蒸馏是一种可用于单步可控图像生成的高效方案，为可控扩散的加速提供了简洁替代设计。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a leading approach in visual generative modeling, particularly effective in text-to-image and controllable generation. Their main drawback is slow inference as it requires many sampling steps. While recent distillation methods enable one-step text-to-image generation, extending these techniques to controllable generation is still challenging. Existing controllable models, such as ControlNet, rely on dual-branch design that make distillation difficult. In this work, we adopt a simpler strategy. Instead of adding extra conditional branches, we finetune a selected subset of layers in the original text-to-image diffusion model to directly enable controllable generation. This results in DeltaControl, a single-flow, multi-step diffusion model capable of supporting spatial-condition generation while matching the performance of dual-branch approaches. Building on this teacher model, we further apply step-distillation to obtain FlashControl, a one-step model capable of spatial controllable generation with a single forward pass.

</details>


### [10] [CFC26: Building Evaluations for Deployment in Sonar-Based Fish Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22549)
*Van Horn, Madison,Stathatos, Suzanne,Brodjian, Sevan,Kay, Justin,Van Brunt, Kai,Young, Erik,Beery, Sara,Perona, Pietro,Hobley, Michael*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Caltech Fish Counting 2026 (CFC26) 数据集与评测协议，模拟已知/新河道的部署场景（ID/OOD 测试），新增 nMNE 与 nMANE 两项面向专家工作流的计数指标，揭示此前被帧级检测指标掩盖的上下游计数方向性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家手动审阅声呐视频做鱼类计数，成本高、难以规模化；已有自动计数系统难以在真实部署中兑现可扩展性承诺，核心症结是评测协议不反映部署条件。

Method: 将 CFC22 扩展至覆盖阿拉斯加至北加州的 9 条太平洋鲑鱼河系、11 个部署点；设计 seen/novel river 的 ID 与 OOD 评测划分；提出 nMNE（归一化平均计数误差）与 nMANE（归一化平均绝对计数误差）两个跟踪级/计数级指标。

Result: 新指标揭示出帧级检测指标无法暴露的上下游计数方向性偏差，该偏差若未被发现将带来显著生态影响；数据集与代码已在 CaltechDATA 与 GitHub 公开。

Conclusion: 评测协议本身是自动计数落地的关键瓶颈，需用贴近业务工作流与跨域部署的基准来驱动改进。

Abstract: Accurate counts of fish populations are essential for monitoring ecosystems and making decisions for conservation, harvest limits, and river management. In many field deployments, these counts are obtained from sonar videos that experts must inspect manually, making large-scale monitoring slow, expensive, and difficult. Automated counting systems have been proposed as a scalable alternative, but so far this promise has been difficult to realize in deployment. Developing evaluations that better reflect real-world deployment conditions will help close this gap and drive improvements in counting performance in practice. To achieve this, we propose Caltech Fish Counting 2026 (CFC26), a new dataset with improved evaluation protocols that simulate deployment to seen and novel rivers, testing in-distribution and out-of-distribution performance. We expand the CFC22 dataset to eleven deployment locations across nine Pacific salmon river systems from Alaska to Northern California, capturing a broader geographical and ecological range of data. We additionally introduce two new evaluation metrics for counting, nMNE and nMANE, that mirror the operational workflow that experts use and help surface errors that frame-level detection metrics do not capture. Using these metrics, we reveal previously-concealed directional bias in upstream vs downstream counts, which would have direct and significant ecological implications if undetected. The dataset and code are publicly available on CaltechDATA and GitHub.

</details>


### [11] [Seeing Seizures: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Video and EEG Deep Learning for Automated Rodent Seizure Detection and Severity Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22566)
*Das, Alloy,Ganguly, Shreyan,Massey, Nyzil,Doan, Hung Phi,Jiang, Zhanhong,Thippeswamy, Thimmasettappa,Sarkar, Soumik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究基于soman诱导癫痫模型的20只大鼠、601场次、24,131对同步视频-EEG片段，独立评测10种视频架构与7种单通道EEG模型在癫痫二分类检测与三/五级行为严重度分级上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床前癫痫研究依赖人工标注同步视频与EEG，构成效率瓶颈，需实证评估自动检测与行为严重度分级的可行性与泛化边界。

Method: 对24,131对片段做独立评测：视频端10种架构（代表为R(2+1)D-18）、EEG端7种单通道模型（代表为GRU），分别在session-exclusive与subject-exclusive两种划分下评测；并做时间采样（多帧）消融；另在RodEpil数据集上做纯视频二分类检测，并采用统一重编码以消除类相关编码线索。

Result: session-exclusive下检测macro-F1：视频0.968、EEG 0.974；五级分级仅0.657与0.560。subject-exclusive下五级分级降至0.475（视频）与0.404（EEG）。多帧采样有增益；检测性能对视频架构不敏感；RodEpil纯视频检测seizure类F1为0.979。

Conclusion: 在既定协议下刻画了现有模型对行为定义严重度标签的能力：检测稳健，细粒度分级（尤其跨个体）仍困难，提示需探索专家辅助标注流程。

Abstract: Manual annotation of synchronized video and EEG recordings is a bottleneck in preclinical seizure research. We present an empirical study of seizure detection and behavioral severity grading using 24,131 paired clips from 601 sessions across 20 rats in a soman-induced epilepsy model. Ten video architectures and seven single-channel EEG models are evaluated independently on binary detection and three- and five-class severity tasks. In the session-exclusive reference comparison, R(2+1)D-18 and GRU achieve detection macro-F1 scores of 0.968 and 0.974, respectively; for five-class grading, the corresponding scores are 0.657 and 0.560. Subject-exclusive evaluation reduces five-class macro-F1 to 0.475 for video and 0.404 for EEG, highlighting the difficulty of grading unseen animals. Temporal-sampling experiments demonstrate the value of multiple frames, while detection performance varies relatively little across the evaluated video architectures. A separate video-only binary-detection evaluation on RodEpil achieves a mean seizure-class F1 of 0.979 after uniform re-encoding to reduce identified class-correlated encoding cues. The study characterizes existing models for behavior-defined severity labels under the evaluated protocol, motivating further investigation of expert-assisted annotation workflows. Our code and github page is available here.

</details>


### [12] [Stuck on Shuffle: On Attribute-Object Binding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22602)
*Tamir, Netanel Y.,Green, Michael,Tzachor, Issar,Shimshi, Or,Samuel, Dvir,Patashnik, Or,Ben-Ari, Rami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SABER 基准，系统评估多模态检索中的属性-物体绑定能力（覆盖四种图文模态配对、多属性类型、最多十个物体），并发现 CLIP 式双编码器与 MLLM 嵌入器均存在显著绑定失败；随后仅用合成属性绑定文本微调 MLLM 嵌入器，即可跨模态迁移并取得 SABER SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索依赖共享嵌入空间，可靠检索要求嵌入不仅编码"存在哪些概念"，还要编码"概念如何组合"，其中属性-物体绑定（哪个属性属于哪个物体）是关键挑战。现有基准只覆盖部分模态配对、简单的两物体交换或小属性集合，无法说明模型在不同配对与更复杂设定下的绑定鲁棒性。

Method: 构建受控基准 SABER：覆盖全部四种图文模态配对（图-图、图-文、文-图、文-文），多样属性类型与最多含十个物体的场景。在诊断出绑定失败后，鉴于图像监督采集困难，仅使用合成属性-绑定文本（可精确构造硬负样本）微调基于 MLLM 的嵌入器，训练过程不含任何图像监督。

Result: SABER 上当前模型（CLIP 式双编码器与近期 MLLM 嵌入器）均出现大量属性-物体绑定错误。仅用文本绑定监督微调后，收益迁移到跨模态与图-图检索，且保持通用多模态嵌入性能；在 SABER 上达到 SOTA，并在否定（negation）与实例检索基准上也有提升。

Conclusion: 属性-物体绑定是多模态嵌入中被系统低估的短板；纯合成文本监督是一种廉价且高效的绑定能力增强方式，并具有跨模态泛化与更广泛的任务收益。

Abstract: Retrieval is a fundamental task in multimodal systems: given an image or text query, the goal is to find the most relevant images or texts in a collection, often using a shared embedding space. Reliable retrieval requires these embeddings to capture not only which concepts are present, but also how they are composed. One key compositional challenge is attribute--object binding: encoding which attributes belong to which objects. Existing benchmarks provide a limited view of this capability, typically covering a subset of modality pairings, simple two-object swaps, or small attribute sets. Consequently, it remains unclear how robustly current models preserve attribute--object bindings across different modality pairings and more complex settings. We therefore introduce SABER, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute--object binding across all four image and text modality pairings, spanning diverse attribute types and scenes with up to ten objects. Evaluating models on SABER reveals substantial binding failures in both CLIP-style dual encoders and recent MLLM-based embedders. These findings motivate us to ask how binding can be improved efficiently. While multimodal supervision may be effective, collecting suitable images is difficult and costly. Text, on the other hand, is well suited to binding supervision: it is cheap to generate, supports a broader range of attributes and enables precisely controlled hard negatives. We thus finetune an MLLM-based embedder exclusively on synthetic attribute-binding text. Despite the absence of image supervision, the gains transfer to cross-modal and image--image retrieval while preserving general multimodal embedding performance. Our model achieves SOTA results on SABER while also improving on negation and instance-retrieval benchmarks, demonstrating broader benefits of our binding-focused supervision.

</details>


### [13] [Three LiDARs, Twenty-Five Datasets: Cross-Source Generalization in Tree-Species Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22606)
*Lee, Jae Joong,Benes, Bedrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paper analyzes FOR-species20K LiDAR tree-species benchmark: proposes multi-resolution multi-view ConvNeXtV2 reaching 85.0% official accuracy (SOTA), then shows the score is largely in-distribution—source dataset identity is recoverable (69% from probabilities, >90% geometric), and under source-species-cell holdout macro accuracy collapses 82%→36%, tree-weighted 95%→60%.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Official FOR-species20K split mixes source datasets into train and test and uses species-balanced hidden test, so leaderboard accuracy may reflect dataset/source memorization rather than source-disjoint species generalization; need to quantify what the metric actually measures.

Method: Multi-resolution, multi-view ConvNeXtV2 classifier (plus two alternative backbones); diagnostic probes (logistic regression on out-of-fold probabilities, geometric feature-based source identification); source-species-cell grouped holdout on 5,566 trees from 8 eligible species with matched LiDAR-type support; count-matched retraining; external PureForest grouped-split experiment.

Result: Hidden-test accuracy 79.5%→85.0% (first at submission); source identity recovered 69% from probabilities, >90% within two shared species via geometry; 18/33 species single-source; source-disjoint setting: species mean 82%→36%, weighted accuracy 95%→60%; gap not explained by reduced support or backbone choice; PureForest shows smaller but consistent grouped-split degradation.

Conclusion: The 85% figure is strong in-distribution performance, not evidence of source-disjoint generalization; argues for balanced macro metrics and source-aware evaluation protocols in LiDAR tree-species benchmarks.

Abstract: FOR-species20K is the largest public LiDAR benchmark for tree-species classification, pooling 25 independently collected terrestrial, mobile, and airborne laser-scanning datasets across 33 species. We introduce a multi-resolution, multi-view ConvNeXtV2 model that improves official hidden-test accuracy from 79.5% to 85.0%, ranking first at the time of submission. We then examine what this score measures. Because the hidden test is species-balanced, its overall accuracy is more comparable to a macro metric than to accuracy under the public set's long-tailed distribution. Moreover, the official random split places every source dataset in both training and evaluation. A logistic probe recovers source identity from out-of-fold model probabilities with 69% accuracy, while geometric features identify the source above 90% within two widely shared species; 18 of the 33 species occur in only one source. Under a source-species-cell holdout, evaluated on 5,566 trees from eight eligible species that retain the same LiDAR-type training support in other sources, the unweighted species mean falls from 82% to 36%, and tree-weighted accuracy falls from 95% to 60%. Count-matched retraining and experiments with two alternative backbones indicate that this gap is not explained by reduced training support alone, while an external PureForest experiment shows a smaller but consistent grouped-split effect. These results establish the 85% leaderboard score as strong in-distribution performance, but not as an estimate of source-disjoint generalization, motivating balanced metrics and source-aware evaluation. The code is available at: https://github.com/JaeLee18/FOR20K-lidar-tree-species

</details>


### [14] [ProactGen: Learning Proactive Clarification for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22608)
*Wang, Zhixiao,Chen, Yang,Xu, Yifan,Li, Chenbin,Chang, Yue,Long, Kai,Dong, Fengming,Wang, Limin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProactGen：训练VLM作为主动提问策略（RL，奖励=最终视频与目标参考的对齐度），基于低步数视频草稿向用户提问澄清意图，单轮澄清即超过最强基线四轮精修效果且仅用29%时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成的用户意图匹配成本高：单次生成昂贵，提示欠定义导致反复"生成-检查-修改"；现有提示自动改写方法无法补充初始描述中不存在的想法与偏好。

Method: 以视频草稿为视觉依据的主动澄清框架：用强化学习训练VLM提问策略，观察初始提示+视频草稿，输出对下游生成最有价值的问题；奖励信号为最终生成视频与目标参考视频的对齐度；并利用"澄清无需终稿质量草稿"的性质，用更少去噪步数生成草稿以降低澄清开销。

Result: 相较直接生成，目标对齐提升至少为最强基线增益的1.8倍；单轮ProactGen的对齐已超过最强基线四轮精修结果，耗时仅为其29%。

Conclusion: 将用户交互建模为可学习的、草稿条件化的提问策略，可用低成本草稿换取更高的意图对齐效率；属视频生成/交互式生成方向，与具身感知、3D重建或机器人操作无直接关联，作为方法学参考（RL训练多模态策略、草稿级低成本迭代）具有间接借鉴价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation let users create high-quality videos from natural language descriptions, yet obtaining results that match user intent remains time-consuming: individual generations are computationally expensive, and underspecified prompts force repeated cycles of generation, inspection, and revision. Existing video prompt optimization methods improve generation through automatic rewriting, but cannot supply the ideas and preferences that were not in the initial description. We propose ProactGen, a video generation framework that learns proactive clarification, eliciting user intent through questions grounded in generated video drafts. Specifically, we train a vision-language model as a questioning policy with reinforcement learning: observing the initial prompt and a video draft, it learns to ask the questions most valuable to downstream generation, rewarded by the alignment between the final generated video and a target reference. We further find that effective questioning does not require drafts of final-output quality, and exploit this property by generating drafts with fewer denoising steps, keeping the added cost of clarification low. Experiments show that ProactGen improves target alignment over direct generation by at least 1.8x the gain of the strongest baselines. Moreover, a single round of ProactGen already exceeds the alignment that the strongest baseline reaches after four refinement rounds, in 29% of its time.

</details>


### [15] [Shift-Reliance Alignment: Ranking Vision-Language Models by the Cues They Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22610)
*Mohammad, Faisal,Younis, Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Shift-Reliance Alignment (SRA)：用100张源图编辑测量VLM对"线索"(场景上下文/图中文字)的依赖度，结合未标注目标域上的线索误导比例估计，可在不运行候选模型的情况下预测目标域最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜只给单一排名，但在"场景暗示但未含物体"的问题上，POPE最优模型未必最优；需要低成本、免目标域推理的域内模型选择方法。

Method: 对每个模型在100张带标签源图上做"线索编辑-匹配对照编辑"以量化可靠性依赖(reliancescore)；用650–1200个带标签目标项校准检测器估计目标域线索误导比例(shift)；SRA分=源错误率+relilance×shift，取最小者。发布CueDial基准。

Result: Reliance可预测贴纸攻击下的被误导情况(Spearman 0.93，9个未参与设计的模型)及未编辑COCO上的幻觉；预注册测试中SRA把选择模型误差从30.4%(源错误选择)降至18.8%(Objects365、OpenImages)，但COCO未如预期改善；CueDial上置信度选择在叠加文字否认可见物体时落后最优模型40+百分点，SRA差距<3点。

Conclusion: 线索依赖是VLM幻觉的跨域可迁移信号，可用于免目标域推理的模型选择；方法存在域依赖失效情形(COCO)，需进一步分析。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are usually chosen from leaderboards, which give one ranking for every application. On questions about objects that a scene suggests but does not contain, however, the model with the lowest error on the POPE hallucination benchmark is not the one with the lowest error on such questions. For yes/no questions about object presence, we show that the best model for a target domain can be predicted without running any candidate on that domain, given 650 to 1,200 labeled target items that calibrate a detector. We measure each model's reliance on a cue, a feature such as scene context or text in the image, by editing 100 labeled source images so that the cue suggests the wrong answer, and comparing with a matched control edit. Reliance predicts which VLMs are misled by physical sticker attacks (Spearman .93 on nine models not used to design the score) and which hallucinate on unedited COCO photographs, beyond what their tendency to answer yes explains. Shift-Reliance Alignment (SRA) scores a model by its source error on the labeled source questions plus its reliance multiplied by the shift. The shift is the proportion of target questions on which the cue misleads, and a detector estimates it on the unlabeled target images. SRA selects the model with the lowest score. In pre-registered tests on new images where most questions mislead, it lowered the error of the selected model from 30.4% under selection by source error to 18.8% on Objects365 and OpenImages but, against its prediction, not on COCO. On CueDial, a benchmark we release that varies how often a cue misleads, selection by model confidence falls more than 40 percentage points behind the best model when overlaid text denies a visible object, and SRA stays within 3 points.

</details>


### [16] [Deep Learning for Deepfake Image Detection: Comparative Model Evaluation and Gradient-Based Visual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22676)
*Korchi, Saad,Waga, Abderrahim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用从零训练的紧凑CNN与ImageNet预训练MobileNetV2（两阶段迁移学习）对比深度伪造人脸检测，并在140k Real and Fake Faces数据集上评估，配合Grad-CAM可视化与Flask原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人脸图像使真实与生成内容的区分对数字取证与在线信任日益重要，需要兼顾性能与可解释性的深度伪造检测流程。

Method: 在140,000张平衡人脸数据集上对比两种分类策略：从零训练的紧凑卷积网络与经两阶段迁移学习微调的ImageNet预训练MobileNetV2；使用Grad-CAM可视化预测相关区域，并构建Flask原型用于并排检视。

Result: 记录的验证协议下，从零训练（含增强）模型达84.15%准确率（损失0.3625）；微调预训练模型达95.90%准确率（损失0.1074）。注：均为验证集结果，非独立测试集估计。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉表征在该设定下具实用价值，但需结合数据集来源、解释方法局限与评测范围审慎解读验证性能。

Abstract: Synthetic facial imagery has made the distinction between authentic and generated content increasingly important for digital forensics and online trust. This study examines an explainable deep-learning workflow for image-level deepfake detection using two contrasting classification strategies: a compact convolutional model trained from scratch and an ImageNet-pretrained MobileNetV2 model adapted through two-stage transfer learning. Experiments were conducted with the balanced 140k Real and Fake Faces dataset, comprising 140,000 facial images. Under the recorded validation protocol, the augmented from-scratch model reached 84.15% accuracy (loss 0.3625), compared with 95.90% accuracy (loss 0.1074) for the fine-tuned pretrained model. Gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) was used to visualize image regions associated with predictions, and a Flask-based prototype was developed for side-by-side inspection. These observations support the practical value of pretrained visual representations in the studied setting while underscoring the need to interpret validation performance alongside dataset provenance, explanation limitations, and evaluation scope. The reported results are validation results, not held-out test estimates.

</details>


### [17] [EgoP$^{2}$C: Internalizing Plan-Perceive-Compose for Spatial Reasoning in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22681)
*Li, Chaoyu,Sadhu, Arka,Li, Yuliang,Kim, Hyo Jin,Chai, Yuning,Fazli, Pooyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoP²C将"规划-感知-组合"（plan-perceive-compose）流程内化进单个VLM：先用Dual-LoRA分别训练Planner与Perception教师，再用MoE学生在其监督下微调，并以段级多教师on-policy蒸馏（MOPD扩展）让学生自采轨迹分段接受不同教师监督；推理时无需外部空间工具即可端到端自适应追问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频中的复杂空间推理需要判断所需证据、主动获取针对性观测并多步组合推理。现有VLM难以可靠完成；agentic系统虽调用外部视觉/几何工具，但训练只更新推理部分、工具本身不变，错误的观测仍会传染到最终答案。

Method: 双教师架构：Planner教师与Perception教师各用一个独立LoRA适配器分别训练。随后在MoE学生中对应专家上做监督微调。再用段级MOPD：学生按当前策略生成轨迹，按段分配给不同监督者——Planner监督"问什么问题、何时停止取证、如何组织最终答案"，Perception监督视频落地的问答内容。推理阶段学生单模型端到端执行该自适应工作流，不依赖外部空间工具。

Result: 在三个空间推理基准上，基于Qwen3-VL-8B与SenseNova-SI-1.3-8B的学生模型分别比基座平均提升9.2与7.5个百分点；两种骨干下推理FLOPs降低65.8–66.3%，相对共享Dual-LoRA教师取得1.29–1.49×加速。

Conclusion: 将工具型agent流程蒸馏/内化为单一VLM的"规划-感知"复合能力，可在去掉外部空间工具的同时提升精度并显著降低计算量，说明段级多教师on-policy蒸馏是训练内化式agentic VLM的有效途径。

Abstract: Answering complex spatial reasoning questions from egocentric video requires determining what evidence is needed, acquiring targeted observations, and combining them through multiple reasoning steps. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to perform this process reliably. Agentic systems address this challenge by querying external vision and geometry tools, but training their reasoning leaves these tools unchanged, so inaccurate observations can still lead to incorrect answers. We introduce EgoP$^{2}$C, a framework that internalizes a plan-perceive-compose process within a single VLM. To this end, we first train specialized Planner and Perception teachers using separate adapters (Dual-LoRA), then fine-tune the corresponding experts in a mixture-of-experts (MoE) student under their supervision. We subsequently train the student on trajectories generated by its current policy using a segment-level extension of multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD), assigning different trajectory segments to different supervisors. The Planner teacher supervises which evidence-seeking questions to ask, when to stop gathering evidence, and how to compose the final answer. The Perception teacher supervises the video-grounded answers to those questions. At inference, the student executes this adaptive workflow end-to-end, without external spatial tools. Across three spatial reasoning benchmarks, EgoP$^{2}$C students built from Qwen3-VL-8B and SenseNova-SI-1.3-8B outperform their base models by an average of 9.2 and 7.5 percentage points, respectively. Across both backbones, EgoP$^{2}$C reduces inference FLOPs by 65.8-66.3% and achieves $1.29$-$1.49\times$ speedups over their shared Dual-LoRA teacher.

</details>


### [18] [Thinking from Above: Efficient Spatial Reasoning with Bird's-Eye-View Visual Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22700)
*Siddiqui, Faizan,Savaliya, Nirav,Chada, Sahith Reddy,Sachdeva, Enna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cambrian-BEV 用深度+相机位姿将各帧 DINOv3 patch 特征反投影到统一 BEV 网格并按格平均，得到参数无关的几何 token 化；128 帧约 10 万 patch 压缩为 729 个 BEV token，LLM prefill 提速 2.9×、峰值显存降 3.6×，VSI-Bench 63.48（+8.5）、ReVSI 44.68（+4.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 MLLM 以时间顺序组织观测，而具身空间推理需要以空间方式组织；其视觉表征在相邻帧局部一致，却缺乏跨视角、跨时间稳定的持久空间上下文。作者因此利用具身智能体天然可得的深度与相机位姿显式建立空间对应关系。

Method: 在 Cambrian-S 基础上扩展：冻结 DINOv3 视觉编码器 + 可训练多模态投影器与 LLM，仅新增参数无关几何 token 化模块——将每帧 patch 特征依据深度和位姿反投影至共享 BEV 坐标系，对落入同一空间单元的 feature 取平均，使同一物理位置的多视角/多时刻观测聚合为统一空间 token；固定 27×27=729 格网格，无需改动其他架构。

Result: 压缩效果：128 帧约 100K patch 特征 → 729 BEV token，prefill 时间降低 2.9×、峰值显存降低 3.6×。VSI-Bench 63.48，较同条件微调的帧 token 对照高 +8.5，且视觉 token 少 12.6×，房间尺寸 +24.5、相对方向 +34.1；ReVSI 44.68，较对照 +4.5。

Conclusion: BEV 几何 token 化以极简、无参数的显式空间对应替换时序组织，兼具持久场景表征与高效压缩双重收益，在具身空间推理基准上以更少视觉 token 取得显著提升，指向"时空重组织"是提升具身 MLLM 空间智力的关键杠杆。

Abstract: Existing video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) organize observations primarily in time, but embodied spatial reasoning requires organizing them in space. Consequently, their visual representations can remain locally consistent across nearby frames while lacking a persistent spatial context that is stable across viewpoints and time. We exploit depth and camera pose, which are naturally available to embodied agents, to explicitly establish this spatial correspondence. We introduce Cambrian-BEV, which extends Cambrian-S with a parameter-free geometric tokenization scheme that back-projects visual patch features from each frame into a shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) coordinate frame and averages features that fall within the same spatial cell. Thus, observations of the same physical location, regardless of viewpoint or time step, are aggregated into a common spatial token. In addition to providing a persistent scene representation, this formulation acts as an efficient compression mechanism: 128 frames containing approximately 100K DINOv3 patch features are consolidated into a fixed 27$\times$27 grid of only 729 BEV tokens, cutting LLM prefill time by $2.9\times$ and peak memory by $3.6\times$. Cambrian-BEV requires no architectural modification beyond BEV tokenization and uses a frozen DINOv3 vision encoder with a trainable multimodal projector and LLM. On VSI-Bench, our model reaches 63.48, $+8.5$ points over an identically fine-tuned frame-token control with $12.6\times$ fewer visual tokens, with particularly large gains on room size ($+24.5$) and relative direction ($+34.1$). On ReVSI, it reaches 44.68, $+4.5$ over the corresponding control.

</details>


### [19] [HilbertGen-3D:Hilbert--Multifractal Conditioning for Topology-Aware 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22720)
*Zhang, Ziyi,Li, Shixuan,Nakahira, Yorie,Bogdan, Paul,Qu, Guannan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HilbertGen-3D：用三维Hilbert曲线序列化多尺度体素测度并估计广义分形维数，作为全局条件注入扩散去噪器，提升点云生成质量与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云扩散生成方法依赖局部体素/patch特征，缺乏全局结构组织信息，且全局条件如何提升生成质量研究不足。

Method: 提出Hilbert-Multifractal Conditioning (HMC)：计算多尺度体素测度，经3D Hilbert曲线序列化以显式暴露空间依赖，再用尺度相关的配分函数估计广义分形维数；将信号编码为全局embedding与多尺度token，通过高效bottleneck融合模块注入扩散去噪网络（TopoDiT-3D基座）。

Result: 在ShapeNet的Chair/Airplane/Car三类上，1-NNA偏离50%的绝对偏差最多降低8.90个百分点，覆盖率最多提升5.88个百分点。

Conclusion: 局部密度线索与全局Hilbert-多重分形结构结合，是提升三维生成保真度与多样性的简单有效方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generative modeling underpins game asset creation, medical imaging, and VR/AR environmental design, where geometric structure directly influences perception and decision-making. Diffusion models represent a powerful paradigm for point cloud generation. However, existing approaches rely heavily on local voxel or patch features and often miss global structural organization, and much less work has studied how global conditioning can be used to improve generation quality. In this paper, we introduce HilbertGen-3D, a novel topology-aware conditional diffusion framework based on Hilbert-Multifractal Conditioning (HMC). HMC computes multi-scale voxel measures, serializes them via a 3D Hilbert curve to expose structured spatial dependencies, and estimates the generalized fractal dimensions from scale-dependent partition functions. We encode these signals as global embeddings and multi-scale tokens, and inject them into a diffusion denoiser through an efficient bottleneck fusion module. Across the Chair, Airplane, and Car categories of ShapeNet, our two conditioning variants reduce the absolute deviation of 1-Nearest Neighbor Accuracy (1-NNA) from 50% by up to 8.90 percentage points and increase coverage by up to 5.88 percentage points relative to TopoDiT-3D. This shows that combining local density cues with global Hilbert-multifractal structure provides a simple and effective recipe for high-fidelity and diverse 3D generation.

</details>


### [20] [Improving OCR Accuracy Through Multi-Classifier Verification of Visually Similar Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22726)
*Khan, Nuzhat,Rahman, Ab Al-Hadi Ab,Kamaruddin, Mohamad Anuar,Bakht, Muhammad Paend,Fatima, Sana,Khodadadi, Farhad,Alshareef, Ibrahim Yousef*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVM是一个字符级后处理框架，用针对易混淆字符对（O/0、Z/2、S/5）的专用二分类器校验并纠正OCR输出中的歧义字符，在三种引擎十个字体上提升3.95–14.81个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR对视觉相似字符的混淆是内在属性问题，图像预处理提升画质、词级后处理纠错都无法解决字符级视觉歧义，而这类错误在序列号、编码、车牌等非词典数据上尤为致命。

Method: 构建Character Verification Model：不改动底层识别模型，在OCR流程后接入模块化的字符级校验器，为每个易混淆字符对训练专门的二分类器，对候选字符做验证与纠正；不依赖词典校验，因此适用于非词汇型字符串。

Result: 在Tesseract、EasyOCR、TrOCR三种引擎与十种字体（复杂度低到高）上准确率提升3.95–14.81个百分点；相似字符错误纠正率87.3%，误纠正率仅2.3%。

Conclusion: 字符级二分类验证是解决OCR视觉相似字符混淆的轻量有效途径，可即插即用地集成到现有OCR管线，特别适合序列号、车牌等非词典场景。

Abstract: Distinguishing visually similar characters is a persistent challenge for optical character recognition (OCR) systems, causing errors in critical applications of text digitization. While preprocessing techniques improve image quality and word-level post-processing corrects spelling errors, neither addresses character-level visual ambiguity, an intrinsic property of certain character pairs that limits even state-of-the-art OCR models. We present the Character Verification Model (CVM), a character-level post-processing framework that verifies and corrects ambiguous characters in OCR outputs using specialized binary classifiers trained on visually confusable pairs such as 'O'/'0', 'Z'/'2', and 'S'/'5'. CVM operates independently of dictionary validation, making it suitable for non-lexical data including serial numbers, alphanumeric codes, and license plates. Experiments on three OCR engines (Tesseract, EasyOCR, TrOCR) and ten fonts with low to high complexity demonstrate accuracy improvements of 3.95-14.81 percentage points, with an 87.3% correction rate for visually similar character errors and a false-correction rate of only 2.3%. The modular architecture integrates with existing OCR pipelines without modification to underlying recognition models.

</details>


### [21] [OmniXRef: A Unified Framework for Multimodal Multi-Reference Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22744)
*Cong, Xiaoyan,Wang, Tianyu,Jeong, Hyeonho,Zhang, Zhifei,Zhou, Yuqian,Li, Yijun,Liu, Shaoteng,Lin, Zhe,Sridhar, Srinath,Kim, Soo Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniXRef：统一的多模态多参考视频编辑框架，支持文本/图像/视频参考的任意组合；提出参考库输入设计、R²-RoPE、单流DiT+MLLM交错序列建模，并构建OmniXRef-Data训练数据与OmniXRef-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑中的创作意图可由文本、图像、视频等多模态参考混合表达，但现有方法通常只支持固定模态或固定数量的参考，且缺乏视频参考这一控制模态的系统评测。

Method: （1）统一输入设计：reference-bank接口 + R²-RoPE，使模型可泛化到可变数量的参考；（2）OmniXRef-Model：单流扩散Transformer，将源视频与MLLM输出的多模态上下文以交错序列联合建模；（3）数据侧：用不同编辑任务与参考组合的合成流水线构建OmniXRef-Data，并采用课程学习逐步提升任务复杂度与分辨率；（4）提出OmniXRef-Bench，覆盖Camera、Motion、Content、Style、VFX五类视频参考属性迁移。

Result: 综合评测显示OmniXRef-Model优于任务专用基线与商业模型；OmniXRef-Bench为视频作为控制模态提供了系统评测手段。

Conclusion: 多参考、多模态的统一视频编辑在架构（参考库+多参考位置编码）与数据（合成流水线+课程学习）两方面均可工程化实现，视频参考可作为一种有效的属性迁移控制信号。

Abstract: Creative intent in video editing can be expressed through text instructions (e.g., "remove the people"), image references (e.g., subject appearance), video references (e.g., camera movement), or a combination of the above. We introduce OmniXRef, a unified framework for multimodal multi-reference video editing that allows users to combine variable numbers of text, image, and video references to control diverse attributes of a source video. We first devise a unified input design with a reference-bank interface and introduce $R^2$-RoPE to support generalization to more references. Then our model, OmniXRef-Model, uses a single-stream diffusion transformer to jointly process the source video and multimodal context from an MLLM in an interleaved sequence. To address the scarcity of training data for video-reference editing, we construct OmniXRef-Data using synthesis pipelines for different editing tasks and combinations of references. We use curriculum learning to progressively increase task complexity and resolution. To support systematic evaluation of video as a control modality, we introduce OmniXRef-Bench, a benchmark for video-reference attribute transfer across Camera, Motion, Content, Style, and Visual Effects (VFX). Comprehensive evaluations show that our OmniXRef-Model outperforms task-specific baselines and commercial models.

</details>


### [22] [Cross-Person Identity Change Under Source-Coupled Multi-Human Generation: A Controlled UniPortrait Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22746)
*Al-Edreesi, Edrees,Al-Gaphari, Ghaleb,Alsurori, Muneer,Edrees, Mohammed,Al-Hamdani, Fouad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在不引入新算法的前提下，用119个双人基线场景与3个固定种子，通过InsightFace buffalo度量人脸相似度，量化UniPortrait在多人物生成中对“非目标人物”身份的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人类个性化生成常只关注名义目标人物的保真度，而忽略对画面中其他人物身份的扰动；作者希望刻画这种“附带身份漂移”现象，作为对现有单ID修复流程假设的检验。

Method: 研究1：源耦合单ID、仅目标生成（A1）；研究2：原生双ID生成（A2）。均使用119个已接受的双人基线场景、3个固定输出种子，以InsightFace buffalo计算连续的参考图对齐人脸相似度；采用配对对比（共享基线相似度在代数上抵消，形成匹配的POST比较），并基于案例聚类bootstrap给出95%置信区间；事后补充独立身份评估器、盲评人类判断与buffalo内部nuisance模型敏感性分析。

Result: A1下受保护人物平均变化为−0.567904、目标为−0.236904（双双为负，提示广泛扰动而非局部修复）；A2下配对对比为正：受保护人物+0.512918（CI [0.471434, 0.550259]），目标+0.158853（CI [0.118903, 0.197523]），基于111个案例中的287对可联合测量样本。验证实验与独立评估器及盲评方向一致。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了“原生双ID”这一完好机制下的行为，但参考图、路由、适配器与token数共同变化，故不能推断组件因果、类别级身份保持、局部编辑成功，也不能外推到其他实现或可测人群之外。属于诊断性/评估性分析。

Abstract: Multi-human personalization must account for identity changes in people outside the nominal target of a generative operation. We characterize this behavior in UniPortrait, without introducing a new preservation algorithm. Two sequential studies use 119 accepted two-person baseline scenes and three fixed output seeds, measuring continuous reference-based facial similarity with InsightFace \buffalo. In Study~1, source-coupled single-ID target-only generation (A1) produces negative mean transitions for both the protected person ($-0.567904$) and target ($-0.236904$), indicating broad perturbation rather than established successful repair with collateral loss. In Study~2, native dual-ID generation (A2) yields more positive paired contrasts: protected $+0.512918$ (95\% case-cluster CI $[0.471434,0.550259]$) and target $+0.158853$ ($[0.118903,0.197523]$), across 287 jointly measurable pairs from 111 cases. Shared baseline similarity cancels algebraically in these paired contrasts, leaving a matched POST comparison. Validation designed after the primary result freeze finds directional agreement with an independent identity evaluator and blinded human judgments; within-\buffalo\ nuisance models provide supportive sensitivity evidence. The findings characterize intact native regimes, whose references, routing, adapters, and token counts co-vary. They do not establish component causality, categorical identity preservation, successful localized editing, or generalization beyond the evaluated implementation and measurable cohort.

</details>


### [23] [MedSTP: Preserving Subspace and Transition Evidence for Medical VQA Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22751)
*Fan, Mengchen,Lan, Qizhen,Xiao, Xi,Zhao, Lin,He, Liang,Wang, Tianyang,Jiang, Xiaoqian,Che, Pulin,Geng, Baocheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Subspace-Transition Token Pruning（训练自由的解码前视觉token剪枝），以主成分空间中各token的谱支撑估计构造成分紧凑子空间，并加入有界的token网格转移先验以保留医学图像的边界/纹理证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VQA的VLM需处理长视觉token序列，剪枝可提速，但医学答案常依赖局部边界与纹理证据，常规剪枝易丢失关键信息；故将剪枝重构为"子空间稳定的证据保留"问题。

Method: 无需训练的预解码剪枝：在PCA空间估计每个token的谱支撑（spectral support），由全token集诱导的主视觉证据子空间构造紧凑子空间表示，按保留token与主子空间的接近度筛选；再用有界token网格转移先验（bounded token-grid transition prior）对边界/纹理敏感token加权。

Result: 在SLAKE、OmniMedVQA（封闭式）与RadImageNet-VQA（封闭式+开放式）上，使用Lingshu-7B与Qwen3-VL-8B骨干测试，封闭式与开放式均取得稳定且较强的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个有效的训练自由剪枝框架，适用于医学领域VQA；但摘要未报告token压缩比、与朴素剪枝基线的数值对比及效率指标。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) for visual question answering (VQA) often pass long visual-token sequences to the language model, making visual-token pruning attractive but risky for medical images, where answers may depend on localized boundary and texture evidence. We study training free pre-decoding pruning and treat pruning as a subspace stable evidence preservation problem. The retained tokens should remain close to the dominant visual evidence subspace induced by the full token set. To this end, we propose \emph{Subspace-Transition Token Pruning} (\method{}), which estimates each token's spectral support from principal component space and constructs a compact representation subspace. To better reflect medical image structure, \method{} further uses a bounded token-grid transition prior to promote boundary and texture sensitive tokens. We evaluate \method{} on the closed-ended SLAKE and OmniMedVQA benchmarks and on RadImageNet-VQA, which provides both closed-ended and open-ended evaluations, using Lingshu-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B backbones. \method{} delivers consistently strong performance on both the closed-ended and open-ended RadImageNet-VQA evaluations. These results show that \method{} is an effective training free pruning framework for medical domain VQA.

</details>


### [24] [Input- and Feature-Space Correction for Cross-Hospital IHC Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22756)
*Lee, Jongwoon,Gallardo, Jhair,Kanan, Christopher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结病理基础模型（UNI、Virchow2）提取特征前后，分别系统评测无监督的输入空间与特征空间校正方法，用于跨医院 IHC 分级迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型主要在 H&E 图像上训练，却被直接冻结用于 IHC 特征提取，而跨医院的染色与成像差异会损害分级性能；目前缺乏对跨医院 IHC 分级校正方法的系统对比。

Method: 在输入图像侧应用校正（提出 FIX，并与染色归一化方法对比），在冻结 FM 特征侧应用校正（特征级标准化、CORAL 等），使用 UNI 与 Virchow2 两个模型、三种 IHC 标记物、六个有向跨医院迁移任务，共 12 个设置，以患者平均的 tile 级评估进行基准测试。

Result: 输入空间中 FIX 的 macro-F1 提升最大，优于经典染色归一化；特征空间中简单逐特征标准化效果最佳，优于 CORAL。FIX 与特征标准化组合后，macro-F1 与 QWK 分别提升 0.181 和 0.208，序数误差降低 0.209。

Conclusion: 输入空间与特征空间校正对跨医院 IHC 分级具有互补收益，可在不微调基础模型的情况下以冻结方式部署，为病理 FM 的跨域部署提供即用型流水线。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (FMs) are trained predominantly on hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) images but are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for immunohistochemistry (IHC), where cross-hospital differences can impair grading. However, systematic comparisons of correction methods for cross-hospital IHC grading remain limited. To address this gap, we benchmark label-free transformations applied to input images before frozen-FM encoding and to features after frozen-FM encoding using UNI and Virchow2, three IHC markers, and six directed cross-hospital transfers, yielding 12 settings. Among the evaluated input-space methods, our proposed method FIX achieved the largest macro-F1 improvement over no correction, outperforming well-known stain normalization techniques. Among feature-space methods, a simple feature-wise standardization achieved the best improvement over no correction, outperforming CORAL. Under patient-averaged tile-level evaluation, combining FIX with feature-wise standardization improved macro-F1 and quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) by 0.181 and 0.208, respectively, and reduced ordinal error by 0.209 relative to no correction. These results show that input- and feature-space transformations can provide complementary benefits for cross-hospital IHC grading with frozen pathology FMs.

</details>


### [25] [Direct Conditional Parameterization for N-Dimensional Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22778)
*Gao, Zhongpai,Planche, Benjamin,Nguyen, Van Nguyen,Zheng, Meng,Choudhuri, Anwesa,Innanje, Arun,Chen, Terrence,Wu, Ziyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"直接条件参数化"，将N维splatting中由协方差推导的条件切片读出替换为显式参数（Cholesky精度因子+可分解查询-位置回归算子），并同时应用于高斯核与Beta核，得到dGS与dBS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-DGS、UBS等N维splatting需在每次渲染查询时对每个primitive做矩阵求逆与回归乘积以恢复条件均值、不透明度与协方差，计算开销大且耦合结构不受约束。

Method: 用显式的per-effect参数替代协方差导出的条件读出：不透明度使用Cholesky精度因子，位置回归使用可分解的query-to-position算子并带可学习的逐维耦合；在加入空间尺度与耦合先验的同时，无约束的条件坐标仍可恢复有效的联合协方差。该参数化同时适配Gaussian与Beta核。

Result: 在五个静态与动态基准上质量持平或更优，主MCMC评测最高提升+1.26 dB；生产切片比即时协方差条件化快6.9–7.7倍，完整渲染在四个训练场景上加速1.25–1.91倍。

Conclusion: 直接条件化提供了一种结构化、核无关的N维splatting替代方案，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: N-dimensional splatting extends 3D Gaussian Splatting with view direction and time to model view-dependent and dynamic effects. Each primitive is lifted into a joint distribution over position and conditioning variables, then conditionally sliced at each rendering query. N-DGS and UBS share this step across Gaussian and Beta kernels: recovering the conditional mean, opacity, and covariance requires per-primitive matrix inversions and regression products in every forward pass. We introduce direct conditional parameterization, replacing this covariance-derived readout with explicit per-effect parameters: a Cholesky precision factor for opacity and a factorized query-to-position regression operator with learnable per-dimension coupling. Unrestricted conditional coordinates recover a valid joint covariance; our factorization adds spatial-scale and coupling priors. Applied to both kernels, the parameterization yields dGS (Gaussian) and dBS (Beta). Across five static and dynamic benchmarks, they match or exceed their covariance-derived counterparts on quality, with gains up to +1.26 dB in the main MCMC evaluation. Production slicing is 6.9-7.7 times faster than on-the-fly covariance-derived conditioning; complete-render profiling gives 1.25-1.91 times speedups on four trained scenes. Direct conditioning provides a structured, kernel-agnostic alternative for N-dimensional splatting. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/dgs/.

</details>


### [26] [STRIDE: Evaluating Spatiotemporal Reasoning in Driving Edge Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22795)
*Liu, Lieqi,gao, Rui,Gu, Jia-Chen,Hu, Wenbo,Mo, Zhaobin,Moradipari, Ahmadreza,Ammar, Nejib,Wang, Wei,Peng, Nanyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 STRIDE，一个面向自动驾驶长尾/安全关键场景的以物体为中心的时空推理基准，包含六类任务与多选题+开放式 VQA 两种形式，12 个模型最高仅 43.4% 平均准确率（随机约 20%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测多用基础感知问答考察边缘案例，或在简单场景中测时序理解，缺乏对复杂时空推理（过去空间观测—未来后果）的检验；而自动驾驶中不理解物体运动与交互会导致危险决策。

Method: 构建 STRIDE 基准，覆盖空间感知、空间理解、时序记忆、时序外推、轨迹预测、场景上下文感知六类任务族；将多选题（单项时空判断）与相关开放式 VQA（场景解释与决策理由）配对；优先选取物体行为异常、交互复杂的交通场景；参考答案由结构化数据标注经自动化流程生成，辅以有限语言模型辅助与人工校验保证质量；在 12 个闭源、开源权重与驾驶专用模型上进行评测。

Result: 最佳模型 GPT-6-Astra 多选题平均准确率仅 43.4%（随机基线约 20%）；分析显示描述驾驶场景能力强并不带来安全关键判断的准确性，且模型重建物体运动的难度高于回忆过去位置；整体 VQA 分数会掩盖这些局限。

Conclusion: STRIDE 揭示了视觉语言模型在自动驾驶时空推理上的系统性短板，说明需要面向长尾安全场景的细粒度评测而非聚合指标。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, perceiving surrounding objects is not enough: failing to understand how they move and interact over time can lead to dangerous decisions. Evaluating vision-language models (VLMs) therefore requires testing whether they connect past spatial observations to future consequences. Yet existing benchmarks often assess edge cases through basic perception questions or test temporal understanding in simple scenarios, leaving complex spatiotemporal reasoning underexplored. We introduce STRIDE (SpatioTemporal Reasoning In Driving Edge Cases), an object-centric benchmark for long-tail, safety-critical driving scenarios. STRIDE covers six task families: spatial perception, spatial understanding, temporal memory, temporal extrapolation, trajectory prediction, and scene-context awareness. It pairs multiple-choice questions probing individual spatiotemporal judgments with related free-form visual question answering (VQA) requiring detailed scene explanations and justifications of driving decisions. We prioritize scenes with unusual object behaviors and complex traffic interactions, generate reference answers from structured dataset annotations through automated procedures, and use limited language-model assistance and human verification to ensure annotation quality. Across 12 proprietary, open-weight, and driving-specialized models, even the best performer, GPT-6-Astra, achieves only 43.4% average multiple-choice accuracy, versus an approximately 20% random-choice baseline. Further analysis reveals that strong performance in describing driving scenes does not necessarily translate to accurate safety-critical judgments, and that models struggle more with reconstructing object motion than recalling past positions. STRIDE thus exposes spatiotemporal driving limitations that aggregate VQA scores can conceal.

</details>


### [27] [Replay Pruning and Few-Bit Integer Inference for Hardware-Efficient Speed-Sign Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22811)
*Park, Se Un,Chung, Kyungkeon,Kim, Huigeol,Park, Junyoung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两级交通标志识别系统：检测端用"channel replay"剪枝（一次性记录通道删除并重放于原网络，配合单次连续知识蒸馏恢复），读取器为仅整数运算的18k参数CNN（W4A8，12.1KB片上内存），整体0.72M参数，在Jetson AGX Orin上达94 FPS、90%精度下95.2%召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载相机SoC上同时部署检测器与分类器受限于内存、算力与能耗预算，需要在不损失精度的前提下大幅压缩模型，并保证纯整数量化推理可落地。

Method: 检测器为YOLO族网络（YOLOv5n/YOLOv8n）。压缩采用channel replay：迭代剪枝级联识别待删通道，将记录的删除操作重放到原始任务训练网络（parent）上，随后仅做一次连续知识蒸馏恢复；变体per-step replay按步重放。读取器为5层CNN（18k参数），配合YOLO头部后处理，全部采用整数运算，权重4-bit、激活8-bit。TensorRT隐式/显式公式与DLA后端部署测评。

Result: YOLOv5n上剪除60.3%参数且保持parent精度，五组匹配随机种子下验证mAP50-95优于迭代剪枝与从头训练，per-step replay为最准剪枝法；YOLOv8n上replay与per-step replay均超过所有对比方法与未剪枝parent。W4A8读取器在12.1KB片上内存下达到FP32精度，可执行流式模型在全部9,850张测试裁剪图上复现INT8类别预测。INT8仅在TensorRT隐式公式下快于FP16，replay检测器在DLA上最快。仅用公开数据训练，0.72M参数系统在留出车载相机上以90%精度识别95.2%标志，94 FPS。

Conclusion: "一次性重放+单次蒸馏"的剪枝范式简单有效且可超越原始未剪枝网络；端到端整数化（含头部后处理与读取器）与加速器后端选择对车载实时部署至关重要，全流程可仅依赖公开数据完成。

Abstract: Speed-limit sign recognition on an automotive camera system-on-chip (SoC) must fit a detector and a classifier (the reader) into the memory, arithmetic, and energy budgets of the device. In our two-stage recognizer, the detector, a YOLO-family network with ReLU and hard-sigmoid operators, is compressed by channel replay: an iterative pruning cascade identifies the channels to remove, the recorded deletions are replayed on the original task-trained network, the parent, and one continuous knowledge-distillation recovery follows. On a YOLOv5n, replay removes 60.3% of the parameters at the accuracy of the parent and, in validation mAP50-95 over five matched seeds, outperforms iterative pruning and training from scratch, and its per-step variant (per-step replay) is the most accurate pruning method. On a YOLOv8n, replay and per-step replay outperform every other method and the unpruned parent. The reader, a five-layer CNN with 18k parameters, and the post-processing of the YOLOv5n and YOLOv8n heads use integer arithmetic only. With 4-bit weights and 8-bit activations (W4A8), the reader matches FP32 accuracy in 12.1 KB of on-chip memory, and an executable streaming model reproduces the INT8 class predictions of its runtime on all 9,850 test crops. On a Jetson AGX Orin, INT8 is faster than FP16 only in the implicit formulation of TensorRT, and the replay detector runs fastest on the deep-learning accelerator (DLA). Trained on public data only, the combined system of 0.72 M parameters recognizes 95.2% of the signs of a held-out automotive camera at 90% precision and 94 frames per second.

</details>


### [28] [VISTA: A Multimodal Egocentric Benchmark for Goal-Oriented Perception and Assistance to Blind and Low-Vision Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22822)
*Mosca, Noé,Barhdadi, Mohamed Rayan,Hamza, Muhammad,Yeh, Joshua,Shahi, Suryodaya Bikram,Li, Zhiyan,Lali, Selim-Antoine,Li, Weiyue,Kurban, Hasan,Serpedin, Erchin,Luo, Gang,Zhang, Yue,Li, Minghan,Wang, Mengyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA：面向盲人及低视力用户的自我中心多模态基准，约1000段Project Aria录制，6路传感器输入、10类任务注释，评测14个视觉语言模型的零样本目标导向辅助能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对日常辅助有效性的检验：输入模态局限于视觉与文本，未利用可穿戴设备上的注视、惯性、空间轨迹等信号；评价偏重场景理解与问答，缺少目标条件化的引导式辅助评测。

Method: 构建VISTA基准（约1,000段Aria眼镜自我中心录制，同步传感流中6种作为模型输入，3类注释覆盖对象定位到危险检测等10个任务类别）；对14个代表性视觉语言模型做零样本评测，并提出整合全部6种模态的轻量多模态基线模型。

Result: 现有模型虽常能描述自我中心场景，但在生成目标导向的辅助性引导上仍表现有限；多模态融合基线相对表现更好。

Conclusion: VISTA提供了一个评估多模态模型日常可访问性辅助能力的基准，揭示场景描述能力与目标导向引导能力之间的显著差距。

Abstract: Blindness and low vision limit many people's ability to complete daily tasks, motivating AI assistants that provide useful guidance. Yet, existing benchmarks do not adequately test whether vision models can provide effective everyday assistance. In particular, i) existing benchmarks primarily rely on visual and textual inputs, with limited support for other modalities that are increasingly available in egocentric wearable devices such as gaze, inertial measurements, spatial trajectories, and others; and ii) existing evaluations emphasize scene understanding and question answering, with limited coverage of goal-conditioned guidance for blind and low-vision users across everyday tasks. To bridge this gap, we introduce VISTA (Visually Impaired Scene and Task Annotations), a multimodal egocentric benchmark for goal-oriented assistance for blind and low-vision users. VISTA contains $\sim$1,000 egocentric recordings captured with Project Aria glasses; each recording provides synchronized sensor streams, six of which serve as model inputs, and three different types of annotation, covering ten task categories (TC), from object localization to hazard detection. We evaluate 14 representative vision-language models zero-shot on VISTA and design a lightweight multimodal model baseline that integrates all six modalities. Experimental results show that, although existing models can often describe egocentric scenes, they remain limited in generating goal-directed assistive guidance. VISTA thus provides a benchmark for evaluating multimodal models that could improve everyday accessibility.

</details>


### [29] [VTrajEmbed: Aligning Text with Video Object Trajectories via Spatiotemporal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22842)
*Chen, Ruizhi,Fan, Zining,Guo, Yudi,Liu, Jiang,Lin, Zhiyu,He, Zhongjiang,Shi, Shouqian,Sun, Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTrajEmbed将视频分解为物体轨迹并做文本-轨迹细粒度对齐：用Slow-Fast双分支（密集低分辨率帧捕高频运动+稀疏高分辨率帧取场景上下文，共享LLM主干）编码轨迹，并用掩码引导对比学习（组内损失+批级双向损失、关系掩码排除未核实关系）训练，同时发布Text2Traj-Bench轨迹级基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本-视频嵌入模型擅长全局检索，但其单一全局表示把多个物体及其时空动态纠缠在一起，无法直接支持物体级grounding，因此需要轨迹级的细粒度对齐。

Method: (1) Slow-Fast Trajectory Encoding：双分支架构，密集采样低分辨率帧获取高频运动线索，稀疏采样高分辨率帧获取场景上下文，由共享LLM主干聚合为轨迹表示；(2) Mask-Guided Contrastive Learning：在关系掩码下耦合组内损失与批级双向损失，剔除未核实关系并防止已确认匹配被当作假负样本；(3) Text2Traj-Bench：模型辅助构建+人工校验查询—轨迹关系的数据集。

Result: VTrajEmbed-5B在text-to-trajectory与trajectory-to-text的R@1上分别比Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B高15.48和18.05个百分点；五个全局检索基准平均R@1为49.48%，对比基线42.86%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级分解表征配合掩码对比学习，可在参数更小（5B vs 8B）的情况下同时提升物体级文本-轨迹检索与全局检索，说明细粒度轨迹嵌入是超越单一全局表示的有效方向。

Abstract: Fine-grained alignment between text and video object trajectories is a core challenge in multimodal understanding. Existing embedding models excel at global retrieval, yet their single global representation entangles multiple objects and their spatiotemporal dynamics, limiting direct object-level grounding. To address this issue, we propose VTrajEmbed, an embedding model that decomposes a video into object trajectories and aligns each trajectory embedding with text for object-level grounding through retrieval. Our main contributions: (1)Slow-Fast Trajectory Encoding, a dual-branch architecture that establishes trajectory representations by densely sampling low-resolution frames for high-frequency motion cues and sparsely selecting high-resolution frames for scene context, aggregated by a shared LLM backbone; (2)Mask-Guided Contrastive Learning, a training objective built upon these representations that couples an intra-group loss and a batch-level bidirectional loss under a relation mask, excluding unverified relations and preventing confirmed matches from becoming false negatives; (3)Text2Traj-Bench, a trajectory-level text-video benchmark with model-assisted construction and human-verified query--trajectory relations. Experimental results show that VTrajEmbed-5B improves text-to-trajectory and trajectory-to-text R@1 over Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B by 15.48 and 18.05 percentage points, respectively, and achieves 49.48% average R@1 across five global retrieval benchmarks, compared with 42.86% for Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B.

</details>


### [30] [Variational and Decomposed method for Mitigating Bimodal Bias in Robust Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22845)
*Zhao, Yihan,Jiang, Yuncheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文分析VQA中的双模态偏置（multimodal bias），发现计数问题与非计数问题的偏置机制不同：计数偏置源于视觉侧并通过模态融合传播，非计数偏置主要源于语言偏置，据此提出VDB（变分分解式双模态偏置缓解）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往VQA去偏工作只关注语言偏置，近期虽注意到多模态偏置，但"多模态偏置究竟是什么"仍不清晰，缺乏对其成因机制的解释。

Method: 分别在计数与非计数问题上剖析偏置来源，给出解释：数量信息来自特定维度上多模态特征的幅值，故计数偏置源自视觉侧并经模态融合传播；非计数偏置以语言偏置为主、视觉偏置为辅。基于此提出VDB：用变分自编码器原理构建偏置模型，一个主要学习轻微视觉影响的语言偏置，另一个双模态偏置模型结合语言与视觉两侧，按不同比例分解并分别缓解。

Result: 在 VQACPv2、VQACPv1、GQAOOD 数据集上的实验支持其机制论断并验证方法有效性，同时对 VQAv2 上性能下降给出解释。

Conclusion: 计数与非计数问题的双模态偏置具有不同成因，需差异化建模；变分分解式双模态偏置建模可有效缓解偏置，但属VQA鲁棒性研究，与具身/机器人任务关联有限。

Abstract: Visual question answering (VQA) models are known to suffer from the bias issue, which refers to overfitting the regularities of frequent patterns. Previously, researchers only consider the language bias. Recently, the multimodal bias is noticed. However, what multimodal bias is remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate the multimodal bias in counting and non-counting questions. We find that they have distinct underlying mechanism, so their biases come from distinct reasons. We provide valuable interpretations of VQA counting mechanism: \textbf{quantity information stems from the magnitude of the multimodal features in specific dimensions.} Thus, counting bias stems from the vision side and propagates to multimodal features through modality fusion, while the non-counting bias stems from primarily language bias and slightly vision bias. Thus, we propose a method to deal with them differently: Variational and Decomposed method for mitigating Bimodal Bias (VDB). It extracts bimodal bias affected by different proportion of vision and language sides. We introduce variational bias models utilizing the principle of Variational Auto-Encoder to learn mainly language bias with slight vision effect, and a bimodal bias model for bimodal bias combining language and vision sides. Extensive experiments on VQACPv2, VQACPv1, and GQAOOD datasets demonstrate the rationality of our claim and the effectiveness of our method. We also provide explanation for the compromised performance on VQAv2.

</details>


### [31] [HAPACT: A Benchmark For Human-Centric Physical Impact Localization in Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22895)
*Kim, Youngrae,Jang, Yejin,Kim, Eunho,Sung, Youjin,Song, Kun-Woo,Kim, Sangpil,Yoon, Sang Ho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以人为中心的冲击定位新任务：在视频中定位冲击发生的时间段，并在人体表面（SMPL-X）上定位冲击位置；构建HAPACT数据集（源自电影片段，2.8K+视频-查询对，20K+细粒度标注帧），并给出基于视频时刻检索与人体-场景接触模型的基线评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频中的人体物理推理研究集中在接触、压力、姿态、运动等日常交互，对冲击（impact）这类短时、强力的物理事件研究不足；需要专门的数据与任务来刻画突发物理事件如何作用于人体。

Method: 构建HAPACT基准：以观众感知为标注依据、文本条件化定位、帧级时间监督与基于SMPL-X的人体空间监督。评测上，时序定位复用视频时刻检索（video moment retrieval）模型；空间定位复用人体-场景接触模型，并提出一个将定位重构为“查询引导的人体区域选择”的文本条件基线。

Result: 现有方法在两子任务上均表现不佳，物理冲击定位仍具挑战性，说明HAPACT作为专门基准具有必要性。

Conclusion: HAPACT填补了以人为中心的冲击事件推理空白，提供了可度量的基准与基线，但未提出强方法突破，属于基准/数据集类贡献，对具身物理推理有间接参考价值。

Abstract: Physical reasoning about humans in videos has largely focused on everyday interactions, where contact, pressure, pose, and motion support embodied perception and contact-aware applications. Still, short and forceful properties like impact events remain relatively underexplored, despite their relevance to broader understanding of how abrupt physical events affect the human body. To fill this gap, we introduce human-centric impact localization, a new task that localizes target physical impacts temporally within a video and spatially on the human body. We further present HAPACT, a HumAn-centric imPACT dataset and benchmark built from movie clips, containing over 2.8K video-query pairs with more than 20K fine-grained human-annotated frames. We built HAPACT around viewer-grounded annotation, text-conditioned localization, and supervision at frame-level temporal and SMPL-X-based spatial granularity. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our benchmark, we conduct extensive evaluations of existing methods, employing video moment retrieval models for temporal localization and human-scene contact models for spatial localization. For the spatial task, we propose a text-conditioned baseline by reformulating localization as query-guided body-region selection. Benchmark results show that physical impact localization remains challenging for existing methods, motivating HAPACT as a dedicated benchmark to support physical impact reasoning in videos.

</details>


### [32] [COMET: Decoupled Distillation, Routing, and Capacity Control for Task-Agnostic Continual Vision--Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22907)
*Woo, Seungyoon,Park, Keonhee,Kim, Gunhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COMET：面向零样本视觉语言模型的免任务身份连续学习框架，用冻结教师+三亲和蒸馏保持多模态几何、学生感知跨专家蒸馏塑造新专家、上下文赌博机做容量增删、重建式路由器做稀疏专家选择，在X-TAIL上验证各组件均有增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP类VLM的开放世界部署常需"任务不可知连续学习"（任务顺序到达、旧数据不可得、任务身份未知、骨干不可再训练）。作者认为该设置下VLM不仅面临稳定性-可塑性权衡，还受三种耦合压力制约：模态一致性漂移、表征空间干扰、逐任务容量不足。

Method: 冻结教师仅在共享图文池上提供特征参考；Tri-Affinity Distillation同时保持图像-图像、文本-文本、图文三种几何关系；Student-Aware Cross-Expert Distillation让新专家与兼容的旧专家对齐，使路由出错时性能平缓退化；容量控制与推理分离——上下文赌博机利用训练后信号决定保留/合并整任务专家，测试时用重建式路由器（不依赖教师语义）选稀疏专家。

Result: 在X-TAIL基准上消融显示所提各组件分别带来连续学习性能提升；摘要未给出具体数值与增益幅度。

Conclusion: 通过构造性地将特征参考、专家塑造、路由与容量控制解耦，COMET实现了多模态几何保持、累积专家协调与容量联合分配的统一框架；但证据仅限分类式基准，缺乏机器人与具身场景验证。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP retain zero-shot recognition ability, but their open-world use often requires task-agnostic continual learning: tasks arrive sequentially, past data are unavailable, task identity is unknown, and the backbone cannot be retrained. We argue that this setting fails VLMs through three coupled pressures beyond the stability-plasticity tradeoff: (1) modal-consistency drift, (2) representation-space interference, and (3) per-task capacity shortfall. We propose COMET (Collaborative Mixture-of-Expert Transfer) to resolve these three issues respectively as follows. A frozen teacher provides only a feature reference on a shared image-text pool; Tri-Affinity Distillation preserves image-image, text-text, and image-text geometry; and Student-Aware Cross-Expert Distillation aligns new experts with compatible prior experts so routing errors degrade gracefully. We design COMET to keep capacity and inference separate: a contextual bandit keeps or merges full per-task experts using post-training signals, while a reconstruction router selects sparse experts at test time without teacher semantics. On the X-TAIL benchmark, we show that each proposed component improves continual learning performance. COMET shows that continual vision-language adaptation can preserve multimodal geometry, coordinate accumulated experts, and allocate capacity jointly when feature reference, expert shaping, routing, and capacity control are separated by construction.

</details>


### [33] [MaskMerge: Structure-aware Token Merging for Efficient Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22921)
*Balaji, Bavesh,Jiang, Letian,Wong, Alexander,Clausi, David,Rambhatla, Sirisha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskMerge 用粗粒度中间掩码预测约束基于特征相似度的 token merging，避免同类相邻实例被错误合并（跨实例 token 融合），在 PanNuke/iSAID 上同时提升 mAP 并降低 FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ViT token merging 方法以特征相似度作为合并判据，对语义分割有效，但在实例分割中同类相邻物体特征高度相似，导致跨实例 token 融合、破坏实例边界，因此需要实例感知的合并约束。

Method: 提出 MaskMerge：利用网络中间层的粗粒度掩码预测作为实例先验，对基于特征相似度的 token 合并施加实例级约束（只在同一实例内合并），从而保留实例边界。

Result: PanNuke 上 mAP 30.1→32.1，计算量 229→153 GFLOPs，速度快 1.18x；iSAID 上 mAP 33.1→36.2，198→182 GFLOPs，快 1.23x；Cityscapes 上在更低 FLOPs 下持平 ALGM；收益可迁移到 MAE 预训练骨干。

Conclusion: 实例感知的 token 合并可在纯外观相似度不足以保证物体身份时实现更激进的压缩，兼顾精度与效率，并具备跨域与跨预训练骨干的泛化性。

Abstract: Token merging improves the efficiency of Vision Transformers by combining redundant tokens, but existing methods largely rely on feature similarity to determine which tokens to merge. This criterion is effective for semantic segmentation, yet can fail in instance segmentation, where adjacent objects of the same class often have highly similar features. We identify this failure mode as cross-instance token fusion. We introduce MaskMerge, an instance-aware token merging method that uses coarse intermediate mask predictions to constrain feature-based merging and preserve instance boundaries. We evaluate MaskMerge on two diverse instance segmentation domains characterized by high inter-instance visual similarity: PanNuke and iSAID. Against the SOTA token merging method ALGM, MaskMerge improves mAP from 30.1 to 32.1 on PanNuke while reducing computation from 229 to 153 GFLOPs and running 1.18x faster, and from 33.1 to 36.2 on iSAID while reducing computation from 198 to 182 GFLOPs and running 1.23x faster. Additionally, we also test it on Cityscapes and match ALGM's performance while using fewer FLOPs. The gains also transfer to an MAE pretrained backbone, demonstrating that instance-aware merging enables more aggressive compression when appearance similarity alone cannot reliably preserve object identity.

</details>


### [34] [ODSS-Net: Orthogonally Decoupled Shared-Specific Representations for Multi-Modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22922)
*Ma, Zhifeng,Feng, Yuhong,Huang, Chongyi,Zhou, Mingyang,Deng, Minwen*

Main category: cs.CV
