TL;DR: ODSS-Net：共享结构特征构建(SSFC)+正交特定特征学习(OSFL)+样本自适应专家混合(SAMoE)的多模态目标重识别框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态ReID中跨模态对齐易将模态共享的结构信息与模态特有线索纠缠，常规融合未能充分利用互补信息。

Method: SSFC抑制模态相关外观变化并提取共享结构特征；OSFL聚合身份相关局部信息并用几何正交投影剔除已对齐的共享分量；SAMoE以共享结构作为路由上下文自适应精炼并融合各模态特有特征。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上验证了有效性，但摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 正交解耦+自适应专家融合可缓解模态间隙与融合不足问题，为多模态ReID提供新范式。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification (ReID) leverages complementary information from heterogeneous sensors. Cross-modal alignment and fusion remain challenging. Existing methods mainly reduce modality gaps via feature alignment and fusion. However, alignment may entangle modality-shared structural information with modality-specific cues, while conventional fusion may not fully exploit complementary information. To address these issues, we propose ODSS-Net, a framework for orthogonally decoupled shared-specific representation learning and adaptive fusion of complementary modality-specific features. First, a Shared Structural Feature Construction (SSFC) module suppresses modality-dependent appearance variations and extracts modality-shared structural information. Then, an Orthogonal Specific Feature Learning (OSFL) module aggregates identity-relevant local information and removes the shared-aligned component via geometric orthogonal projection. Finally, a Sample Adaptive Mixture of Experts (SAMoE) module uses the shared structure as routing context to adaptively refine and fuse modality-specific features. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ODSS-Net for multi-modal object ReID.

</details>


### [35] [DICL-Qwen3VL: Decoupled Confidence Calibration for Interpretable Workwear Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22929)
*Wang, Meng,Zeng, Wu,Li, Mei,He, Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦可解释VLM框架DICL-Qwen3VL，用LoRA适配Qwen3-VL定位并识别工作服，分离"视觉观测"与"合规推理"两类置信度，并以GRPO联合优化检测、输出合法性与置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内工地光照差、遮挡多使衣着线索模糊，工装款式多样使合规判定困难；VLM虽能做定位与语义推理，但预测缺乏显式证据，单一置信度无法区分"看不清"与"判不准"两类不确定性。

Method: 1) 用LoRA微调Qwen3-VL，定位可见上半身衣着并判断是否为工装；2) 解耦校准：将视觉观测与合规推理分离，各赋置信度，视觉置信度以token熵与对图像扰动的敏感度为监督信号；3) 用GRPO联合优化检测精度、输出合法性与置信度校准。

Result: 在自采ICSWD与网络采集WICWD上mAP@50分别为0.730与0.753，优于所评估的LoRA适配VLM基线（含Qwen3-VL）；框架同时输出显式证据与分离置信度，支持人工复核；WICWD已开源。

Conclusion: 解耦式证据与双置信度设计对工业安全合规监测有实用价值，但属领域CV应用，缺乏机器人或3D/世界模型层面的方法洞见，可读性一般。

Abstract: Poor illumination and occlusion obscure clothing cues in indoor work sites, while diverse clothing styles complicate workwear compliance judgments. Although vision-language models (VLMs) support localization and semantic reasoning, their predictions often lack explicit supporting evidence, and a single confidence score cannot distinguish visual uncertainty from ambiguity in compliance judgments. We propose DICL-Qwen3VL, a decoupled interpretable VLM that integrates structured workwear detection, evidence generation, and separate visual and reasoning confidence estimates. First, low-rank adaptation (LoRA) adapts Qwen3-VL to localize visible upper-body clothing and identify workwear. Second, decoupled calibration separates visual observations from compliance reasoning and assigns a confidence score to each. Token entropy and sensitivity to image perturbations provide supervision for visual confidence, linking confidence estimation to the generated observations and their dependence on image content. Finally, group relative policy optimization (GRPO) jointly optimizes detection accuracy, output validity, and confidence calibration. Experiments on the company-collected Indoor Construction Site Workwear Dataset (ICSWD) and the web-collected Indoor Construction Workwear Dataset (WICWD) yield mAP@50 values of 0.730 and 0.753, respectively, outperforming the evaluated LoRA-adapted VLM baselines, including Qwen3-VL. The framework also provides explicit evidence and separate confidence scores to support manual review in industrial safety monitoring. WICWD is publicly available at https://github.com/DaErwen1/WICWD.

</details>


### [36] [Query-adaptive Recurrent Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22957)
*Balaji, Bavesh,Gopinath, Pranav,Sadeghi, Pouya,Rambhatla, Sirisha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QART：与主干无关的查询自适应循环视觉 tokenizer，按查询相关性决定视觉内容与 token 预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉/视觉语言模型基本与查询无关地编码图像，并对每个输入分配固定视觉 token 预算；但图像中值得提取的信息及其所需容量实质上取决于文本查询。

Method: 在冻结编码器特征基础上，QART 循环地构建紧凑视觉表示，并以"查询相关视觉支撑区域上的重建质量"作为停止准则，逐步追加 token 直到相关特征被足够高保真地表示；模块与主干无关。

Result: Visual7W 81.96%、VQAv2 76.37%，仅约 29 个视觉 token，较稠密 VLM 输入减少 20–25 倍；CORE 上 P@1 达 92.0%，PODS 上 PR-AUC 36.3%，超过 SteerViT 8.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 视觉表示在内容与容量上都无需固定，可按每个查询的需求自适应调整；结果验证了查询引导 + 预算自适应的可行性。

Abstract: Visual information worth extracting from an image and the token budget it warrants depend on the text query. Yet modern vision and vision-language models largely encode images independently of the query and allocate a fixed visual token budget to every input. We introduce the Query-Adaptive Recurrent Tokenizer (QART), a backbone-agnostic module that makes visual representations both query-steerable and budget-adaptive. QART steers the visual representation toward query-relevant evidence while adapting its token budget to the amount of information required. Its key principle is simple. Additional tokens are allocated until the visual features relevant to the query are represented with sufficient fidelity. Starting from frozen encoder features, QART recurrently constructs a compact representation and uses reconstruction quality over the query-relevant visual support to control token allocation. On Visual7W and VQAv2, QART achieves 81.96% and 76.37% accuracy using only about 29 visual tokens, reducing token counts by 20-25x relative to dense VLM inputs. It further reaches 92.0% P@1 on CORE and 36.3% PR-AUC on PODS, outperforming SteerViT on PODS by 8.4%. Together, these results show that visual representations need not be fixed in either content or capacity and can instead adapt to what each query requires.

</details>


### [37] [ANCHOR: Audio-Visually Grounded Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22964)
*Julin, Joel,Kundu, Souraja,Dahiya, Liza,Wei, George Z.,Xie, Liuyue,Yi, Jingru,Zhang, Xu,Yang, Hao,Jeni, Laszlo A.*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ANCHOR基准与评测框架，衡量多模态大模型音视频问答中"推理依据是否与感知证据对齐"，含2319个视频、8272个QA对及结构化推理链、时间区间、空间框和音频线索标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频问答基准只看最终答案正确率，无法判断模型给出的推理理由是否真的基于相关的视觉区域、时间片段和音频事件；正确答案可能与证据脱钩（"对而无论证"）。

Method: 构建ANCHOR数据集：2319视频/8272 QA对，含推理轨迹、时间证据、空间bbox与显著音频线索，另有224视频/975 QA对的人工精修测试集。解耦评估答案正确性、幻觉、逻辑偏离与简洁性，并在子集上直接测量时空grounding。对12个MLLM评测，并用人工精修、Gemini、GPT三种参考做一致性检验与去视频/去框/去音频的模态消融。

Result: 12个模型存在显著"对齐缺口"：答案表现相近的模型在解释是否被标注证据支持上差异明显；100视频/446 QA匹配子集上三种参考的排序高度稳定（平均Pearson r=0.99），排除模型-参考循环性；全人工测试集消融显示去除视频掉点38.4–41.8，去bbox掉3.2–7.7、去音频掉4.2–5.6，呈一致模态层级。

Conclusion: 仅以答案正确率评测会掩盖关键的grounding失败；ANCHOR可作为评估"准确且证据对齐"的音视频推理的测试平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) perform well on audio-visual question answering, but correct answers do not necessarily imply that their stated reasoning is grounded in relevant perceptual evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate final-answer accuracy, offering limited insight into whether model-generated rationales correctly identify visual regions, temporal intervals, and audio events supporting predictions. We introduce ANCHOR, a benchmark and evaluation framework for measuring grounded-rationale alignment in multimodal reasoning, comprising 2,319 videos and 8,272 question-answer pairs with structured reasoning traces, temporal evidence, spatial bounding boxes, and salient audio cues, including a human-refined test set of 224 videos and 975 question-answer pairs. We separately measure answer correctness, hallucination, logical divergence, and conciseness, with direct spatial and temporal grounding measurements on a focused subset. Across the full benchmark, twelve contemporary MLLMs show a substantial alignment gap: models with comparable answer performance can differ markedly in whether their explanations are supported by annotated evidence. On a matched subset of 100 videos and 446 question-answer pairs, rankings remain highly stable across human-refined, Gemini-generated, and GPT-generated references (mean Pearson r=0.99), providing evidence against model-reference circularity within this subset. Grounding-cue ablations of three MLLMs on the full human-refined test set reveal a consistent modality hierarchy: removing video reduces accuracy by 38.4-41.8 points, while removing bounding boxes (3.2-7.7 points) and audio (4.2-5.6 points) causes smaller, complementary, model-dependent drops. These findings show answer-only evaluation obscures important grounding failures and position ANCHOR as a testbed for evaluating accurate and evidence-aligned audio-visual reasoning.

</details>


### [38] [Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention for Efficient Inference in T2I/T2V Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22975)
*Xing, Bingxin,Zhang, Yu,Ding, Jiatong,Lu, Lei,Wang, Ping*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention：免训练的 DiT 注意力替换，用少量离线校准得到的静态 sink 掩码 + 轻量 pooled-KV 分支替代稠密自注意力，在 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 与 Z-Image-Turbo 上分别取得约 1.54×/1.57× 端到端加速且质量基本无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 已成为高质量文生图/文生视频的主流骨干，但随 token 序列增长及去噪步数众多，重复执行的稠密自注意力成为主要推理瓶颈。

Method: 研究发现预训练 DiT 的稠密注意力中存在按层/头区分、可复用的大质量（high-mass）结构；据此提出 Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention：在小型离线校准集上识别静态 sink 掩码，推理时复用该稀疏支撑，并以轻量 pooled-KV 分支补齐稀疏化损失的全局信息；模型权重与采样调度保持不变，属免训练（train-free）注意力替换。

Result: Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 上端到端加速 1.54×且生成质量保持；Z-Image-Turbo 上加速 1.57×，GenEval2 表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: 可复用、重要性感知的稀疏注意力支撑可作为 T2V 与 T2I DiT 通用的免训练加速路径；但仅在两个模型上验证，缺少与训练式稀疏注意力基线的系统性对比及开源代码信息。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become a common backbone for high-quality text-to-image and text-to-video generation, but repeated dense self-attention remains a major inference bottleneck as token sequences grow and the same operator is invoked across many denoising steps. Our study suggests that dense attention in pretrained DiTs contains reusable layer/head-specific high-mass structure that can be identified from a small offline calibration set and reused across future generations. This leads to Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention, a train-free attention replacement that reuses static sink masks and supplements them with a lightweight pooled-KV branch, while leaving model weights and sampling schedules unchanged. On Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, the method attains 1.54$\times$ end-to-end speedup while maintaining generation quality. On Z-Image-Turbo, it attains 1.57$\times$ end-to-end speedup with competitive GenEval2 performance. These results indicate that reusable importance-aware sparse support can provide an effective train-free acceleration path for both T2V and T2I DiTs.

</details>


### [39] [How Murky Before a VLM Knows It Cannot See? Accuracy, Confidence and Abstention on a Physical Visibility Axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22981)
*Fauzan, Mochamad Rizal,Surya, Irgi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文把VLM鲁棒性评测从无量纲"level 1-5"改为以米为单位的物理能见度轴（雾用Koschmieder模型、水下用纯水吸收谱缩放，结合单目度量深度），在1000张COCO图像和5个2-5B小模型上测量精度、内部置信度、口头置信度与显式弃答，发现模型在精度跌至随机时置信度仍高，形成"盲区"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM鲁棒性研究使用1-5级无量纲severity分级，缺乏物理单位，且很少追问模型置信度是否随可见度下降而同步下降；ImageNet-C类雾等级与实际物理能见度的对应关系也不清楚。

Method: 构建物理能见度轴V（250 m至1.95 m，八个对数间隔档），雾按Koschmieder模型、水下按纯水吸收谱缩放，结合单目度量深度图渲染；在1000个COCO场景上对五个2-5B开源小VLM做带框物体识别，同时测量准确率、token概率内部置信度、口头置信度与显式弃答选项，并定义盲区宽度W=log10(V_acc/V_aware)。

Result: 准确率在窄能见度带内跌至随机水平（V_acc：雾中12.1-15.9 m，水下20.5-38.6 m）；四个干净图上自信的模型在V=1.95 m时准确率已随机但内部置信度仍为0.68-0.84、口头置信度0.61-1.00，盲区宽度W≥0.79个数量级（约6.2倍能见度）；另一模型全程欠自信且内部置信度随准确率变化；指明原因的弃答随准确率变化（雾中W为-0.21至-0.06），泛化"I don't know"则滞后（W最高0.18）。ImageNet-C雾等级1-5仅对应55-110 m能见度，落在失效带之外，准确率变化在5个百分点内。

Conclusion: 无量纲severity等级严重低估了VLM在真实低能见度下的失效风险，模型存在精度与置信度脱钩的显著盲区，需以物理单位重新设计鲁棒性与弃答评测；结论为描述性，受单次采样、假定尺度深度和2-5B模型范围限制。

Abstract: Robustness studies of vision-language models (VLMs) grade degradation on nominal severity scales ("level 1-5") that carry no physical units, and they rarely ask whether a model's confidence follows the loss of visibility. We measure accuracy, internal (token-probability) confidence, verbalized confidence and an explicit abstain option on a visibility axis in metres: fog follows the Koschmieder model, an underwater arm scales the absorption spectrum of pure water, both are applied with a monocular metric depth map, and the visibility range V is swept over eight log-spaced levels from 250 m to 1.95 m. On 1000 COCO scenes and five small open VLMs (2-5 B parameters), accuracy on a boxed-object identification task falls from near its clean value to chance within a narrow band (half-way visibility V_acc of 12.1-15.9 m in fog and 20.5-38.6 m under water) while, for the four models that are confident on clean images, the confidence stays high: at V=1.95 m in fog their accuracy is at chance but their mean internal confidence is 0.68-0.84 and their verbalized confidence 0.61-1.00, a blind-spot width W=log10(V_acc/V_aware) of at least 0.79 decades, i.e. a factor of 6.2 in visibility. The remaining model is under-confident throughout and its internal confidence follows accuracy. An explicit abstain option follows accuracy when it names the reason (W from -0.21 to -0.06 in fog) but, for one model, lags with a generic "I don't know" (W up to 0.18). ImageNet-C fog severities 1-5 correspond to only 55-110 m of physical visibility, outside the failure band, and leave the accuracy of the models within 5 percentage points of its clean value. Blank-image and shuffled-image controls, a prior-matched answer set and a 200-image pilot that preceded the study are reported. Findings are descriptive: one draw of 1000 images, depth of assumed scale, and models of 2-5 B parameters.

</details>


### [40] [UniField++: A Unified Agentic Framework with Loop Reasoning for MRI Field-Strength Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22982)
*Lin, Yiyang,Wang, Chenhui,Yao, Tianliang,Li, Shuaibo,Sun, Zijun,Deng, Yiming,Yuan, Yixuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniField++ 提出双智能体（VLM 规划器 + flow-matching 增强器）框架，用跨场强统一学习与风险引导的区域化迭代精修，实现 64mT→3T、3T→7T 等 MRI 场强增强，减少解剖学幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低场 MRI 的可及性与高场影像质量之间存在鸿沟；现有增强方法易产生解剖学幻觉，原因有二：一是均匀处理使困难脑区（易幻觉区域）得不到足够关注，二是稀缺的配对数据限制了模型学习组织对比度与解剖可见性随场强变化的规律。

Method: 两智能体框架：VLM 规划器基于放射科医师对易幻觉区域的标注与对应修正方案训练，预测区域级风险并生成针对性恢复策略；flow-matching 增强器在轨迹上进行循环推理，依据计划对高风险区域反复处理以额外精修。多个场强转换任务（64mT→3T、3T→7T）统一训练，使规划器与增强器共享跨任务的场强相关影像特征。

Result: 相比对比方法平均提升 PSNR 2.74 dB、SSIM 14.05 个百分点；相比消融基线提升 3.97 dB 与 13.92 个百分点。在含未见场强的外部队列上保持增强质量，下游全脑分割 Macro Dice 较原始低场图像平均提升 0.1457。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 跨场强统一学习与风险引导的循环式区域精修可有效抑制解剖学幻觉，并实现跨未见场强的泛化与下游任务收益。

Abstract: MRI field-strength enhancement seeks to combine the accessibility of low-field scanners with the image quality of high-field scanners. Yet, existing methods remain prone to anatomical hallucinations, as uniform processing leaves difficult brain regions insufficiently examined and scarce paired data limit their learning of how tissue contrast and anatomical visibility change across field strengths. To address these challenges, we propose UniField++, a two-agent framework that unifies enhancement tasks across multiple field-strength transitions, including 64mT-to-3T and 3T-to-7T. Unified learning allows both the VLM-based planner and the flow-matching-based enhancer to draw on field-dependent image characteristics observed across tasks, rather than relying on a single transition. Trained with radiologists' annotations of hallucination-prone regions and corresponding correction plans, the planner predicts regional risk and formulates targeted restoration strategies. Guided by these plans, the enhancer performs loop reasoning along the flow-matching trajectory, repeatedly processing high-risk regions for additional refinement. Together, cross-transition learning and risk-guided refinement support anatomically faithful enhancement across field strengths. Across the evaluated tasks, UniField++ improves PSNR by an average of 2.74 dB and SSIM by 14.05 percentage points over comparison methods, with corresponding gains of 3.97 dB and 13.92 percentage points over the ablation baseline. It also maintains enhancement quality on an external cohort with an unseen field strength and improves downstream whole-brain segmentation Macro Dice by an average of 0.1457 over the original low-field images. Code is available at https://github.com/CUHK-AIM-Group/UniField_plusplus.

</details>


### [41] [A100: A Hybrid Vision Foundation Model for Agricultural Plant Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22991)
*Das, Mayur Pratim,Dubey, Ajai,P, Aksshay Mathew,Afreen, Sana,Soni, Rishi Kumar,Meena, Deepak,Kumari, Pratibha,Saini, Mukesh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: A100: 基于300万无标注农业图像自监督预训练的农业视觉基础模型，混合CNN-Transformer骨干，冻结即优于ImageNet-12K监督表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业视觉任务（作物监测、病害诊断、表型分析）缺乏专家标注数据，尤其在发展中国家；需减少对标注的依赖。

Method: 在超100种作物的300万无标注图像上做DINO自蒸馏+iBOT掩码块重建+KoLeo均匀性正则联合预训练；骨干为MBConv局部特征与Transformer全局上下文融合的混合CNN-Transformer。

Result: 冻结骨干作物分类95.37%（+2.56% vs ImageNet-12K），GroMo25叶片计数误差降10.4%；全微调后32类作物识别99.90%、44类病害检测99.02%；PlantDoc上64.89%超过CvT-13（63.56%）；1%标注（每类约284张）达98.01%，比FoMo4Wheat和LeafVision高14点以上。

Conclusion: 自监督农业基础模型在有限标注下具备强标签效率，计划公开预训练权重供农业从业者适配。

Abstract: The use of accurate vision models for crop monitoring, disease diagnosis, and plant phenotyping in agriculture is hindered by the lack of expertly annotated data, especially in developing countries. We propose A100, an agricultural vision foundation model that is pre-trained using self-supervised learning on 3 million unlabeled images from fields covering more than 100 crop species. A100 uses a hybrid CNN-Transformer backbone that fuses MBConv stages for local feature extraction with Transformer stages for global contextual representation. A100 uses pre-training with the integration of DINO self-distillation, iBOT masked patch reconstruction, and KoLeo uniformity regularization, resulting in features that are better than ImageNet-12K supervised representations for agricultural applications without any fine-tuning. By utilizing a frozen backbone, the accuracy for crop classification of A100 is 95.37%, which is +2.56% better than ImageNet-12K, and reduces the leaf count error by 10.4% on the GroMo25 phenotyping benchmark, proving that the same backbone can be used for classification and regression tasks. After full fine-tuning, the performance of A100 is 99.90% on 32-class crop identification and 99.02% on 44-class disease detection. On the difficult PlantDoc dataset, A100 outperforms all baselines, such as CvT-13 (64.89% against 63.56%). Remarkably, A100 obtains 98.01% accuracy on plant classification using just 1% of the labelled data (about 284 images per category), showing great label efficiency for use cases with limited labeling. Under an identical protocol, A100 also outperforms the publicly released agricultural foundation models FoMo4Wheat and LeafVision by more than 14 points at 1% labels. We plan to publicly release our pretrained model to allow agricultural professionals to adapt A100 for various plant analysis applications using limited annotations.

</details>


### [42] [BRICE: Brain-inspired Recurrent Illusory Contour Encoder for Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23001)
*Wang, Yunlei,Li, Dawei,Tang, Xue-song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出脑启发的循环错觉轮廓编码器BRICE，基于预测编码(PC)的反馈-前馈框架用于3D点云补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流点云补全方法反复堆叠注意力模块，偏离统一可解释的网络设计，且几乎仅用前馈连接传播信息，缺乏深层反馈；而哺乳动物V1区的前馈-反馈环路可生成错觉轮廓(IC)，本质是大脑的视觉补全机制。

Method: 构建基于预测编码(Predictive Coding)基础模型的循环结构编码器BRICE，通过前馈-反馈回路"想象"不完整点云中缺失的部分；设计为即插即用模块，可无缝嵌入主流点云补全基线。

Result: 在MVP2K、PCN、PlantPCom等多个3D数据集上，BRICE 可提升多种补全基线的性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 神经科学启发的前馈-反馈循环架构是点云补全的有效且可解释的替代方案，作为插件具良好通用性。

Abstract: Mainstream point cloud completion methods are unanimously based on deep learning architectures. On one hand, these methods tend to repetitively stacking attention modules to enhance network performance, which drifts away from the neat design of unified and interpret-able networks; on the other hand, nearly all existing architectures rely solely on feed-forward connections to propagate information, neglecting feedback from deeper layers. The recurrent neural architecture, formed by feedforward-feedback loops, have shown its ability to generate Illusory Contours (ICs) in the V1 brain region of mammals; and ICs are essentially a completion mechanism of the brain for visual information. Motivated by this, we design a Brain-inspired Recurrent Illusory Contour Encoder (BRICE) to "imagine" what is missing in the incomplete 3D point clouds. Building upon the foundation model of Predictive Coding (PC), the plug-and-play nature enables BRICE to be seamlessly integrated into mainstream point cloud completion baselines. Experimental results show that BRICE can enhance various completion baselines on different 3D datasets such as MVP2K, PCN, and PlantPCom. Our code: Https://github.com/wangyunlei-wyl/BRICE.

</details>


### [43] [Semantic-Critical Regions for Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23021)
*Fang, Yun,Guo, Wei,Liang, Zhuonan,Wang, Heng,Cai, Weidong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCR-V2A：定位视频中与发声相关的细粒度视觉区域（语义关键区域SCR），以此作为显式视觉条件驱动视频转音频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2A方法依赖全局视觉表征的隐式条件或时序/空间线索的显式控制，难以捕捉细微的“发声—示声”交互，导致声音事件语义与时序动态的视觉条件不够精确。

Method: 先用声音相关的视觉定位识别SCR区域，再通过基于adapter的双路视觉编码（全局上下文+细粒度发声证据）将定位区域转为控制表征，条件化下游音频生成模型。

Result: 在域内与域外基准上，分布匹配与音频质量表现强，跨模态对齐具竞争力，且可训练参数很少。

Conclusion: 优先对细粒度、发声相关的视觉区域建模对V2A生成有价值，SCR式区域级条件是可复用思路。

Abstract: In real-world videos, sound events are often associated with localized and fine-grained visual cues, such as object motion, object-part dynamics, contact events, and interactions between objects. Existing video-to-audio (V2A) methods employ implicit conditioning based on global visual representations or explicit control derived from temporal and spatial cues. However, subtle sound-producing and sound-indicative interactions remain insufficiently captured, leaving room for more precise visual conditioning of sound-event semantics and temporal dynamics. Motivated by this observation, we refer to regions containing such sound-relevant visual evidence as Semantic-Critical Regions (SCRs) and introduce SCR-V2A, a V2A generation framework that explicitly localizes and encodes SCRs as fine-grained visual guidance for audio generation. Specifically, we first identify SCRs through sound-related visual localization. We then transform the localized regions into a control representation through adapter-based dual visual encoding, which jointly captures global visual context and fine-grained sound-relevant evidence. The representation is used to condition a downstream audio generation model. Experiments on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that SCR-V2A achieves strong performance in distribution matching and audio quality while remaining competitive in cross-modal alignment and requiring few trainable parameters. These results highlight the value of prioritizing fine-grained, sound-relevant visual regions for V2A generation.

</details>


### [44] [Frame-Consistent Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23039)
*Vinasirajan, Viruthshaan,Rodrigo, Ranga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出，冻结位姿估计器 VGGT 与冻结重建骨干 TokenGS 组合时质量下降的主因并非位姿噪声，而是坐标系不一致与平移尺度失配两个可确定性纠正的集成故障；修正后无需训练即可达到接近 COLMAP 参考的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段前馈高斯方法（位姿估计 + 重建）各自专精更优，但 VGGT-X、3R-GS 等先前工作将质量退化归因于位姿噪声并引入学习式精修。作者希望验证这一归因是否正确，找出真正的瓶颈。

Method: 在 DL3DV 与 MipNeRF360 共 107 个场景上，以 COLMAP 位姿为参考做逐相机分析；在 140 个 DL3DV 场景上做受控消融，考察位姿误差与集成层面的影响。

Result: 对齐后旋转误差中位数仅 1.6°，而平移尺度存在数据集一致的大幅偏移；消融表明瓶颈是坐标系不一致与平移尺度失配。确定性修正后，冻结 VGGT + 冻结 TokenGS 达 22.47 PSNR，距 COLMAP 参考仅 0.51 dB，且无需训练；稀疏四上下文图像设定下同样达到该质量。

Conclusion: 质量退化的根因是可确定性纠正的集成故障而非位姿噪声，学习式精修并非必需；由此可实现无 COLMAP、无精修的全流程前馈重建管线。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian models have made novel view synthesis almost instantaneous. Feed-forward methods predict Gaussians directly, either in two stages or in a single merged pass. Using two separately trained models is attractive because each can specialize in its own objective: one for accurate pose estimation and the other for high-quality reconstruction, rather than forcing a single jointly trained network to compromise between the two. A natural instantiation pairs a frozen pose estimator, VGGT, with a frozen reconstruction backbone, TokenGS. Prior work, such as VGGT-X and 3R-GS, also adopts a two-stage design, but attributes the resulting quality degradation to noisy pose estimates and introduces learned refinement. We show that this is not the primary source of the problem. Through per-camera analysis across 107 scenes from two datasets, DL3DV and MipNeRF360, using COLMAP poses as the reference, we find that the rotation error after alignment is small (median 1.6 degrees), whereas the translation scale is offset by a large, dataset-consistent factor. Controlled ablations over 140 DL3DV scenes show that the primary obstacles are two integration failures rather than pose error: a coordinate-frame inconsistency and a translation-scale mismatch, both of which can be corrected deterministically. With these fixes, a frozen VGGT driving a frozen TokenGS achieves 22.47 PSNR, within 0.51 dB of the COLMAP reference, with no additional training, when poses are estimated from all scene views. In the sparse setting, where reconstruction uses only four context images under the standard pose-free protocol, the same pipeline matches this all-view quality, yielding a fully COLMAP-free pipeline without any refinement step.

</details>


### [45] [TriFold: Folding irregular 3D Gaussians into structured latents for 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23050)
*Wang, Yun,Wang, Chiyu,Jiang, Fan,Zhu, Ziyu,Sun, Zhaoxu,Fang, Guangchi,Xu, Mu,Wu, Dapeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriFold: 一个原生3D高斯场景VAE，将高斯场景编码为固定布局triplane潜变量，并用单元级多槽查询编码、占用预测+集合预测式解码联合重建几何与外观，再基于该潜空间训练flow-matching模型实现点云条件与单图条件（amodal）3DGS生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生3D高斯场景数量可变、空间密度不均、相邻基元几何外观差异大，导致难以学习紧凑、固定结构的生成式潜表示；现有方法多依赖单特征池化或非原生表示，损失信息且破坏空间自适应性。

Method: 编码端：将高斯投影到triplane网格，每个cell使用cell-wise可学习查询将投影高斯聚合为多个slot，避免单特征池化的信息损失；解码端：先预测占用确定活跃区域，再在活跃cell内用query-based set prediction与候选选择重建变数量局部高斯集合，从而在固定布局潜空间上保留高斯空间自适应性。随后在该潜空间上训练latent flow-matching模型，条件为点云或单张图像。

Result: 实验表明TriFold实现高保真重建，并能在仅给定单张图像（部分观测）条件下生成完整场景，支持点云条件合成与单图条件amodal生成。

Conclusion: 通过“固定布局潜空间 + 保留空间自适应性的编解码”设计，原生3D高斯VAE可有效支撑场景级3DGS生成任务，为单图amodal场景生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D generation has motivated growing interest in compact generative representations of complex 3D scenes. For native 3D Gaussian scenes, however, learning such representations remains challenging due to widely varying primitive counts, nonuniform spatial density, and differences in geometry and appearance even among spatially neighboring primitives. In this work, we introduce TriFold, a native 3D Gaussian VAE that encodes Gaussian scenes into a fixed-layout triplane latent and jointly reconstructs their geometry and appearance. The key insight is to impose structural regularity on the latent space while preserving the spatial adaptivity of the underlying Gaussians. During encoding, we use cell-wise learned queries to aggregate projected Gaussians into multiple slots per cell, reducing the information loss caused by single-feature pooling. During decoding, occupancy prediction identifies active regions, where query-based set prediction and candidate selection reconstruct local Gaussian sets with spatially varying primitive counts. Building on this representation, we train a latent flow-matching model for conditional scene-level 3DGS generation, enabling point-cloud-conditioned synthesis and single-image-conditioned amodal generation. Experiments demonstrate that TriFold achieves high-fidelity reconstruction and generation from a single image that provides only a partial observation of the scene, validating its effectiveness for native 3D Gaussian representation learning. https://fantasy-amap.github.io/TriFold

</details>


### [46] [Scene-Designer: Learning Design Intelligence for Agentic 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23121)
*Liu, Xuanyu,Feng, Hongyan,Pan, Miao,Chen, Sunlai,Liang, Lizhou,Sun, Bing,Zhang, Wenqi,Zhang, Xuhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scene-Designer：通过"设计+批评"双经验训练的智能体框架，用 Scene-OTA-35K / Scene-OTC-15K 两个数据集与 Critic 引导的 Scene-GRPO，从自然语言生成物理合理、功能正确、布局连贯的 3D 室内场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到 3D 场景方法要么依赖缺乏领域设计经验的通用模型，要么仅从静态场景中学习，缺少"设计—批判—修正"的闭环设计智能，导致场景物理不合理、功能错误、反复试错成本高。

Method: 构建两个 OTA（Observation–Thought–Action）与 OTC（Observation–Thought–Critic）监督数据集；提出 Scene-Designer 智能体框架进行设计与纠错；提出 Scene-GRPO，一种 Critic 引导的 GRPO 强化学习框架，并在测试时引入 Critic 指导以减少无效试错。

Result: 在物理有效性、功能正确性与布局连贯性上均优于基线；学到的 Scene-Critic 在场景批评任务上显著超过通用模型；测试时 Critic 引导可进一步提升生成质量，且设计与批评能力呈相互增强趋势。

Conclusion: 将"设计经验"与"批评经验"结合并用于强化学习，可有效提升语言驱动的 3D 场景生成质量与效率，Critic 在训练与推理阶段均有增益，构成可迭代的设计智能范式。

Abstract: Generating complete, coherent, and usable 3D indoor scenes from natural language requires strong \textbf{design intelligence}: the ability to design high-quality scenes and critique their quality. However, existing approaches either rely on general-purpose models lacking domain-specific design experience or learn from static scenes. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Scene-Designer}, an agentic framework that learns design intelligence through complementary design and critique experiences. We construct two datasets: \textbf{Scene-OTA-35K}, which provides Observation--Thought--Action supervision for learning scene design and efficient error correction, and \textbf{Scene-OTC-15K}, which provides Observation--Thought--Critic supervision for learning scene quality critique across physical validity, functional correctness, and layout coherence. Based on these experiences, we propose \textbf{Scene-GRPO}, a Critic-guided GRPO framework that improves design decisions and reduces unnecessary trial-and-error corrections. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Scene-Designer achieves superior performance in physical validity, functional correctness, and layout coherence, while the learned Scene-Critic significantly outperforms general-purpose models in scene critique. Moreover, test-time Critic guidance further improves scene generation, showing that the Critic can provide useful guidance beyond training. Additional analysis suggests a trend of mutual enhancement between scene design and critique abilities.

</details>


### [47] [Reliability-Consistent Appearance Modeling for RGB-Infrared Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23125)
*Wang, Bingyang,Yu, Jiazuo,Zhang, Lu,Zhang, Lihe,Lu, Huchuan,He, You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RCA-MOT 提出可靠性一致的外观建模框架，将 RGB、红外与融合身份表征作为可同时访问的外观状态分别保存，并用免训练的目标场景估计器为每个检测生成分支级可靠性，用于分支调度、边界候选校准与记忆更新强度控制，在 VT-MOT 与 UniRTL 上刷新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RGB-红外多目标跟踪方法通常把两种模态证据压缩为统一描述子，一旦某模态短期退化，跟踪器无法单独调控其贡献，不可靠证据会污染关联结果与外观记忆。

Method: 保留 RGB 专属、红外专属与融合三类身份表征作为联合可访问的外观状态；对每个检测用简单的免训练目标场景估计器输出分支级外观可靠性。相对可靠性用于调节不同身份分支的权重，绝对可靠性用于校准 ReID 接受边界附近的临界候选，同一分支级可靠性还用于控制匹配描述子对历史外观记忆的更新强度。各组件刻意保持简单，以凸显可靠性可控的外观组织机制而非复杂融合或学习式可靠性估计。

Result: 在 VT-MOT 与 UniRTL 上分别较此前最佳方法提升 HOTA 5.402/6.075 点、IDF1 11.549/12.789 点；事后分析进一步验证可靠性作为信息性控制信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过保持模态表征分离并显式建模可靠性，可在不引入复杂融合模块的前提下显著提升跨模态 MOT 性能，说明"表征后证据可控"是可复用的设计原则。

Abstract: Recent RGB-infrared multi-object tracking (MOT) methods have substantially improved cross-modal representation learning and multimodal information integration. However, most existing methods compress RGB and infrared evidence into a unified descriptor. When one modality is temporarily degraded, the tracker can no longer regulate its contribution separately; unreliable evidence may distort association and contaminate appearance memory. We therefore propose RCA-MOT, a reliability-consistent appearance modeling framework that preserves cross-modal complementarity while retaining post-representation evidence controllability. RGB-specific, infrared-specific, and fused identity representations are maintained as jointly accessible appearance states. For each detection, a simple training-free target-scene estimator produces branch-wise appearance reliability used across the two temporal roles of appearance: relative reliability regulates different identity branches, absolute reliability calibrates borderline candidates near the ReID acceptance boundary, and the same branch-wise reliability controls how strongly matched descriptors update historical appearance memory. The individual components are intentionally simple, focusing evaluation on reliability-controllable appearance organization rather than elaborate fusion or learned reliability-estimation modules. RCA-MOT sets new SOTA results on VT-MOT and UniRTL, improving the previous best HOTA by 5.402/6.075 points and IDF1 by 11.549/12.789 points, respectively, while post-hoc analyses further validate reliability as an informative control signal. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [48] [From GAN-Based Models to GPT-5: A Taxonomy-Guided Study of Human Performance in Detecting Deepfake Face Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23144)
*Guo, Zhiqing,Jia, Lixin,Sun, Luning,Chen, Xiaowei,Yu, Guofeng,Wang, Liejun,Yang, Gaobo,Stillwell, David,Holz, Thorsten,Li, Shujun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出深伪人脸伪影分类法（DFAT），并在线实验（n=524）比较通用训练与模型专属训练对五类生成模型（3个GAN、1个扩散模型、MLLM GPT-5）深伪图像检测能力的影响，含五个月随访（n=342）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式深伪（GAN→扩散模型→多模态大模型）快速演进，但人类检测能力的实证研究滞后：尚不清楚检测表现是否随生成模型变化，以及模型专属训练是否优于通用训练。

Method: 构建跨模型的“深伪人脸伪影分类法”（DFAT），据此制作通用与模型专属训练材料；在线受控实验（n=524）测量检测准确率，覆盖5个代表性生成模型；五个月后进行追踪测试（n=342）评估保持性与主观压力。

Result: 检测准确率高度依赖生成模型，且更先进的新模型生成图像并非更难被检出；训练后多数条件下显著提升，且提升幅度因模型而异；模型专属训练在数值上普遍优于通用训练；五个月随访准确率无显著下降、感知压力无显著上升。

Conclusion: 为设计有效、可持续、针对特定生成模型的深伪检测培训方案提供可操作依据，提示需按模型定制训练而非仅依赖通用训练。

Abstract: Generative models for deepfakes have rapidly evolved from GAN-based architectures to diffusion models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, empirical research on human performance in detecting deepfake face images has not kept pace. It remains unclear if (and how) detection performance varies across generative models and whether model-specific training improves detection more effectively than general-purpose training. In this paper, we propose the \emph{Deepfake Face Artifact Taxonomy} (DFAT), a cross-model taxonomy of human-recognizable visual artifacts in deepfake face images, based on which we build both general-purpose and model-specific training materials. Through an online experiment ($n = 524$), we examined human performance in detecting deepfake face images and evaluated training efficacy across five representative generative models: three GAN-based, one diffusion-based, and one MLLM (GPT-5). Our results show that participants' detection accuracy was strongly model-dependent and that images generated by more recent and advanced models were not necessarily harder to detect. After training, participants achieved significant performance gains across most conditions, and their improvement varied by model. Notably, model-specific training generally yielded greater numerical gains than general-purpose training. In a five-month follow-up test ($n = 342$), we observed no significant decline in detection accuracy and no significant increase in perceived stress. Overall, our findings provide actionable insights for designing effective, sustainable, and targeted training programs to counter the growing threat posed by deepfakes.

</details>


### [49] [Knowledge-Guided Distributional Prompt Learning with Visual Hierarchy for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23172)
*Ham, Jiyul,Yoon, Seongho,Jung, Yonggon,Lee, Sangjae,Baek, Jun-Geol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiDist: a zero-shot anomaly detection framework that models prompt tokens as diagonal Gaussians, parameterizes anomalous prompt distributions as deviations from normal references, and specializes them into global/local prompts for image-level detection and pixel-level localization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing vision-language ZSAD methods improve alignment via textual-visual matching and distributional prompting, but fail to explicitly couple normality-referenced distribution modeling with hierarchy-specific prompt specialization, limiting joint image-level detection and fine-grained localization quality.

Method: Each prompt token is modeled as a diagonal Gaussian with Monte Carlo sampling to produce stochastic prompt variants; anomalous prompt distributions are parameterized as deviations from learned normal references rather than learned independently; distributions are specialized into global (image-level) and local (pixel-level) representations, with an adapted global visual representation guiding hierarchical alignment.

Result: Experiments on seven industrial and medical datasets show strong cross-dataset performance in anomaly detection and localization; ablations and representation analyses support the distributional modeling and hierarchy-specific design.

Conclusion: Explicit normality-referenced distributional prompting plus hierarchy-specific prompt specialization outperforms independent distributional prompting for zero-shot anomaly detection and localization.

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) aims to detect anomalies in previously unseen classes using auxiliary images, with applications such as industrial visual inspection and medical imaging. Recent vision-language-model-based approaches improve ZSAD by aligning textual and visual representations, while distributional prompting further models prompt variability for unseen categories. However, existing methods do not explicitly combine normality-referenced distributional modeling with hierarchy-specific prompt specialization, limiting their ability to model anomalous semantics relative to normality while addressing both image-level detection and fine-grained localization. We propose Hierarchical Knowledge-Guided Distributional Prompt Learning (HiDist), a normality-referenced hierarchical distributional prompt framework for ZSAD. HiDist models each prompt token with a diagonal Gaussian distribution and generates stochastic prompt variations through Monte Carlo sampling. Rather than learning normal and anomalous distributions independently, anomalous prompt distributions are parameterized as deviations from learned normal references, explicitly encoding their dependency. The resulting distributions are specialized into global and local representations for image-level detection and pixel-level localization, respectively, while an adapted global visual representation guides hierarchical alignment. Experiments on seven industrial and medical datasets demonstrate strong cross-dataset performance across anomaly detection and localization tasks. Extensive ablation and representation analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed distributional modeling and hierarchy-specific representation learning.

</details>


### [50] [MLLM Frame Subset Ensembling for Audio-Visual Video QA and MLLM-based Reranking for Ad-hoc Video Search in TRECVID 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23214)
*Goulas, Andreas,Galanopoulos, Damianos,Patras, Ioannis,Mezaris, Vasileios*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CERTH-ITI 在 TRECVID 2025 AVS/VQA 赛道的系统概述：AVS 用多 VLM 初检索 + MLLM 重排序的两阶段免训练框架；VQA 微调音视频 MLLM 并用帧子集集成与多数投票/均值池化解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRECVID AVS 与 VQA 赛道需要在不进行大规模训练的前提下利用基础模型提升开放域文本-视频检索与视频问答效果，并为竞赛提供可复现的系统方案。

Method: AVS：第一阶段用 LLM 对查询做改写增强，多视觉-语言模型编码查询与候选镜头，加权融合相似度初检；第二阶段对 top-N 镜头用多模态 LLM 做语义对齐重排序，给出更新相关性分数，全程零训练。VQA：在 TRECVID 训练集上微调音视频 MLLM，并采用推理时缩放（inference-time scaling）；AG 任务从视频的不同随机帧子集贪婪采样多份回答，多数投票聚合；MC 任务对各候选答案的 logits 做均值池化。

Result: VQA AG 任务在 3 项指标上取得最高分，VQA MC 任务取得第二名。

Conclusion: 表明多模型融合+LLM 重排序可免训练地提升文本-视频检索精度，而微调结合帧子集集成与推理时缩放对视频问答有效；属竞赛系统报告，未披露绝对性能数值、消融与开源细节。

Abstract: This paper presents the overview of the runs related to the Ad-hoc Video Search (AVS) and Video Question Answering (VQA) tracks of TRECVID 2025 on behalf of the CERTH-ITI team. For the AVS track, we introduce a two-stage framework built on foundation models. In the first stage, multiple vision-language models (VLMs) encode both the input query, augmented through LLM-generated rephrasings, and the candidate video shots, producing weighted similarity scores for initial retrieval. In the second stage, we utilize a Multimodal-LLM(MLLM)-based reranking module that evaluates the semantic alignment between each shot among the top-N highest-ranked ones and the original query, generating updated relevance scores for reordering these shots. This MLLM-driven reranking significantly improves contextual matching and produces more accurate final rankings without requiring any model training. Regarding the VQA track, we fine-tune an audio-visual MLLM model on the provided TRECVID training dataset and we implement an inference-time scaling technique to enhance the multimodal understanding capabilities of the MLLM. For the open-ended Answer Generation (AG) task, we aggregate multiple model responses per question via a majority vote. The responses are generated with greedy sampling from different random frame subsets of the video and they are ranked based on the number of votes. For the Multiple-Choice (MC) task, instead of voting, we use mean pooling on the logits assigned by the fine-tuned model to each candidate response. Through the combination of fine-tuning and frame subset ensembling we achieve the highest score across 3 metrics in the VQA AG task and the second highest in the VQA MC task.

</details>


### [51] [RAP: A Dataset for Paper-to-Poster Generation via Declarative Markup from Human-Crafted Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23230)
*Han, Haonan,Lin, Yukang,Lin, Minliang,Tan, Haoxian,He, Jun-Yan,Yang, Rui,Cheng, Zhi-Qi,Hu, Jie,Wan, Guanglu,Li, Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAP：把论文转海报当作"视觉编程"任务的25K+论文-海报三元组数据集与基准，含自动反渲染出可重填槽位的HTML/CSS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论文转海报基准规模小、仅标注布局框，导致评测只能依赖VLM打分或人工清单，缺乏客观的渲染比对；且方法从零生成布局，人工设计海报无法作为可检索设计库或验证监督。

Method: 构建RAP数据集：2022-2026年多机器学习会议25K+经核验的论文-海报对，带三层内容分类法与150篇留出测试集；提出全自动反渲染管线，将海报转为带类型化、可重填槽位的styled HTML/CSS，形成可由渲染比对验证的论文-海报-标记三元组；提供PosterRAG（免训练多智能体检索样例并仅重填槽、不改布局样式）及SFT+RL训练专用模型两种用法。

Result: 得到可渲染比对的客观评测管线与大规模验证三元组语料，支撑训练-free检索式流水线与SFT+RL模型两条基线，并提供在线demo。

Conclusion: 将海报制作形式化为可反渲染、可验证的视觉编程任务，使人工海报兼具设计库与监督价值。

Abstract: Making a conference poster is visual coding in miniature: an entire paper must be compressed onto a single page, which exercises summarization, visual design, and code implementation at once, using nothing beyond the paper itself. Yet benchmarks for this task in the context of visual coding remain scarce: existing resources are small and annotated only with layout boxes, so evaluation must fall back on VLM-as-judge or human checklists rather than objective render-and-compare, and the generation methods they support design layouts from scratch, leaving human-crafted posters unusable as either a retrievable design library or as verified supervision. We introduce RAP (Reconstruct A Poster), a dataset and benchmark for paper-to-poster generation as a visual coding task. RAP contains 25K+ verified paper-poster pairs from various machine learning conferences held between 2022 and 2026, annotated with a three-level content taxonomy, plus a held-out benchmark of 150 test papers each paired with its human poster. A fully automated derendering pipeline turns each poster into styled HTML/CSS of typed, refillable slots, so every sample is a paper-poster-markup triplet verifiable by rendering against the source poster. The corpus serves both inference and training: PosterRAG, a training-free multi-agent pipeline, retrieves an exemplar and refills its slots without touching layout or styling, while an SFT+RL recipe trains a dedicated model from the same triplets. An online demo is available at https://reconstruct-a-poster.github.io/.

</details>


### [52] [X-Splat: Unified Gaussian Splatting for Joint RGB and X-ray 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23237)
*Xu, Wangze,Zhou, Chu,Yamaguchi, Mae,Dai, Linrui,Shen, I-Chao,Ijiri, Takashi,Sato, Imari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一高斯表示联合重建RGB与X-ray：所有高斯基元共享同一套几何参数，仅表面附近基元附带外观属性；通过干净的多视角立体与X-ray几何代理做跨模态配准，而非对齐两组优化好的高斯，并联合优化加表面深度一致性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB+X-ray联合重建可连接外观与内部结构，但现有方法按模态分别训练，存在几何冗余、对齐伪影与松耦合问题，难以发现跨模态洞察。

Method: 统一几何的高斯基元表示（表面前沿基元额外带外观属性）；用MVS与X-ray几何代理进行跨模态配准以提升稳定性；双模态监督下联合优化，并引入X-ray几何导出的表面-深度一致性损失指导RGB重建。

Result: 在InsideOut数据集与自建Flower & Insect数据集上，跨模态对齐更鲁棒，RGB渲染质量更高且不牺牲X-ray重建，表示比两套独立几何更紧凑。

Conclusion: 共享几何、分离外观的统一表示是耦合外观与内部结构的有效范式，可同时改善对齐稳定性、渲染质量与表示紧凑性。

Abstract: Jointly reconstructing RGB and X-ray provides new insights by connecting external appearance with internal structure, enabling a more complete understanding of real-world objects than single modality. Yet existing methods train separate models per modality and suffer from redundant geometry, alignment artifacts, and loose coupling that limits cross-modal insight discovery. We propose a unified representation in which all Gaussian primitives share a single set of geometric parameters, with only surface-proximal primitives additionally carrying appearance attributes. We perform cross-modal registration through clean multi-view stereo and X-ray geometric proxies instead of aligning two sets of optimized Gaussians, improving alignment stability. We then jointly optimize the unified representation via supervision from both modalities, complemented by a surface-depth consistency loss derived from X-ray geometry to guide RGB reconstruction. Experiments on the InsideOut dataset and our Flower & Insect dataset show that our framework achieves more robust cross-modal alignment, higher RGB rendering quality without compromising X-ray reconstruction, and a more compact representation than maintaining two independent geometries.

</details>


### [53] [Kingfisher: Fast Personalized Visual Autoregressive Generation via Scale-Aware Prompt-Conditioned Scale Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23238)
*Vo, Dinh-Khoi,Sugimoto, Akihiro,Tran, Minh-Triet,Le, Trung-Nghia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kingfisher：在冻结的视觉自回归（VAR）生成器上做免训练个性化，利用早期尺度码决定结构/风格、后期尺度仅精修的性质，通过提示条件尺度码子空间分解内容与风格码，再重组为紧凑语义前缀，实现快速图像个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型个性化依赖权重/文本嵌入/LoRA/Adapter 的逐主体优化，准备时间长。作者希望借助 VAR 的尺度空间结构实现快速、免调优的个性化。

Method: 观察早期自回归尺度码的前缀即可强决定物体结构与全局外观；遂将早期残差尺度码经提示条件子空间分解为内容分量与风格分量，把图A内容+图B风格重组为语义前缀，冻结生成器通过逐位自纠错（bitwise self-correction）预测后续尺度。

Result: CSD-100 上内容保持强、风格与文本对齐最佳；配对准备时间较扩散方法约快 54×，较 VAR 类方法快 7.5×。

Conclusion: 直接操纵冻结 VAR 生成器的原生尺度空间表示即可有效实现个性化，为生成模型个性化提供免训练、高效率的新范式。

Abstract: Personalized image generation has been extensively studied in diffusion models, often relying on subject-specific optimization of model weights, textual embeddings, LoRA modules, or adapters. In this work, we explore a different direction for fast personalization in visual autoregressive models. Our key observation is that a short prefix of early autoregressive scale codes is sufficient to strongly determine both object structure and global appearance, while later scales primarily refine the established generation trajectory. Based on this insight, we introduce Kingfisher, a model-tuning-free framework that decomposes early residual scale codes into content- and style-related components through prompt-conditioned scale-code subspaces. Given two real images, Kingfisher first decomposes their early scale codes, then recomposes the content components of one image with the style components of the other into a compact semantic prefix. The frozen generator subsequently predicts the remaining scales through bitwise self-correction for coherent refinement. Experiments on CSD-100 show that Kingfisher achieves strong content preservation and the highest style and text alignment among the evaluated methods, while reducing pair-specific preparation time by approximately 54x compared with diffusion-based methods and 7.5x compared with VAR-based alternatives. These results demonstrate that effective personalization can be achieved by directly manipulating the native scale-space representation of a frozen visual autoregressive generator.

</details>


### [54] [Can the Parts Fool the Test? Counterfactual Pairing Cycles for Relational OOD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23248)
*Zhou, Yibo,Xiang, Yirong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"反事实配对循环"评测原语：用同样两张图、同样两条文本在比较两侧复用，只改变图文配对，使图像单模态、文本单模态与加性捷径在有限样本下精确抵消；同一对比也可作为关系型OOD训练损失与双线性VLM评分诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系型分布外错误（图自然、句子流畅、物体常见但关系错）难以干净评测；现有图文匹配评测的负样本常在风格、模板、长度、物体频率或图像统计上不同，模型不检查配对也能"解出"题目。

Method: 构造配对循环：固定两张图像与两条描述，在两个方向上交换配对形成对比，使仅图像、仅文本及可加性分量捷径相消；由此导出关系型outlier exposure训练损失，并用于诊断双线性视觉-语言打分。实验覆盖合成控制、GQA、COCO，以及被捷径污染的COCO OE压力测试。

Result: 循环能区分粗粒度图文匹配与方向性角色绑定失败，并识别在全局特征中仍显著可见的几何尺寸关系；捷径污染设定下，验证AUROC近完美的捷径选择模型虽保留关系信号但弱于循环训练，CycleAcc由0.882提升至0.927。

Conclusion: 测试模型是否理解配对应保持部件不变、只改变配对，仅询问耦合本身；该评测原语同时可作为训练目标与诊断工具，官方代码已开源。

Abstract: Relational out-of-distribution errors are easy to describe and hard to evaluate cleanly. An image may be natural, a caption may be fluent, and all mentioned objects may be common, yet the caption may describe the wrong relation in the image. Many evaluations intended to test this behavior can be solved without checking the pairing at all, because negatives differ in caption style, template, length, object frequency, or image statistics. We propose counterfactual pairing cycles, a blocked evaluation primitive that reuses the same two images and the same two captions on both sides of the comparison, changing only which image is paired with which caption. This makes image-only, text-only, and additive component shortcuts cancel exactly in finite samples. The same contrast also gives a training loss for relational outlier exposure and a diagnostic for bilinear vision-language scores. Across synthetic controls, GQA, and COCO, cycles separate coarse image-caption matching from directional role-binding failures, while also identifying geometric size relations that remain strongly visible in global features. In a shortcut-contaminated COCO outlier-exposure stress test, a model selected by a near-perfect shortcut validation AUROC retains substantial relational signal but remains below cycle training on exact cycles; cycle training improves CycleAcc from 0.882 to 0.927. The main lesson is simple: to test whether a model understands a pairing, the benchmark should keep the parts fixed, change the pairing, and ask only about coupling. The official implementation is available online.

</details>


### [55] [LonGaussian: Tracking Foundation Priors for Long-Context Feedforward Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23250)
*Xie, Zhoufeng,Wu, Guanjun,Zhang, Shuai,Wang, Xinggang,Liu, Wenyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LonGaussian：基于预训练流式重建骨干的流式前馈框架，将长视频几何特征解码为稠密高斯，并在块内用多帧点轨迹引导学习式合并去除冗余高斯、用渲染深度一致性过滤剔除跨块错误遮挡的高斯，实现紧凑且可全局渲染的高斯场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈高斯重建方法在长序列上有两个瓶颈：联合处理所有视角导致显存激增；逐帧累积像素对齐预测会冗余表示同一表面，增加存储与渲染开销。目标是在未知相机位姿下从长视频流式重建紧凑、可全局渲染的场景，服务机器人感知与具身智能。

Method: 以预训练流式3D重建骨干为基础，解码长上下文几何特征得到稠密高斯；按chunk流式处理。块内利用多帧点轨迹（point tracks）关联同一表面的重复观测，驱动可学习的merger合并对应高斯预测；块间用渲染深度一致性过滤抑制其他块中错误遮挡本地重建表面的高斯。输入为已知内参、未知位姿的长图像序列。

Result: 在DL3DV与RealEstate10K基准上，新视角渲染质量优于评估的前馈基线，且所用高斯数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 流式前馈+轨迹引导高斯合并+深度一致性过滤可有效解决长序列重建的显存与冗余双瓶颈，得到紧凑且可全局渲染的高斯表示，是长视频前馈3D重建的实用路线。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-quality, renderable 3D scenes from long videos is essential for robotic perception and embodied intelligence. Many feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction methods rapidly predict 3D scenes from a small set of images, but extending them to longer sequences presents two bottlenecks: jointly processing all views increases GPU memory requirements, while accumulating pixel-aligned predictions redundantly represents the same surfaces and increases storage and rendering costs. We present LonGaussian, a streaming feed-forward framework that reconstructs compact, globally renderable Gaussian scenes from long image sequences with known intrinsics and unknown camera poses. Built on a pretrained streaming 3D reconstruction backbone, LonGaussian decodes long-context geometric features into dense Gaussians. Within each chunk, multi-frame point tracks link repeated observations of the same surface and guide a learned merger to consolidate the corresponding Gaussian predictions. Rendered-depth consistency filtering further suppresses Gaussians from other chunks that incorrectly occlude locally reconstructed surfaces. On DL3DV and RealEstate10K benchmarks, LonGaussian improves novel-view rendering quality while using substantially fewer Gaussians than the evaluated feed-forward baselines.

</details>


### [56] [SCALE: Improving Guidance-Free Visual Generation via Self-Contrastive Alignment for Likelihood Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23263)
*Hur, Jiwan,Lee, Dong-Jae,Lee, Dongyeun,Han, Gyojin,Kim, Junmo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自对比引导 SCG 与免引导训练框架 SCALE，用真实样本与自生成样本的预测对比作为模型内在信号替代 CFG，单次前向即可获得增强预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG 能显著提升视觉生成质量但需两次推理，采样开销大；现有免引导方法只是近似/模仿 CFG，性能上限受 CFG 本身局限约束。

Method: 以模型在真实样本与自生成样本上的预测差异构造自对比信号，将生成推向真实数据流形；通过重参数化把 SCG 融入标准似然目标，得到免引导训练框架 SCALE，推理时单次前向输出 SCG 增强预测。

Result: 在类别条件与文本到图像生成任务上，覆盖扩散模型与自回归模型，SCALE 稳定优于模仿 CFG 的免引导基线；与 DPO 结合可进一步提升。

Conclusion: 自对比信号与 CFG、偏好微调具有互补性，可在不增加采样开销的前提下超越 CFG 近似方法的性能上限。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is essential for improving sample quality in visual generation, but it incurs substantial sampling overhead. Existing guidance-free approaches halve this cost by approximating the CFG with a single model inference, but their performance is bounded by the CFG and inherits its limitations. In this paper, we propose self-contrastive guidance (SCG), which contrasts the model's prediction on real and self-generated samples to obtain a direct, model-based signal that pushes generation toward the true data manifold. Integrating SCG into the standard likelihood objective via reparameterization yields Self-Contrastive Alignment for Likelihood Estimation (SCALE), a guidance-free training framework that produces SCG-enhanced predictions in a single forward pass. Across class-conditional and text-to-image generation on diffusion and autoregressive models, SCALE consistently outperforms CFG-mimicking guidance-free baselines and improves further when combined with DPO, demonstrating that the self-contrastive signal yields gains complementary to both CFG and preference-based fine-tuning.

</details>


### [57] [Agentic Image Generation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23269)
*Li, Yifan,Qin, Bowen,Zhou, Kun,Zhao, Haochen,Liu, Zikang,Li, Zheng,Hao, Minsheng,Cheng, Yuhao,Zou, Haosheng,Chen, Jeff,Zhao, Wayne Xin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"野外图像生成"(Image Generation in the Wild)设定:面向仅数天前出现的新实体,由智能体检索网络参考图再生成。构建自动流水线(从新闻标题合成提示、保留检索结果高度混淆者)得到 Savanna 基准(500条提示,16个子类)。提出 Locate-Corroborate-Isolate 范式(先定位身份、再多源验证候选图、最后裁剪干净参考图),用15K轨迹训练 Ranger-8B,搜索得分67.2,超过前沿模型的 harness 框架,并在多个生成器上取得最佳生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体图像生成通过搜索代理从网络检索参考来扩展生成器知识边界,但真实请求常涉及几天前才出现的实体:网络视觉证据稀少、检索结果被易混淆实体淹没、目标很少单独出现。现有基准与代理范式未覆盖此"野外"设定,需要新基准与针对性的代理策略。

Method: (1) 自动化数据流水线:从近期新闻标题合成提示,仅保留真实检索结果高度混淆的样本,构建 Savanna 基准(500 prompts / 16子类);(2) 提出 Locate-Corroborate-Isolate(定位—佐证—隔离)三阶段代理范式:文本搜索锁定目标身份→多源交叉验证候选图像→裁剪出干净参考图交给生成器;(3) 在该范式下采集 15K 轨迹训练 8B 规模的 Ranger-8B。

Result: 在 Savanna 上,即便基于前沿模型的 harness 框架也仅约半数提示能检索到正确参考;Ranger-8B 搜索得分 67.2,超过前沿模型 harness;在所有测试生成器上取得最佳生成质量,并在其他图像生成基准上优于先前的智能体方法。已开源流水线、基准、训练数据与模型。

Conclusion: 针对时效性强、易混淆的"野外"检索-生成场景,结构化的定位—佐证—隔离流程配合轨迹级训练显著提升参考检索与生成质量,表明过程范式设计比单纯堆叠前沿模型更关键。

Abstract: Agentic image generation pairs an image generator with a search agent that retrieves references from the web, extending generation beyond the generator's training data. Real-world requests, however, often concern entities that appeared only days ago. We call this setting Image Generation in the Wild. In this setting, the web offers limited visual evidence, search results are crowded with easily confused entities, and the target is rarely shown on its own. To study this setting, we build an automated pipeline that synthesizes prompts from recent headlines and keeps only those whose real search results are highly confusing, yielding requests that are hard to solve yet natural to ask. With it, we construct Savanna, a benchmark of 500 prompts across 16 subcategories. On Savanna, even harness frameworks built on the frontier models retrieve a correct reference for only about half of the prompts. We then propose Locate-Corroborate-Isolate, a paradigm in which the agent first pins down the target's identity through text search, then verifies candidate images against multiple sources, and finally crops a clean reference for the generator. Trained on 15K trajectories that follow this paradigm, Ranger-8B achieves a search score of 67.2, outperforming harnesses equipped with frontier models. It also achieves the best generation quality with every generator we test, and surpasses prior agentic methods on other image generation benchmarks. We release the pipeline, benchmark, training data and model at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/Agentic-ImageGen-Wild.

</details>


### [58] [Xiaomi-Lens-0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23279)
*Fu, Pei,Liu, Yike,Sun, Yue,Wang, Hanmo,Xi, Xiuwen,Zhang, Ruoceng,Zhang, Shaojie,Jiang, Chao,Li, Hang,Liu, Bingrui,Luo, Zhenbo,Qu, Heng,Xu, Changjian,Xu, Ming,Luan, Jian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Xiaomi-Lens-0：4B 多模态模型在 Qwen3.5 基础上让语言模型直接生成区域掩码，实现"用视觉潜变量推理"及 grounded 思维链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 擅长整图问答但细粒度区域理解弱，根因是纯文本监督从不指明哪块区域支持答案，缺少区域级 grounding 信号。

Method: 冻结视觉编码器 patch 特征与语言模型 mask-token 隐状态经轻量解码器融合输出掩码，无额外分割编码器，掩码监督可回传至共享骨干；每个潜变量对应一个掩码，从而在链式推理中交错生成区域潜变量，并用 grounded 推理轨迹监督其访问区域。

Result: 在 RefCOCO/+/g 全部 8 个 split 上平均比 Qwen3.5 提升 5.6 点，分割 cIoU 为对比模型最高；全部感知 benchmark 超过 Qwen3.5。

Conclusion: 把掩码预测内嵌进 MLLM 骨干可同时提升指代理解与分割，并实现可解释的区域级视觉推理。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) answer questions about whole images well, yet they still struggle with fine-grained region understanding. A key reason is that text-only supervision never specifies which regions support an answer. To close this gap, we present Xiaomi-Lens-0, a 4B MLLM built on Qwen3.5 that predicts masks from its own visual representations. Specifically, a lightweight decoder combines vision-encoder patch features with visual latents, the language model's mask-token states, so mask supervision reaches the shared backbone without an additional segmentation encoder. Because each latent decodes to a mask, the model can interleave reasoning with region-specific latents and thus think with visual latents, while grounded reasoning traces supervise the regions it visits during chain-of-thought. As a result, across all eight RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg splits, Xiaomi-Lens-0 gains 5.6 points on average over Qwen3.5 in referring comprehension and achieves the highest segmentation cIoU among compared models. Moreover, it surpasses Qwen3.5 across all perception benchmarks. Homepage: \href{https://seerray-lab.github.io/Xiaomi-Lens-0}{this https URL}

</details>


### [59] [I2V-DETACH: Source Grounding Detachment for Unauthorized Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23287)
*Lee, Chanhui,Song, Yeonghwan,Kang, Yewon,Son, Jeany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: I2V-DETACH：将图生视频防护重构为"削弱源内容条件传播"，通过抑制源-视频对齐、增强视频内部交互，实现源接地解耦的图像免疫，且具黑盒迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型使图像条件视频生成易被未授权滥用；现有 I2V 免疫方法依赖破坏视觉质量或时间一致性的"降质"策略，副作用大且不符合防护本质。

Method: 提出 I2V-DETACH，将免疫目标改为降低源内容保持度。两条互补扰动：(1) 抑制源图—视频隐变量的 grounding，减少源线索传播；(2) 增强视频内部(token 间)交互，使去噪偏向视频侧动态而非源图。为使优化信号有信息量，用"源导向但偏离源保持轨迹"的状态初始化辅助视频隐变量，使其仍处于源条件生成域内。

Result: 在多种扩散架构上实验，源内容保持度显著下降，优于既有免疫方法，并展现较强的黑盒迁移性。

Conclusion: I2V 防护应从"输出降质"转向"切断源接地"，通过注意力层面的双向干预可在保持输出自然性的同时有效阻抗未授权生成。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models have advanced image-conditioned video generation, but their accessibility raises risks of unauthorized manipulation. Image immunization has emerged as a promising defense for Image-to-Video (I2V) protection, yet existing methods often rely on degrading visual quality or temporal coherence. In this work, we revisit I2V immunization as reducing source-content preservation, shifting the focus from output degradation to weakening source-conditioned content propagation. To this end, we propose I2V-DETACH, an image immunization method that prevents unauthorized I2V generation through source grounding detachment. I2V models preserve source content by propagating source-image cues into video latents during denoising. Accordingly, I2V-DETACH disrupts this process in two complementary ways: it suppresses source-video grounding to reduce source cue propagation, and strengthens intra-video interactions to shift denoising toward video-side dynamics with reduced reliance on the source image. To obtain informative optimization signals, we initialize auxiliary video latents with source-oriented states that remain within the source-conditioned generation regime while deviating from source-preserving trajectories. Extensive experiments across diverse diffusion architectures show that I2V-DETACH substantially reduces source-content preservation, improves over prior immunization methods, and exhibits strong black-box transferability.

</details>


### [60] [FlashReFocus: Interactive Image Refocusing in Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23297)
*Cheng, Ching-Heng,Lee, Chia-Ming,Chang, Ming-Ching,Li, Xin,Liu, Yu-Lun,Hsu, Chih-Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashReFocus：单图重对焦只在拍摄后跑一次扩散模型恢复全清晰图像，后续每次编辑用轻量渲染器（显式散焦图条件+defocus-bias attention）快速生成模糊，比 GenRefocus 快 148.3x，单次编辑约 0.5s（462x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式重对焦方法每次编辑都重跑大扩散模型，交互延迟高。作者观察到问题可分拆：全清晰恢复是病态的、需生成先验；而模糊渲染主要由深度与焦平面决定，可用轻量网络完成。

Method: 两步解耦：1）单步扩散去模糊器+解码器精修器，一次恢复全清晰图像（兼顾保真与感知质量）；2）轻量渲染器以显式散焦图为条件，采用 defocus-bias attention 使相似散焦像素共享信息、避免跨深度干扰，保证对焦区域不变；编辑散焦图即可实现移轴与双焦平面。另构建三相机基准 3CReal（89 对）。

Result: 在 EBB! 与 3CReal 上零样本散景渲染取得最佳感知保真度；首次重对焦较 GenRefocus 快 148.3x，后续每次编辑约 0.5 秒、快 462x。

Conclusion: 将病态恢复与可控渲染分离并复用单次扩散推理，可显著提升交互式重对焦效率且不损感知质量，并具备跨相机泛化能力。

Abstract: Single-image refocusing changes the focal plane and depth of field of a photograph after capture. It is inherently interactive: users try many focal points and blur strengths on one image, so each edit should be fast and change only the blur. Recent generative methods, however, rerun large diffusion models for every edit. We observe that the two sub-problems differ in kind: recovering the all-in-focus image is ill-posed and needs a generative prior, whereas rendering blur is largely determined by depth and the focal plane. We therefore propose FlashReFocus, which runs diffusion once per photograph and renders every edit with a lightweight network. To address the ill-posed restoration, a one-step diffusion deblurrer with a decoder refiner recovers fine detail efficiently while balancing fidelity and perceptual quality. For precise control, the renderer is conditioned on an explicit defocus map and uses defocus-bias attention, which lets pixels at similar defocus share information without interference from pixels at other depths; in-focus content stays unchanged, and editing the map yields tilt-shift and two focal planes. To further test generalization to unseen cameras, we collect 3CReal, a three-camera benchmark of 89 pairs. FlashReFocus achieves the best perceptual fidelity for zero-shot bokeh rendering on EBB! and 3CReal, refocuses 148.3x faster than GenRefocus, and takes about half a second per further edit, 462x faster.

</details>


### [61] [Look, Reason, Draw a Region: Recursive Refinement for Spatial Context Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23302)
*Tahir, Wajeeha,Tegon, Anna,Kesting, Lionnus,İslamoğlu, Gamze,Karacs, Kristóf,Benini, Luca,Ingolfsson, Thorir Mar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spatial-TRM：冻结VLM特征+凸区域解码器+权重共享递归推理（仅11.2M可训练参数），在RoboSpatial-Home上空间上下文预测准确率达29.51%，较同参数量单步模型提升9.02个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间关系与物理约束对大型VLM仍是难点；递归推理在离散/抽象任务上表现强，作者希望借此提升自然图像中的空间上下文预测（识别相对物体的空闲可放置区域）。

Method: 保持视觉-语言编码器冻结，接一个凸区域解码器；核心是一个权重共享（weight-tied）的递归推理模块，迭代精炼预测，支持6种空间关系、3种参考坐标系与开放词表锚定物体；总参数877.8M中仅11.2M可训练。

Result: RoboSpatial-Home上上下文准确率29.51%，相对同参数量的单独训练单步模型提升9.02pp（相对提升44.0%）；总参数约为RoboPoint（13B）的1/15。绝对精度仍较低，且仅在单一benchmark上验证。

Conclusion: 在固定可训练参数预算下，对预训练VLM特征做重复精炼可改善空间上下文预测；但证据限于离线基准，缺乏真实机器人闭环验证与消融细节。

Abstract: Spatial context prediction requires visual grounding and spatial reasoning to identify vacant regions relative to objects in a scene. Spatial relationships and physical constraints remain challenging for large vision-language models (VLMs). Recursive reasoning has demonstrated strong performance on discrete and abstract reasoning tasks, motivating its use for improving spatial context prediction in natural images. Weight sharing enables higher accuracy with a small trainable parameter count. We introduce Spatial-TRM, a compact recursive model with frozen vision-language features, a convex region decoder, and just 11.2M trainable parameters. This weight-tied reasoner supports six spatial relationships, three reference frames, and open-vocabulary anchor objects. On the RoboSpatial-Home benchmark, Spatial-TRM achieves 29.51% context accuracy, a gain of 9.02 percentage points (44.0% relative) over a separately trained single-step model with the same parameter count. Including the frozen encoders, the model has 877.8M total parameters, approximately $15\times$ fewer than the reported 13B size of RoboPoint. These results show that repeated refinement improves spatial context prediction from pretrained vision-language features at a fixed trainable parameter count on this benchmark.

</details>


### [62] [CatchUp: Depth-Aware Re-Admission of Visual Tokens for Efficient Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23324)
*Park, Eunju,Hwang, Uiwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CatchUp: 免训练、深度感知的视觉token可重入路由框架，通过参考-当前转移信号近似被跳过token的表示演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM逐层处理数百视觉token开销大；现有推理时剪枝不可逆地丢弃低分token，而高查询条件注意力的token集合随解码深度变化；可恢复路由虽保留token，但重入时使用过时表示。

Method: 发现用全token参考轨迹的当前深度oracle状态替换陈旧重入状态可显著提升grounding；CatchUp在每个解码区间重新评估查询条件视觉相关性，仅处理被选中的token，推迟其余token；重入时用语义与空间相关的视觉token的reference-to-current转移信号近似其缺失的表示演化。无需训练。

Result: 3个指代表达式grounding数据集与6个多模态benchmark上，在64/128/192 token预算下两组平均相对保留率最高；64-token预算下活跃视觉token减少88.9%，六项benchmark平均保留全模型92.17%性能，端到端延迟降低8.0%。

Conclusion: 深度感知的可重入token路由优于不可逆剪枝与陈旧重入，是LVLM高效推理的有效免训练方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) repeatedly process hundreds of visual tokens across decoder layers, incurring substantial computation and memory costs. Existing inference-time visual token pruning methods often irreversibly remove low-ranked tokens, although the set of visual tokens receiving high query-conditioned attention can change across decoder depth. Recoverable routing preserves skipped tokens for later reconsideration, but re-admits them with stale representations after bypassing intermediate transformations. We further find that replacing stale re-admitted states with their current-depth oracle states from a full-token reference trajectory substantially improves grounding performance. We therefore propose CatchUp, a training-free framework for depth-aware visual-token re-admission. CatchUp repeatedly re-evaluates query-conditioned visual relevance, processes only selected visual tokens through each decoder interval, and preserves deferred tokens for later re-admission. Upon re-admission, CatchUp approximates missed representation evolution using reference-to-current transition signals from semantically and spatially related visual tokens. Across three referring-expression grounding datasets and six multimodal benchmarks, CatchUp achieves the highest average relative retention in both benchmark groups at 64-, 128-, and 192-token budgets. At a 64-token budget with 88.9% fewer active visual tokens, CatchUp retains 92.17% of the full model's performance on average across six multimodal benchmarks while reducing end-to-end latency by 8.0%. Code is available at https://github.com/pej0918/CatchUp

</details>


### [63] [STC-MGNet: Spatial-Temporal-Channel Attention-Enhanced Multimodal Graph Network for Pedestrian Intention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23356)
*Li, Yuanzhe,Wang, Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STC-MGNet：时空-通道注意力增强的多模态图网络，用于行人过街意图预测，融合姿态图、视觉与自车运动信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中行人-车辆交互安全依赖于准确的过街意图预测，但姿态序列中的细微时空模式难以捕捉，且视觉与自车运动等互补模态信息难以有效整合。

Method: 用时空-通道(STC)注意力增强的姿态图编码器提取判别性表征：三条并行注意力分支分别关注骨骼关节、观测帧与特征通道，输出以可学习权重融合；并引入多阶段跨模态通道调制机制，在图编码各阶段通过模态专属通道门控自适应精修姿态表征，以利用视觉与自车运动信息。

Result: 在JAADbeh、JAADall、PIE上准确率分别为70%、87%、88%，优于多个代表性基线；消融实验显示视觉与自车运动信息对预测性能有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 注意力增强的多模态图网络能有效提升行人过街意图预测性能，但方法偏增量式模态融合，且仅在公开数据集上验证，缺乏实车部署证据。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of pedestrian crossing intentions plays a critical role in enhancing the safety of pedestrian-vehicle interactions in autonomous driving. However, effectively capturing subtle spatiotemporal patterns in pedestrian pose sequences and integrating complementary visual and ego-vehicle motion information remain challenging. This paper proposes a spatial-temporal-channel attention-enhanced multimodal graph network (STC-MGNet) for pedestrian crossing intention prediction. STC-MGNet introduces a spatial-temporal-channel (STC) attention-enhanced pose graph encoder to learn discriminative representations from pedestrian pose sequences. Three parallel attention branches selectively emphasize informative skeletal joints, observation frames, and feature channels, with their outputs integrated through learnable fusion weights. To leverage complementary visual and ego-vehicle motion information, a multi-stage cross-modal channel modulation mechanism is introduced to adaptively refine pose representations through modality-specific channel gating at successive graph encoding stages. Experimental results demonstrate that STC-MGNet achieves accuracy rates of 70%, 87%, and 88% on the JAADbeh, JAADall, and PIE datasets, respectively, outperforming several representative baseline methods. Ablation experiments further highlight the complementary contributions of visual and ego-vehicle motion information to prediction performance.

</details>


### [64] [UniPolar: Joint Image and Polarimetric Restoration under Compound Degradations via Physical Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23360)
*Jin, Yang,Guo, Guangyu,Shen, Linghao,Wang, Binglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniPolar：面向复合退化CPFA观测的“图像-斯托克斯”耦合框架，双流联合恢复四个偏振角图像并直接预测斯托克斯差分，配合双域频率交互、退化条件调制与物理一致性学习，实现视觉保真与偏振可靠性同时提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏振图像复原方法通常只单独处理器件退化或环境退化，但CPFA成像中两者常共存且耦合，共同扰动强度与跨角度偏振差分，导致仅提升图像保真度并不能保证Stokes、DoLP、AoLP的准确恢复。

Method: 提出UniPolar耦合框架：两条互补支路分别重建四幅偏振角图像并直接预测Stokes差分分量；用退化条件跨域交互（Dual Domain Frequency Interaction做频率选择性证据交换 + Degradation Conditioned Modulation做条件自适应特征校正）协调两支路；输出端以Image-Stokes Physical Consistency Learning对齐图像导出与直接预测的Stokes分量，并正则化角度保真度及DoLP物理可行性。

Result: 在复合退化基准上，平均图像PSNR 31.522 dB、DoLP PSNR 24.097 dB、AoLP平均误差16.833°，同时兼顾视觉保真与偏振可靠性。

Conclusion: 复合退化下必须联合建模图像保真与偏振物理量可靠性；该工作为基于学习的偏振复原提供了物理约束视角，但属偏振成像低层复原，与机器人/3D重建核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: Polarimetric image restoration aims to recover polarization angle images and reliable polarimetric quantities from degraded observations. Beyond visual fidelity, it also requires preserving the physical relationships underlying reliable polarimetric estimation. However, existing methods typically address either device- or environment-induced degradations in isolation, limiting their applicability to color polarization filter array (CPFA) imaging, where both often coexist. Such compound degradations jointly perturb image intensities and cross angle polarization differences. Consequently, improved image fidelity alone does not necessarily ensure accurate Stokes, DoLP, and AoLP recovery. To address this challenge, we propose UniPolar, a coupled Image Stokes framework for joint image polarimetric restoration from compound-degraded CPFA observations. Specifically, UniPolar employs two complementary streams to reconstruct four polarization-angle images and directly predict Stokes difference components. To coordinate these streams, Degradation Conditioned Cross Domain Interaction combines Dual Domain Frequency Interaction for frequency selective evidence exchange with Degradation Conditioned Modulation for condition adaptive feature correction. Furthermore, at the output level, Image Stokes Physical Consistency Learning aligns image derived and directly predicted Stokes components while regularizing angular fidelity and the physical feasibility of DoLP. On the compound-degradation benchmark, UniPolar achieves average image and DoLP PSNR of 31.522 dB and 24.097 dB, respectively, with an average AoLP error of 16.833 degree. These results underscore the importance of jointly considering visual fidelity and polarimetric reliability, offering a physically grounded perspective on learning based restoration under compound degradations.

</details>


### [65] [MOSS-Image Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23374)
*AI, MOSI.*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSS-Image：从零预训练 5B 单流 DiT，结合意图感知的 Agent 式提示增强模块，实现中英长文本渲染与精确布局控制的开放权重文本生成图像系统，并配套 LLTB 与 RealLayoutArena 两个评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源图像生成系统已能生成信息密集的图形，而开放权重文生图模型在密集文字与复杂布局上仍显不足，作者希望以开源方案缩小差距。

Method: 1) 以精心设计的配方从零预训练 5B 单流 DiT，支持双语长文本渲染与布局控制；2) 构建意图感知的 Agent 式提示增强（PE）模块，将用户请求转译为细化的内容与布局规格；3) 提出 LongLongTextBench（中英长文本渲染）与 RealLayoutArena（端到端富布局生成）评测框架。

Result: 在 LLTB 上取得开放权重模型中的 SOTA，平均文本覆盖率达 96.3%；在 RLA 端到端系统对比中排名第二，而其骨干在使用统一增强提示的生成器对比中排名第一。

Conclusion: MOSS-Image 通过大尺度开源 DiT 训练与提示侧 Agent 增强的组合，在长文本渲染与富布局生成上达到开放权重领先水平，并承诺开源模型、训练配方与评测框架。

Abstract: Recent closed-source image generation systems can produce diverse visual content from simple compositions to information-dense graphics, yet open-weight text-to-image models still struggle with dense text and intricate layouts. To narrow this gap, we introduce MOSS-Image, an open generation system that combines high-fidelity visual synthesis with intent-aware composition. First, we pretrain a 5B single-stream diffusion transformer (DiT) from scratch with a carefully designed recipe, enabling bilingual long-form text rendering and precise layout control. Second, we develop an intention-aware agentic prompt enhancement (PE) module that translates diverse user requests into detailed content and layout specifications. Together, they enable MOSS-Image to generate text-rich and layout-coherent images. We further introduce LongLongTextBench (LLTB) for evaluating long-form English and Chinese text rendering, and RealLayoutArena (RLA) for end-to-end layout-rich generation. MOSS-Image achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-weight models on LLTB, with an average text coverage of 96.3%. On RLA, MOSS-Image ranks second in the end-to-end system comparison, while its backbone ranks first among generators evaluated with shared enhanced prompts. We will release the model, training recipe, and evaluation framework to support further research and promote the text-to-image community.

</details>


### [66] [FloodVLM-Pix: Language-Guided Pixel-Level Flood Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23377)
*Zhang, Chengyang,Wang, Zhihao,Long, Zhiyu,Wang, Xueqian,Yao, Quanming,Wu, Wei,Li, Gang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建首个大规模遥感洪水多模态基准（131,200图块/443,100文本），并提出语言引导的像素级洪水理解模型FloodVLM-Pix，含洪水先验模块与多时相像素解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在洪水任务上存在注意力偏置与洪水量级描述不准的问题，且缺少支持语言级理解与像素级分割的大规模基准。

Method: 1) 构建洪水多模态基准，覆盖洪水严重程度与演化的语言标注及像素级分割标签；2) FloodVLM-Pix中洪水先验模块自动蒸馏子区域洪水空间先验并注入模型，缓解语言理解中的注意力偏置；3) 多时相洪水像素解码器引入通用-专用特征融合机制，抑制云、山影等干扰导致的性能退化。

Result: 在FloodVL-Pix上与先进VLM及洪水分割模型对比，洪水描述质量与像素级分割性能均有提升。

Conclusion: 该工作以大规模基准+语言引导的像素级理解框架推进遥感洪水理解，代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: As climate change intensifies, floods are becoming more frequent and destructive, making accurate flood understanding an urgent priority. However, existing vision-language models perform poorly on flood-related tasks, suffering from attentional bias and inaccurate descriptions of flood magnitude. To bridge this gap, we construct the first large-scale remote sensing flood multi-modal benchmark that supports language-level flood understanding and pixel-level flood segmentation. The benchmark contains 131,200 flood image patches and corresponding 443,100 textual descriptions for flood severity and evolution. Based on this benchmark, we propose FloodVLM-Pix, a language-guided pixel-level flood understanding model that includes a flood prior module and a multi-temporal flood pixel decoder to achieve accurate flood understanding. The flood prior module automatically distills sub-region flood spatial priors and inputs priors into FloodVLM-Pix, solving the attentional bias problem in flood language understanding. The flood pixel decoder introduces a novel general-specific feature fusion mechanism to address performance degradation caused by interference (e.g., clouds or mountain shadows) during flood segmentation. Experiments on FloodVL-Pix demonstrate that FloodVLM-Pix improves both flood description quality and pixel-level flood segmentation performance compared with state-of-the-art VLMs and flood segmentation models. Our code and data will be released at https://github.com/Haomiaoyanmeng/FloodPix.

</details>


### [67] [LithoWorld: An Endpoint World Model for Lithography Process-Window Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23408)
*Zhao, Botong,Yu, Fang,Chen, Xinyuan,Lu, Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LithoWorld：以“端点世界模型”从SEM图像特征出发，用因果预测分支对齐合格参考表示、条件流策略输出二维光刻参数修正（action chunk），实现工艺漂移的闭环参数恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光刻工艺漂移会使图案超出工艺窗口，导致SEM形貌不合格；需要一种能从SEM形态反推并恢复合格参数的模型化方法，而非依赖精确参考坐标匹配。

Method: 构建端点世界模型：内部代表性目标同时定义合格参考外观与监督信号；因果预测分支将当前图像特征对齐端点表示；条件流匹配策略生成二维校正动作块。训练数据为173组实测SEM按聚焦-曝光矩阵组织，未测单元保持为未知。

Result: 在73组子集的200个测试对上，合格区域成功率88.5%、内部区域成功率46.0%，分别比最强基线高2.5与6.0个百分点；消融实验分离了目标选择、端点对齐与输出参数化的贡献，定性SEM检索显示无需精确坐标匹配即可恢复至合格内部形貌。

Conclusion: 将世界模型/流策略范式引入半导体工艺参数恢复是可行且有效的，端点表示与动作块生成是关键设计；但内部区域成功率仍偏低，泛化性与实际良率收益待验证。

Abstract: Lithography process drift can move a fabricated pattern outside its process window, producing unqualified scanning electron microscopy (SEM) morphology. We present LithoWorld, an endpoint world model for SEM-guided parameter recovery. Interior representative targets jointly determine qualified reference appearance and correction supervision. A causal predictive branch aligns currentimage features with endpoint representations, while a conditional flow policy generates a two-dimensional correction as an action chunk. The model learns from 173 groups of measured SEMs organized as focus-exposure maps, preserving unmeasured cells as unknown. On 200 test pairs from the 73-group subset, LithoWorld achieves 88.5% qualified-region success and 46.0% interior success, exceeding the strongest comparison method by 2.5 and 6.0 percentage points, respectively. Ablations separate the effects of target selection, endpoint alignment, and output parameterization.
