Qualitative SEM retrieval illustrates recovery to qualified interiors without requiring exact reference-coordinate matching.

</details>


### [68] [Can We Use Saliency Maps for the Evaluation of Generative Image Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23411)
*Ristori, Eleonora,Bindini, Luca,Lippi, Marco,Frasconi, Paolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 训练针对特定生成器的真假分类器，用显著性图分析其判据，发现分类器学的是各生成器特有的伪影，并据此构造与人类感知一致的复合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成图像评估指标依赖为其他任务预训练的固定特征提取器，与人类真实性感知在不同数据集和模型族上并不一致。

Method: 为每个待评估生成器单独训练真假分类器（不用大规模预训练骨干），仅用该模型的真实/生成样本；再用saliency map分析分类器决策依据，结合分类准确率与显著性信息得到复合评分。

Result: 分类器学到的不是通用"虚假性"，而是生成器特有伪影，跨生成器迁移性差、准确率高；显著性图能定位各模型输出的典型视觉线索与结构不规则。

Conclusion: 该框架无需预训练骨干，只用被评估模型自身的样本即可获得与人类判断对齐的评估分数，并具备可解释性。

Abstract: Evaluating generative image models remains challenging, as commonly used metrics depend on fixed pretrained feature extractors optimized for different tasks that do not always align with human realism perception across datasets and model families. To address this limitation, we investigate a human-inspired evaluation framework that avoids representations learned for unrelated tasks. Specifically, we train a generator-specific classifier to distinguish real images from samples produced by a given model and analyze its decisions using saliency maps. Across diverse benchmarks, we observe that these classifiers learn generator-specific artifacts rather than a universal notion of "fakeness", exhibiting strong accuracy and limited cross-generator transfer. This makes saliency analysis particularly informative: the resulting attribution maps highlight visual cues and structural irregularities characteristic of each model's outputs. By combining classification accuracy and saliency information, we obtain a composite score aligned with human judgments, without relying on large pretrained backbones and using only real and generated samples from the evaluated model.

</details>


### [69] [GeoReason: Bridging Logical Reasoning and Spatial Fidelity in Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23416)
*Fan, Cheng,Wu, Han,Luo, Tingzhang,Dong, Minjing,Tang, Yehui,Guo, Jianyuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoReason针对遥感推理分割中[SEG] token在自回归长链推理中与视觉证据逐步"疏离"导致的空间保真退化问题，提出"预期先验"(将[SEG]前置进用户查询作为持续空间锚)、位置感知CoT(框级粗到细定位)与潜空间Patch-to-Token对齐损失(PTA)，在Earthreason、RRSIS-D上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Embedding-as-Mask"范式下MLLM的指令驱动感知已较成熟，但推广到遥感推理分割时，杂乱地物背景与极端尺度变化造成空间保真退化与目标误识别。作者指出的关键瓶颈是：[SEG] token仅在长推理链末端生成，依赖的视觉证据已被语言抽象和自回归噪声逐级稀释；对像素足迹极小的遥感目标，这种信息衰减直接导致定位失败。

Method: 1) Anticipatory Prior：把[SEG] token预先注入初始用户查询，从"终端输出"变为"首要条件"，作为贯穿推理过程的持久空间锚；2) Location-aware CoT：用bounding box强制粗到细的定位推理链；3) Latent Patch-to-Token Alignment (PTA) Loss：显式对齐潜空间分割token与真实遥感纹理特征。

Result: 在Earthreason与RRSIS-D两个遥感推理分割基准上一致优于现有SOTA，作者称空间保真与目标辨识均得到改善；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作把"分割token时序疏离"定位为MLLM推理分割的结构性瓶颈，并通过token前置化、定位CoT与潜空间特征对齐三项轻量改造缓解信息衰减；思路可迁移到其他需要跨长推理链保持视觉锚点的指令驱动感知任务，但验证仍限于遥感领域基准。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced instruction-driven perception through the "Embedding-as-Mask" paradigm. However, extending this capability to Remote Sensing (RS) reasoning segmentation remains exceptionally challenging under cluttered geographical contexts and extreme scale variations, often leading to Spatial Fidelity Degradation and Target Misidentification. We identify a critical yet underexplored bottleneck: the sequential alienation of the segmentation token [SEG] from its initial visual grounding within the autoregressive generation paradigm. Produced only at the end of long reasoning chains, [SEG] relies on visual evidence that has been progressively diluted by linguistic abstraction and autoregressive noise. This information decay is particularly catastrophic for RS targets with minuscule pixel footprints, where even marginal fidelity loss leads to localization failure. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GeoReason, a novel framework designed to fortify logical deduction and preserve spatial integrity. First, we introduce an Anticipatory Prior mechanism that injects the [SEG] token directly into the initial user query, shifting it from a terminal output to a primary condition. This paradigm shift transforms the token into a persistent spatial anchor, preventing information decay during long-form linguistic inference. Second, we enhance visual reasoning via a Location-aware Chain-of-Thought (CoT) that enforces coarse-to-fine localization using bounding boxes, coupled with a Latent Patch-to-Token Alignment (PTA) Loss that explicitly aligns the latent segmentation token with physical RS textures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoReason consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the Earthreason and RRSIS-D benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Fanc0126/GeoReason.

</details>


### [70] [Momentum: Persistent Dynamics for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23423)
*Savov, Nedko,Ailuro, Stefan Maria,Paudel, Danda Pani,Du, Yilun,Wang, Xi,Van Gool, Luc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Momentum 两阶段视频世界模型：用多相机参考机位组（8 相机）构建图结构，WorldUpdate 联合生成参考相机视频作为场景动态共享记忆，WorldInteract 在该记忆上做相机可控视频生成，使离视内容（车辆、行人）出画后仍被表示并可一致复现；配套 CARLA 同步数据采集与一致性评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型用观测历史、静态点云或图像缓存来维持视锥外内容，代价高且无法更新视锥外的动态场景内容，导致物体离开视野后被遗忘或复现时物理状态错误。需要一种可持续演进、覆盖 360° 周边的场景表示。

Method: 以 8 相机参考 rig 同步视频为场景表示，将多个 rig 建成图并在相邻 rig 间传播视频生成以协调跨位置、跨时间的动态；两阶段流水线：WorldUpdate 联合生成参考相机视频作为动态“共享记忆”，WorldInteract 以该记忆为条件（grounding）做相机可控生成。构建 CARLA 同步采集管线（参考 rig + 移动目标相机）及评价框架，度量世界状态内部一致性与目标观测一致性。

Result: 在生成街景中，相比静态图像条件，未见场景下动态场景一致性提升 126.2%，同时视觉保真度提升；模型与代码将开源。

Conclusion: 用多视角参考相机图作为可更新的动态场景记忆，比静态点云/图像缓存更有效地维持离视动态内容一致性，为可导航、长时一致的交互式视频世界模型提供了可行路线。

Abstract: Video world models must capture how a scene evolves beyond the current camera view to generate consistent observations when its content reappears. Existing approaches rely on observation history, static point clouds, or image caches constructed from past frames. These representations are often expensive and, more importantly, cannot update dynamic scene content outside the field of view: generated vehicles and pedestrians may be forgotten after leaving the field of view or reappear in physically inaccurate states. We represent an evolving scene through synchronized videos from reference-camera rigs, each comprising eight cameras that jointly cover the surrounding scene. We connect these rigs in a graph and propagate video generation between neighboring rigs to coordinate scene dynamics across locations and time. We introduce Momentum, a two-stage pipeline: WorldUpdate jointly generates reference-camera videos as a shared memory of scene dynamics, and WorldInteract grounds camera-controlled video generation in this evolving memory, allowing moving content to remain represented outside the target view and reappear consistently. To support training and evaluation, we develop a CARLA pipeline that captures synchronized videos from reference camera rigs and a moving target camera, together with an evaluation framework measuring consistency within the generated world state and the target observations.Experiments on generated street scenes show that grounding target videos in scene dynamics generated by Momentum improves dynamic scene consistency by 126.2% in unseen scenes over static image conditioning, while also improving visual fidelity. Our models and source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [ShadowCast: On-the-Fly Context-Aware Shadow Augmentation for Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23440)
*Kim, Yechan,Yoon, Dongho,Joo, Jongmin,Park, JongHyun,Jeon, Moongu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShadowCast 提出在训练阶段主动合成并注入阴影的数据增强方法，用于遥感语义分割：从分割标签提取建筑/树木等高于地面的物体掩膜，通过探测其周围亮度估计图像特有的投影方向，再沿该方向投影合成阴影，无需额外网络或采集元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高处物体投射的阴影会改变地物光谱表现并模糊目标边界，降低遥感影像语义分割性能。现有方法多将阴影视为需检测和去除的退化，依赖专用网络与阴影专属标注，成本较高。作者希望以"反向思路"——不消除阴影，而是合成阴影作为训练增强——低成本提升分割模型的阴影鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 直接从语义分割标签中提取建筑、树木等高出地面物体的掩膜；2) 通过探测这些物体周围的亮度分布，估计该图像特有的投影方向线索；3) 沿推断方向对掩膜做几何投影生成合成阴影，并注入到训练影像中。整个流程为无学习参数、无需网络训练，也不需要采集元数据（如太阳方位角、时间、DEM）。在 Vaihingen、Potsdam、LoveDA 三个基准上，跨多种 SOTA 分割架构验证其相对基线的增益，并与标准增强/正则化手段组合测试互补性。

Result: 在 Vaihingen、Potsdam、LoveDA 三个广泛使用的遥感基准上，ShadowCast 在多种 SOTA 分割架构上一致优于基线；与标准数据增强和正则化技术结合时可带来额外性能提升，表明随机阴影注入是一种简单且互补的增强策略。

Conclusion: 将阴影从"待去除的退化"重构为"可控的训练增强信号"是可行且有效的：利用分割标签与亮度线索即可零成本合成物理合理的投影阴影，显著提升遥感分割的阴影鲁棒性与跨架构泛化性。

Abstract: Shadows cast by elevated objects alter the spectral appearance and obscure the boundaries of objects in remote sensing images, thereby degrading semantic segmentation performance. Most existing approaches handle shadows as degradations to be detected and removed, requiring additional costs from dedicated networks and shadow-specific annotations. We propose ShadowCast, which takes the opposite approach: it synthesizes shadows and injects them during training for remote sensing segmentation. It extracts masks of elevated objects, such as buildings and trees, directly from the segmentation labels and estimates an image-specific cast-shadow direction cue by probing the brightness around them. It then projects synthetic shadows along the inferred cast-shadow direction, requiring no learned network and no acquisition metadata. Experiments on three widely used remote sensing benchmarks, Vaihingen, Potsdam, and LoveDA, show that ShadowCast consistently outperforms the baseline across diverse state-of-the-art segmentation architectures. Moreover, it brings additional performance gains when combined with standard augmentation and regularization techniques, indicating that stochastic shadow injection serves as a simple and complementary augmentation strategy for remote sensing segmentation.

</details>


### [72] [TriINR: Unsupervised Atmospheric Turbulence Mitigation with Triple Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23454)
*Ogino, Yuka,Otsuka, Rikuto,Shoji, Yuho,Ogawa, Takuya,Toizumi, Takahiro,Ito, Atsushi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriINR: 用三个隐式神经表示（INR）分别建模清晰图像、tilt 与 blur，并将 tilt/blur 建模为时空 INR，从而摆脱逐帧参数、实现帧数无关的湍流退化表示与内存高效优化，并引入湍流物理与统计先验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气湍流使远距离成像退化，退化由时空变化的 tilt（偏移）与 blur（模糊）两部分构成。现有无监督湍流缓解方法虽用隐式神经表示（INR）表达清晰图像，但 tilt 与 blur 的表示仍是逐帧的，优化参数量随输入帧数增长，难以扩展到多帧、长序列，且内存开销大。

Method: 提出 TriINR 无监督框架，从多帧退化图像恢复清晰图像。用三个 INR 分别表示清晰图像、tilt 场与 blur 场；将 tilt 和 blur 建模为时空 INR（以空间+时间坐标为输入），使退化表示的参数量与帧数无关；并把大气湍流的物理/统计特性（如 tilt 的统计分布、blur 的物理约束）作为 INR 的先验约束项加入优化目标。

Result: 在三种退化强度的合成湍流数据上，TriINR 在不同湍流强度下均比已有湍流缓解方法恢复得更稳定，在所比较的无监督方法中精度最高；在包含额外非理想运动的真实远距离场景上也表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将 tilt 与 blur 统一建模为时空 INR，可有效消除逐帧参数膨胀问题，实现无监督、内存友好的多帧湍流缓解；结合湍流物理统计先验能进一步提升复原精度，并在真实场景中具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Atmospheric turbulence degrades image quality in long-range imaging. The degradation is generally modeled with two spatio-temporally varying components, tilt and blur. Recent unsupervised turbulence mitigation methods have demonstrated the effectiveness of representing a sharp image as an implicit neural representation (INR). However, their tilt and blur representations remain frame-dependent, and the number of optimization parameters grows with the number of input frames. To address this limitation, we propose TriINR, an unsupervised turbulence mitigation method that recovers a sharp image from multiple degraded frames. TriINR represents the sharp image, tilt, and blur as three implicit neural representations. By modeling tilt and blur as spatio-temporal INRs, TriINR achieves frame-count-independent degradation representation and memory-efficient optimization. We further incorporate physical and statistical properties of atmospheric turbulence as constraints on the INRs to improve restoration accuracy. Experiments on synthesized turbulence data with three degradation strengths show that TriINR restores images more robustly across varying turbulence strengths than prior TM methods and achieves the highest accuracy among the evaluated unsupervised methods. Our method also demonstrates robustness on real long-range scenes containing additional non-ideal motion.

</details>


### [73] [When Evidence Vanishes: Distilling Visual-Evidence Dependency in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23469)
*Lan, Qizhen,Ma, Yiling,Chen, Ruiqi,Fan, Mengchen,Xiao, Xi,Zhao, Lin,He, Liang,Geng, Baocheng,Che, Pulin,Wang, Tianyang,Jiang, Xiaoqian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出证据条件依赖蒸馏（ECDD），配对原图与证据缺失的控制图，蒸馏教师答案margin的下降量，使医疗VLM的答案支持真正依赖视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑医疗VLM常靠问题措辞或先验答题，图像可有可无；标准KD只复制教师对原图的答案偏好，未约束该偏好是否条件于问题所需的视觉证据。

Method: 以"医疗视觉证据依赖"为蒸馏新目标：每个训练图像配一张证据水平匹配的控制图（区域遮挡/器械移除/全图内容替换），蒸馏教师在这对样本上的margin下降；采用下界目标仅惩罚不足（正依赖转移），并保留原图监督以维持准确率；统一适配查询区域、手术器械、全图三类证据。

Result: 三个医疗VQA设置下原图准确率与KD/对齐基线持平（DME主目标上差0.11点），但正例margin对目标遮挡（相对随机遮挡）的响应从KD的0.786升至2.023，答案支持对证据移除显著更敏感。

Conclusion: 医疗VLM蒸馏应同时转移教师的答案偏好与其对视觉证据的依赖性；依赖性是可行的可蒸馏目标。

Abstract: Compact medical vision-language models (VLMs) can answer clinical questions correctly without reliably using the image, relying instead on question wording or learned priors. Standard knowledge distillation copies a teacher's answer preference on the original image, but it leaves unchecked whether that preference is conditional on the visual evidence required by the question. We study this missing target as medical visual evidence dependency: support for the correct answer should decrease when the matched evidence is absent. We introduce Evidence-Conditioned Dependency Distillation (ECDD), which pairs each training image with a control matched to the evidence level and distills the teacher's drop in answer margin across the pair. A lower bound objective transfers positive dependency by penalizing only shortfalls, while original-image supervision preserves task accuracy. The same formulation applies when the evidence is a queried clinical region, a surgical instrument, or whole-image visual content. Across three medical VQA settings, ECDD keeps original-image accuracy competitive with distillation and alignment baselines while making answer support more sensitive to evidence removal. Under the DME headline objective, for example, the positive-case margin response to target rather than random masking rises from 0.786 with KD to 2.023 with ECDD, while original-image accuracy differs by 0.11 points. These results suggest that medical VLM distillation should transfer both the teacher's answer preference and its dependence on visual evidence.

</details>


### [74] [TAN: A Size- and Form-Adaptive Masked-Convolution Encoder for Tissue Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23475)
*Herschel, Jan,von Gladiss, Anselm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAN：一个0.41M参数的紧凑卷积编码器，直接在超像素原始形状与范围上运算并排除背景，使节点嵌入对填充值严格不变；在BACH上与303M的UNI精度相当（81.1% vs 80.7–82.6%），参数少约740倍、FLOPs少约1190倍且无需预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织图方法把超像素裁剪为包围盒后需pad/resize/mask，导致背景或填充值渗入节点特征；在BACH上仅改变masked区域的填充值就能让冻结编码器精度波动达6个百分点，超过四种最佳编码器之间的差异，且最优填充值随数据集变化，无法一次性固定。

Method: 提出TAN（Tissue-Aware Network）：直接在原生形状和范围上处理每个超像素，在每一层操作中排除背景，从而在构造上保证嵌入对填充不变；在相同掩码与相同图分类器下与基础模型/ResNet18对比，并做“相同架构但标准fill-padded裁剪”的消融。

Result: BACH上TAN（81.1%）与UNI（80.7–82.6%，随填充变化）无显著差异，而参数少约740倍、FLOPs少约1190倍、无预训练；BRACS上接近同协议训练的ResNet18但落后于病理基础模型；消融显示掩码化在不到一半FLOPs下精度相当（BACH名义更高、BRACS更低，均在噪声范围内）。

Conclusion: 预处理中的填充泄漏是可量化的真实问题，按原生形状排除背景的轻量编码器可在不改动下游图分类器的前提下获得填充不变性，并以极小计算代价接近大型基础模型的表现；但评测限于病理分类数据集，缺乏跨域泛化与真实部署验证。

Abstract: Tissue graphs represent histology images as graphs over tissue-conforming superpixels, which keeps homogeneous tissue regions intact instead of cutting them into rectangular patches. To embed superpixels, current encoders crop them to bounding boxes and then pad, resize, or mask them, so background or fill values can leak into the node features. This matters in practice, and especially in medical imaging, where a prediction should depend on the tissue and not on an arbitrary preprocessing choice: on BACH, changing only the fill of the masked region shifts the accuracy of frozen encoders by up to 6 percentage points, larger than the differences among the four best encoders under mean fill, and the preferred fill differs between datasets, so it cannot be fixed once and for all. We present TAN (Tissue-Aware Network), a compact convolutional encoder (0.41M parameters) that processes each superpixel at its native shape and extent and excludes background from every operation, which makes its embeddings exactly invariant to the fill by construction. Under identical masks and an identical graph classifier, TAN's accuracy on BACH is not significantly different from that of the 303M-parameter foundation model UNI (81.1% vs. 80.7-82.6%, depending on the fill), with ~740x fewer parameters, ~1,190x fewer FLOPs, and no pretraining. On BRACS, TAN is close to a ResNet18 trained under the same protocol and trails the histopathology foundation models. An ablation with TAN's architecture but standard processing of fill-padded crops shows that masking keeps accuracy similar (nominally higher on BACH, lower on BRACS, within noise) at less than half the FLOPs.

</details>


### [75] [PONDER: Learning to Generate in the Right Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23531)
*Erbach, Julius,Narnhofer, Dominik,Schiele, Bernt,Lenssen, Jan Eric,Schindler, Konrad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PONDER 用强化学习（组相对策略梯度）学习掩码生成模型的"填充顺序"与"重思"（重新遮蔽先前预测）策略，并提出以 FID 一阶变化为图像级奖励的 FIDgrad。冻结生成器下，ImageNet MAR-B FID 由 4.09 降至 2.78，视觉数独准确率由 3.9% 升至 56.6%（加入 rethinking 达 96.7%，FID 2.70）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码生成模型的采样质量高度依赖于逐步填充缺失变量的顺序，因为顺序决定后续步骤可见的上下文；常用的随机顺序或局部置信度贪心无法全局自适应，也无法修正早期错误预测。

Method: 冻结生成器，训练一个以部分生成样本与生成器特征为输入的策略，决定下一步填充哪些位置以及/或重新遮蔽已填位置（rethinking）；用 group-relative policy gradient 优化，奖励可为约束满足、人类偏好或分布质量；分布质量奖励 FIDgrad 取加入生成样本后 Fréchet Inception Distance 的一阶变化作为图像级奖励。

Result: MAR-B ImageNet 上 FID 4.09→2.78；困难视觉数独准确率 3.9%→56.6%，优于不确定性贪心顺序的 38.7%；叠加 rethinking 后 FID 2.70、数独 96.7%；固定策略下增大推理期 rethinking 预算可持续提升。

Conclusion: 生成顺序与回溯修正可作为可学习的策略问题被有效优化，且不改变生成器权重即可获得显著质量与约束满足提升，推理期可通过预算调节实现质量-计算折中。

Abstract: We introduce PONDER, a method that learns in which order an image generator should fill in content and which of its earlier predictions to revise. Masked generative models construct a sample by repeatedly filling in subsets of missing variables. The order of these partial predictions determines the context available in subsequent steps and therefore affects the quality of the final sample. Common strategies are to choose the order randomly or based on local confidence estimates. Conditioned on the partially generated sample and the generator's features, our policy chooses which positions to fill next and/or which previously filled positions to mask for later regeneration, a mechanism we call rethinking. In this way, the generation process is able to adapt to the evolving context and also to revise earlier predictions. We optimize the policy using group-relative policy gradients, with rewards that measure constraint satisfaction, human preference, or distribution quality. For the latter, we introduce FIDgrad, an image-level reward derived from the (first-order) change in Fr\'echet Inception Distance caused by adding a generated sample to the current feature distribution. With the generators frozen, learned ordering reduces MAR-B ImageNet FID from 4.09 to 2.78 and raises the accuracy on hard visual Sudokus from 3.9\% to 56.6\%, surpassing uncertainty-based greedy ordering (38.7\%). Rethinking further improves these results to 2.70 FID and 96.7\% accuracy, while increasing the rethinking budget of a fixed policy yields continued improvements at inference time.

</details>


### [76] [ReVision: Reprogramming Image Generators for Dense Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23533)
*Yang, Yitong,Wang, Yinglin,Liu, Xuexin,Chen, Wei,Xu, Zhaoyang,Lin, Jiaxin,Chen, Rui,He, Shuting,Wang, Kai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReVision：冻结多模态理解模型+冻结图像生成器，用指令生成瞬时低秩参数实现分割/深度/法向多任务稠密预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将多任务编码进共享参数化的做法对异构数据混合比例敏感、易产生任务间干扰；希望利用生成模型的视觉先验支持稠密感知，同时避免任务特定头、任务ID和存储的adapter。

Method: 以冻结的多模态理解模型联合编码图像与自由形式指令，得到多模态表示，再由可训练参数生成器映射为生成器权重的瞬时低秩更新；每对图像-指令产生一组实例专属预测参数，主干保持冻结，统一RGB输出接口。

Result: 在多样化稠密预测基准上与SOTA竞争性表现；梯度分析显示任务间存在显著梯度冲突；生成更新在权重空间呈结构化、任务与输入相关的组织方式。

Conclusion: 实例级参数生成可缓解多任务共享参数化带来的冲突，为指令驱动的多任务稠密视觉感知提供可行路径；但尚属视觉感知层面，未涉及机器人或3D场景应用。

Abstract: Recent image generators exhibit rich visual priors that can support dense visual perception. However, existing approaches typically encode multiple perception tasks into a shared parameterization, making joint training sensitive to the mixture and weighting of heterogeneous data and prone to cross-task interference. To address this issue, we introduce ReVision, an instruction-conditioned parameter generation framework built on a frozen multimodal understanding model and a frozen image generator. Given an image and a free-form instruction, the understanding model jointly encodes them into a multimodal image--instruction representation, which a trainable parameter generator maps to transient low-rank updates for the generator. Each image--instruction pair induces a distinct set of transient prediction parameters, enabling instance-specific adaptation while both pretrained backbones remain unchanged. ReVision requires no task-specific heads, explicit task identifiers, or stored adapters. A shared RGB output interface unifies segmentation, depth estimation, and surface-normal prediction. Across diverse dense prediction benchmarks, ReVision achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods while supporting flexible task specification through natural-language instructions. Our analysis further reveals substantial gradient disagreement across tasks, highlighting the conflict introduced when heterogeneous objectives share a single parameterization. Meanwhile, the generated updates exhibit structured task- and input-dependent organization in weight space, further validating instance-level parameter generation for multi-task perception.

</details>


### [77] [PreciseEdit: Strong Yet Stable Semantic Transport for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23535)
*Wang, Jingyi,Lu, Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PreciseEdit，一个模型无关、免训练、免反演的一步式图像编辑框架，通过"可靠方向+可靠空间位置"约束强语义传输，并用双噪声尺度角色分解实现方向一致性与空间结构探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一步式 T2I 编辑依赖条件预测间的简单向量运算构造全局传输场，强传输虽能推动语义但常沿不可靠方向或在非编辑区域释放，导致编辑区结构畸变与背景漂移；而全局平滑或均匀减弱传输场又使大幅语义变化过于保守。作者据此重新审视"语义强度 vs 传输稳定性"的张力，指出强语义传输本身并非不稳定，关键在于施加的方向与位置是否可靠。

Method: 模型无关、免训练、免反演的一步式编辑框架 PreciseEdit：不再全局施加传输场，而是将强语义传输约束到可靠方向与可靠空间区域；并提出双噪声尺度角色分解策略——高噪声尺度负责产生用于实际编辑的强目标导向传输，低噪声尺度作为探针，联合估计方向一致性与空间结构信息，从而在保留背景保真与结构一致性的同时强化目标语义对齐。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 上的大量实验表明 PreciseEdit 达到 SOTA 性能，并在多种编辑任务上展现出有效性。

Conclusion: 强语义传输并非天生不稳定，通过双噪声尺度分解可靠地限定传输的方向与空间作用域，可在免训练条件下兼顾编辑强度、背景保真与结构一致性，为一步式交互式 Web 图像编辑提供通用、可插拔的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent one-step text-to-image (T2I) models enable efficient image synthesis, opening up new possibilities for interactive Web-based image editing. However, existing one-step editing methods often construct a global transport field through simple vector operations between conditional predictions. While such strong transport can effectively drive the image toward the target semantics, it may be released along unreliable directions or over non-edited regions, leading to structural distortions within the editing region and background drift elsewhere. More importantly, stabilizing the editing process through global smoothing or uniformly weakening the transport field often makes large semantic changes overly conservative. In this work, we revisit one-step image editing from the perspective of the tension between semantic strength and transport stability, and identify a key insight: strong semantic transport is not inherently unstable. What matters is whether it is applied along reliable directions and at reliable spatial locations. Motivated by this insight, we introduce PreciseEdit, a model-agnostic, training-free and inversion-free one-step editing framework that strengthens target-semantic alignment while preserving background fidelity and structural consistency. We further propose a dual-noise-scale role decomposition strategy: a higher-noise scale produces the strong target-directed transport used for actual editing, whereas a lower-noise scale jointly serves as a probe for directional agreement and spatial structure. Extensive experiments on PIE-Bench demonstrate that PreciseEdit achieves state-of-the-art performance and effectiveness on various editing tasks.

</details>


### [78] [PanoBench: A Panoptic Occupancy Prediction Benchmark for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23537)
*Feng, Yi,Guo, Zizhan,E, Junwu,Ma, Yu,Zhang, Mengtan,Wu, Jin,Ye, Wei,Wang, Hanli,Chen, Qijun,Wang, Danwei,Fan, Rui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoBench：面向全景占据预测的相机中心基准，融合CarlaOcc、nuScenes、KITTI360，提供多分辨率、实例身份持久化的全景标注与统一评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景占据（几何+语义+实例身份）研究受限于实例级标注稀缺；现有语义占据基准多由不完整传感器观测重建，全景数据集则受限于域覆盖或标注完整性。

Method: 构建统一数据接口与评测协议的三分支基准：合成分支由仿真网格导出完整全景几何；真实数据分支提出新流程——用几何先验与拓扑约束重建背景、由多帧多视角图像生成前景网格资产、融合为稀疏全景体素。评测采用统一valid-mask协议：仅评时序观测支撑的完整thing实例，stuff按体素级评分。

Result: 在观测保真度、几何完整性、空间连续性、时序一致性上优于现有占据基准；对语义占据、全景占据、4D占据预测代表方法进行基准测试，分析体素分辨率、合成到真实迁移与存储效率，揭示体素式与射线式指标的互补性及真实场景实例感知预测的持续挑战。

Conclusion: PanoBench为相机中心全景占据提供完整实例标注与统一协议，暴露真实域实例感知预测的瓶颈；但其贡献主要在基准与标注管线层面，未提出可迁移的预测方法。

Abstract: Panoptic occupancy unifies 3D geometry, semantics, and instance identities, but its development is limited by scarce instance-aware annotations. Existing benchmarks mainly provide semantic labels reconstructed from incomplete sensor observations, while panoptic datasets have limited domain coverage or annotation completeness. We introduce PanoBench, a camera-centric panoptic occupancy prediction benchmark spanning synthetic and real-world driving scenes. It combines PanoBench-CarlaOcc, PanoBench-nuScenes, and PanoBench-KITTI360 through canonical data interfaces and unified evaluation protocols, providing multi-resolution annotations with persistent instance identities. The synthetic branch derives complete panoptic geometry from simulated meshes. For real-world data, a new pipeline reconstructs backgrounds using geometric priors and topology constraints, generates foreground mesh assets from multi-frame multi-view images, and fuses them into sparse panoptic voxels. A unified valid-mask protocol evaluates complete thing instances supported by temporal observations while scoring stuff regions at the voxel level. Analyses of observation fidelity, geometric completeness, spatial continuity, and temporal consistency demonstrate the benefits of the resulting annotations over existing occupancy benchmarks. We benchmark representative methods on semantic occupancy prediction, panoptic occupancy prediction, and 4D occupancy forecasting, and study voxel resolution, synthetic-to-real transfer, and storage efficiency. These results reveal complementary strengths of volumetric and ray-based metrics and persistent challenges in real-world instance-aware prediction. PanoBench is available at https://mias.group/PanoBench.

</details>


### [79] [Execution Before Memory: Diagnosing Memory Failures in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23542)
*Jin, Weiyang,Yu, Jiwen,Xu, Sihan,Niu, Yuwei,Liu, Xihui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MIST 评测，将"场景设定"与"指令变更执行"解耦以诊断视频世界模型的记忆瓶颈，并据此提出 M3-Agent 递归改写指令以提升变更执行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型记忆测试把"场景是否被设定"、"变更是否被执行"、"末帧是否呈现结果"折叠为单一分数，低分易被误读为遗忘；若变更未被执行，隐藏状态转移根本不存在，测的并非记忆。

Method: MIST 先让模型在变更隐藏的情况下设定场景，之后才要求呈现变更，并控制此前的泄漏；据此定位瓶颈为"能设定场景但未执行指令"。进而提出 M3-Agent，递归修订输入指令细节以促使冻结模型成功执行所要求的变更。

Result: MIST reveal 分数相对基线提升 3 倍以上；WRBench 的 T 分数提升 26.1%，MBench-T 的 U 分数提升 52.7%。

Conclusion: 从"执行侧"切入：说明指令被明确陈述后冻结模型可达到的能力上限，并给出更严格的评测基线。

Abstract: A memory test for a video world model should say whether the scene was set, whether the asked change was carried out, and whether the last frame shows the result. Current tests fold these into one number, so a low score is easy to read as forgetting. When the change is not carried out, the hidden state transition cannot appear, so the test is not really a test of memory. We introduce MIST, an evaluation that separates the change from the memory it should leave behind. The model first sets the scene while the change stays hidden, and only later is asked to show it, with leakage before that point kept under control. This separation locates the bottleneck: the model sets the scene but does not carry out the instructed change. The same gap appears in general memory evaluations. On that basis we propose M3-Agent (Make the Missing Move), which recursively revises the details of the input instruction so that the model successfully carries out the requested instruction. Relative to the baseline, it more than triples the MIST reveal score. On the benchmarks, WRBench's score T rises by 26.1% and MBench-T's score U by 52.7%. The method meets the problem from the execution side: it shows what the frozen model can do once the move is stated, and it sets a stricter baseline.

</details>


### [80] [LiveSync: Iterative Self-Training for Lip-Sync Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23558)
*Luo, Huaishao,Zhang, Huan,Bao, Guanbo,Liu, Yinglu,Wu, Youzheng,He, Xiaodong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveSync：基于掩码模型的迭代自训练框架，用冻结模型以独立音频生成"嘴部编辑"伪参考，与对齐掩码输入配对后混入真实数据继续精炼同一模型，在不增加推理阶段的前提下提升音频驱动唇形同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然视频中缺少"身份与姿态一致、仅语音口型不同"的训练对；掩码式训练虽可用重建目标，但随机参考输入与推理时的对齐自参考存在分布差异（train-test mismatch）。

Method: 单阶段GAN掩码模型先在精选真实视频语料（并补充外部生成片段覆盖欠代表性视觉条件）上训练；冻结checkpoint用独立采样音频为真实帧生成嘴部编辑参考（掩码外像素直接拷贝源帧）；置信度过滤后与对齐掩码输入、原始真实帧目标配对；伪对与真实数据混合，从当前权重继续精炼同一模型。

Result: 在HDTF上取得领先的同步性能；在AIGC-LipSync子集上与基线相比具竞争力；保持低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 自训练式伪配对精炼可在不新增推理模块的情况下缓解参考输入分布差异，框架原则上可迁移到扩散类模型；本质上仍属说话人脸生成，与具身智能/3D重建主线关联弱。

Abstract: Audio-driven lip synchronization aims to match target speech through localized mouth and jaw articulation while preserving subject identity, head pose, and surrounding facial context. Naturally recorded videos provide synchronized reconstruction targets but rarely offer pairs that are matched in identity and pose yet differ primarily in speech-related articulation. Mask-based training enables reconstruction from these videos, but random-reference inputs differ from the aligned self-reference used at inference. We present LiveSync, an iterative self-training framework in which a single mask-based model generates pseudo pairs and is refined for deployment. The model is first trained on a curated real-video corpus augmented with externally generated clips covering under-represented visual conditions. A frozen checkpoint then uses independently sampled audio to generate mouth-edited references from real-video frames, copying pixels outside the edit mask from each source frame. After confidence filtering, these references are paired with aligned masked inputs and original real-frame targets. The retained pseudo pairs are mixed with real training data to refine the same model from its current weights, updating training supervision without adding inference stages. Although we instantiate the framework with a single-pass GAN-based model, the self-training strategy could in principle also be adapted to diffusion-based models. Experimental results show that LiveSync achieves leading synchronization performance on HDTF and competitive performance compared with the baselines on the AIGC-LipSync subset, while maintaining low inference latency.

</details>


### [81] [From Adult Renal Masses to Pediatric Wilms Tumors: Evaluating Cross-Domain Generalization of Kidney Lesion Segmentation on MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23568)
*de Boer, Sarah,Buser, Myrthe A. D.,Häntze, Hartmut,Nawabi, Jawed,Adams, Lisa C.,van der Steeg, Alida F. W.,Heuvel-Eibrink, Marry M. van den,Littooij, Annemieke S.,Bressem, Keno K.,Prokop, Mathias,Hering, Alessa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在成人肾脏MRI上训练的分割模型可直接迁移到儿童Wilms瘤，中位DSC达0.85（儿科专用模型0.90），但未达到非劣性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童肿瘤分割数据稀缺（小样本、单中心），而成人与成人肾癌数据规模大；作者希望检验成人肾病灶预训练模型能否泛化到儿童Wilms瘤，以缓解儿科数据不足问题。

Method: 在单序列T1或T2加权成人肾脏MRI上训练分割模型，在留出的成人测试集与儿童Wilms瘤队列上评估，并在儿科队列上与儿科专用nnU-Net对比；指标为Dice相似系数（DSC）与95% Hausdorff距离。

Result: 成人测试集中位DSC 0.86；儿科队列中成人训练模型中位DSC 0.85，儿科专用模型0.90，差异不大但非劣性检验未通过。

Conclusion: 跨年龄、解剖与病种的成人数据预训练对儿童Wilms瘤分割有可行性，但儿科专用训练数据仍具显著价值；后续可探索微调策略。

Abstract: Deep learning segmentation models for pediatric oncology are often trained on small, single-center cohorts, whereas much larger datasets exist for adult diseases, such as kidney cancer. Whether models trained on adult renal lesions generalize to pediatric Wilms tumors remains unclear. We trained a segmentation model on single-sequence T1- or T2-weighted adult kidney MRI and evaluated it on a held-out adult test set and a pediatric Wilms tumor cohort, where it was compared with a pediatric-specific nnU-Net. Segmentation performance was quantified using the Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) and 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance. On the adult test set, the model achieved a median DSC of 0.86 for renal lesions. On the pediatric cohort, the adult-trained model achieved a median DSC of 0.85 compared with 0.90 for the pediatric-specific model. Despite differences in age, anatomy, and tumor type, the adult-trained model showed promising performance on pediatric Wilms tumors, with only a modest difference in median DSC compared with the pediatric-specific model. However, non-inferiority could not be shown, highlighting the value of pediatric training data. In future work, a fine-tuning strategy could be explored.

</details>


### [82] [CLEAR: Cooperative Latent Flow for Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Accurate Region Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23577)
*Zeng, Yan,Guo, Changlu,Kristoffersen, Oskar,Christensen, Anders Nymark,Hannemose, Morten Rieger,Dahl, Anders Bjorholm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR：基于潜空间 Flow Matching 的视觉反事实解释框架，利用流匹配的显式瞬时速度作为生成方向参考，将分类器引导分解为协同/冲突分量，仅自适应衰减冲突部分，并动态识别可编辑区域以保持事实内容，兼具潜空间效率与图像空间质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像空间VCE质量高但计算昂贵，潜空间方法高效但忠实度差。作者指出两个成因：(1) 潜生成恢复自然样本的方向与分类器引导方向可能相反；(2) VAE 重建会改动本应保持不变的区域。

Method: 提出 CLEAR：潜空间 Flow Matching 反事实框架。以 Flow Matching 的瞬时速度作为参考方向，将分类器引导梯度分解为与生成方向协同和冲突两部分，保留协同分量、按冲突强度自适应衰减冲突分量；并分析生成全程冲突的频率与强度。同时动态定位可编辑区域，约束潜变量修改，并在区域外直接保留事实内容以减少 VAE 引入的变化。

Result: 在两个数据集、五个双向任务上，CLEAR 保持潜空间效率，反事实质量与最强图像空间基线相当或更优，并相对最强基线实现 2.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: 冲突感知的引导分解与编辑区域约束可有效弥合潜空间与图像空间 VCE 的质量—效率差距，为高效高保真反事实生成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations (VCEs) should alter a classifier's prediction while remaining realistic and preserving decision-irrelevant content. Image-space methods provide high-quality counterfactuals but are computationally costly, whereas latent-space methods are more efficient but often less faithful. We identify two sources responsible for this gap. First, latent generation recovers a natural sample from a perturbed representation, whereas classifier guidance pushes it toward a target class, so their update directions may oppose. Second, VAE reconstruction can alter regions that should remain unchanged. We introduce CLEAR, a latent Flow Matching framework for VCEs. CLEAR uses Flow Matching's explicit instantaneous velocity as a reference for the generative direction to decompose classifier guidance into cooperative and conflicting components. It preserves the cooperative component and adaptively attenuates only the conflicting one according to conflict strength. We further analyze conflict frequency and strength throughout generation to reveal how classifier guidance interacts with the generative process. CLEAR also dynamically identifies editable regions, constrains latent modifications, and directly preserves factual content outside them to reduce VAE-induced changes. Across five bidirectional tasks on two datasets, CLEAR retains latent-space efficiency while achieving counterfactual quality comparable to or better than strong image-space baselines, with a 2.5x speedup over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://yan-zen9.github.io/CLEAR/

</details>


### [83] [Enhancing RAG-based Medical VQA with Gated Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23588)
*Jimenez-Pano, Daniela,Escalante, Hugo Jair,Hernández-Farías, Delia Irazú*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态RAG框架，用门控多模态单元(GMU)学习图像+问题的联合查询表示，用于医学VQA的证据检索，Med-LVLM保持冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Med-LVLM在医学视觉问答中仍会产生事实不一致的幻觉响应；RAG可引入外部临床证据缓解，但现有检索通常由单一模态引导，忽略医学图像与临床问题的联合语义。

Method: 保持底层Med-LVLM冻结，仅训练一个门控多模态单元(Gated Multimodal Unit)将图像与文本问题融合为联合查询表示，以此驱动外部证据检索，再做检索增强生成。

Result: 在基准数据集上，多模态查询表示稳定优于参考Med-LVLM，并与当前SOTA方法具有竞争力，但未给出具体数值或泛化证据。

Conclusion: 多模态查询表示能提升医学VQA中的证据检索效果，为多模态RAG在医学问答中的应用提供了初步验证。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-LVLMs) have shown strong performance in Medical Visual Question Answering (MVQA), yet they may still produce factually inconsistent responses. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can mitigate this issue by incorporating external clinical evidence; however, retrieval is often guided by a single modality, potentially overlooking the joint semantics of medical images and clinical questions. We introduce a multimodal RAG framework that learns a joint query representation through a Gated Multimodal Unit, enabling evidence retrieval to leverage information from both modalities while keeping the underlying Med-LVLM frozen. We evaluate the proposed approach on benchmark datasets. Results show that the multimodal query representation consistently outperforms a reference Med-LVLM and remains competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our findings demonstrate the potential of multimodal query representations for more effective evidence retrieval in Medical VQA.

</details>


### [84] [SemPurify: Semantics-Aware Data Purification for Black-box Backdoor Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23589)
*Yu, Jiawei,Meerza, Syed Irfan Ali,Wu, Yi,Sadovnik, Amir,Liu, Jian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemPurify：首个语义感知的数据净化框架，用低层语义嵌入与高层图像标签引导 T2I 模型修复被变换的样本，实现黑盒后门防御并保留语义信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像变换（如模糊）的后门净化方法会破坏触发模式但同时损伤图像质量；即便借助扩散模型修复，仍会改变视觉或语义信息，降低下游任务效用。需要一种在去后门的同时保语义的净化方案。

Method: 提出 SemPurify：先对样本施加变换以破坏触发，再在 text-to-image 框架内显式引入多级语义（低层语义嵌入 + 高层图像标签）作为条件，引导 T2I 模型对变换后样本进行修复式重建，从而在恢复过程中保留视觉细节与语义。

Result: 在分类模型、CLIP 类编码器与生成模型等多种后门场景下实验，SemPurify 能可靠移除后门，同时有效保留语义信息，维持下游任务效用。

Conclusion: 语义感知的生成式修复是黑盒后门净化中兼顾安全性与数据效用的可行路径，扩展了 T2I 模型在数据安全领域的应用。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks have emerged as a critical security threat in machine learning, where adversaries embed triggers within poisoned training samples to manipulate a model's predictions on targeted inputs while leaving its general behavior unaffected. This stealthy nature makes backdoor attacks difficult to detect, as the model functions normally until activated by a specific malicious trigger. Current purification-based defenses use image transformations (e.g., blurring) to disrupt trigger patterns but often degrade image quality. Even with advanced restoration techniques like diffusion models, these approaches can still alter visual or semantic information, reducing utility for downstream tasks. To address this, we propose the first semantics-aware data purification framework for black-box backdoor defense, SemPurify, which explicitly incorporates multi-level semantics (i.e., low-level semantic embeddings and high-level image tags) within a text-to-image (T2I) framework to guide image restoration during purification. Using this semantic information, the T2I model subsequently refines the transformed samples, helping preserve visual details and semantic information during restoration. Extensive experiments spanning multiple backdoor scenarios, i.e., classification models, CLIP-style encoders, and generative models, demonstrate that SemPurify reliably removes backdoors while effectively preserving semantic information for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [85] [Learning to Discriminate Scene Structures Makes Self-Supervised Depth Learning Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23600)
*Zhang, Mengtan,Cui, Yuchuan,Ma, Yu,Guo, Zizhan,Ye, Wei,Fan, Rui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaSS框架将结构表征通路与上下文特征显式解耦，并用场景-结构判别目标引导，获得可泛化的自监督单目深度结构表征，首次实现多域混合训练持续增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督单目深度估计本应受益于无标注数据规模扩展，但现有方法在多样场景联合训练时性能退化：模型学到的是数据集特有的结构偏置而非可迁移的场景几何，导致跨域优化冲突。

Method: 提出专用结构表征分支，与上下文特征通路解耦，由新的"场景-结构判别"（scene-structure discrimination）目标监督，学习布局感知的通用结构特征供深度解码使用。

Result: 在四个多样数据集上单独与联合训练均显著优于先前自监督方法，且是首个能从混合域训练中稳定获益的方法；在另外八个数据集上的零样本评测展现强泛化性，尤其在完全分布外场景。

Conclusion: 解耦结构表征是消解跨域优化冲突、实现自监督深度可扩展训练的有效途径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation is appealing for its potential to scale with unlabeled data, yet existing methods often degrade when trained on diverse scenes. This study finds that, under self-supervision, models tend to encode dataset-specific structural biases rather than transferable scene geometry, leading to cross-domain optimization conflicts. To address this issue, this study proposes the MaSS framework, aiming to make self-supervised depth learning scalable to diverse data. MaSS introduces a dedicated structural representation pathway explicitly decoupled from contextual features, which is guided by a novel scene-structure discrimination objective to learn generalized, layout-aware structural representations for depth decoding. Trained and evaluated on four diverse datasets, both individually and jointly, MaSS substantially outperforms prior self-supervised methods and is the first to consistently benefit from mixed-domain training. Zero-shot evaluation on eight additional datasets further demonstrates its remarkable generalizability, particularly on those entirely out-of-distribution scenes. The source code is publicly available at https://mias.group/MaSS.

</details>


### [86] [ADA 2025: A Multimodal Dataset for Water-Deficit Stress Detection in Sugar Beets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23609)
*Powell, Piper,Franz, Robert,Smith, Amos,Ihorst, Isaak,Hertzberg, Joachim,Atzmueller, Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布 ADA 2025：>500 GB 多模态甜菜幼苗数据集（热成像、高光谱、RGB、深度 + 环境/元数据），含相对含水量与叶片厚度真值，面向水分亏缺胁迫检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业 AI 应用（尤其水分亏缺胁迫检测）缺乏公开数据集，现有数据多为单一 RGB 模态、样本量少、采样间隔大、缺乏准确元数据，无法支撑多模态早期检测研究。

Method: 构建并公开大规模多模态采集数据集：对健康与水分亏缺条件下的甜菜幼苗同步采集热成像、高光谱、RGB 与深度图像，并记录环境参数与元数据，提供相对含水量（RWC）和叶片厚度两类真值标注。

Result: 数据集规模超 500 GB；示例分析显示胁迫与健康植株之间存在可检测的模态差异，验证了数据集的可用性与标注有效性。

Conclusion: ADA 2025 为农业多模态胁迫检测提供了首个规模化公开基准，可作为多模态表征学习与早期胁迫检测的数据基础，但未提出新的建模方法。

Abstract: Research and development into artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) applications in crop management are hindered by a lack of suitable public datasets. Whereas applications in disease and pest detection benefit from a comparatively higher amount of available data, applications like water-deficit stress detection often have very few if any public datasets available. Moreover, most datasets that are available are single-modality and frequently RGB-only, leaving the early detection and enhanced performance offered by other methods, and particularly by multimodal approaches, out of reach. Datasets with accurate metadata, higher numbers of plants, and smaller intervals between data points are also scarce. To address this, we publicly release ADA 2025, a >500 GB comprehensive multimodal dataset of young sugar beet plants under healthy and water-deficit conditions combining thermal, hyperspectral, RGB, and depth imagery along with relevant environmental and metadata and both relative water content (RWC) and leaf thickness as ground truth sources. Exemplary results showcase the dataset's validity in terms of differences detectable between stressed and healthy plants.

</details>


### [87] [Not Every Mention Is a Claim: Rethinking Object Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23610)
*Hsu, Hsin-Ling,Kirtane, Neeraja,Huang, Kuan-Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 NAME-Bench，将 VLM 图像描述中的对象提及区分为「断言型（具体/幻觉）」与「非断言型（描述性/目的性/否定/模糊限定）」四亚类，共标注 25,497 条提及，揭示现有幻觉检测器普遍将非断言提及误判为幻觉（14%–80%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流基准与检测器默认「提到某物体=断言其存在」，导致使用 such as "a bench for people to sit on" 这类非断言表达的 VLM 被虚高错误率，需要将「提及」与「断言」区分开来评估幻觉。

Method: 构建 NAME-Bench：5 个 VLM 对 COCO 图像生成的描述中抽取 25,497 条对象提及，人工标注为 concrete / hallucinated / non-assertive，并将非断言细分为 depictive、purpose、negation、hedged 四类；评估现有幻觉检测器，并尝试将非断言提及作为负样本加入检测器训练。

Result: 所有被测检测器均以 14%–80% 的比例将非断言提及误标为幻觉；在 LLaVA-1.5 上加入非断言负样本可基本纠正该问题，而在四个更新 VLM 上需 5–10 倍数据才能达到相当纠正效果，且召回真实幻觉的能力随之下降。

Conclusion: 当前检测器混淆了「提及」与「断言」，且这一混淆在新一代 VLM 上更难消除；幻觉评测应显式区分描述所断言的内容与其仅提及的内容。

Abstract: Object hallucination in vision-language models (VLMs) is usually measured by checking each object a caption names against the image. Most benchmarks and detectors alike treat every object mention as a claim that the object is present. This assumption fails when a caption names an object without asserting it, as in "a bench for people to sit on", which mentions people without claiming that any are in the image. We call such mentions non-assertive. Treating them as hallucinations inflates the error rates attributed to models that use such language. To separate them from real hallucinations, we introduce NAME-Bench (Non-Assertive Mention Evaluation Benchmark), 25,497 object mentions from captions that five VLMs generate for COCO images. Each mention is labelled concrete (asserted and present), hallucinated (asserted and absent), or non-assertive, and non-assertive mentions are further divided into four subtypes: depictive, purpose, negation and hedged. All the hallucination detectors that we evaluate flag non-assertive mentions as hallucinations, at rates between 14% and 80%. Adding non-assertive mentions to detector training as negatives largely corrects this on LLaVA-1.5. On the four newer VLMs, a comparable correction takes five to ten times more data, and the detectors then catch fewer real hallucinations. These findings show that current detectors conflate mentioning an object with asserting it, and that this conflation is harder to undo on the four newer VLMs than on LLaVA-1.5. Hallucination evaluation should therefore separate what a caption asserts from what it merely mentions.

</details>


### [88] [Stereo LWIR-RGB Automatic Labeling for Low-Light Camera-Based VRU Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23621)
*Bouzoulas, Dimitrios,Jäppinen, Pyry,Habibiroudkenar, Pejman,Alamikkotervo, Eerik,Ojala, Risto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用LWIR-RGB立体相机的自动流程，从热红外检测结果生成低光照下VRU标注，免去专用对齐硬件；生成标签训练的检测器在F1、mAP@50、LAMR上优于现成基线与手工标注微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照VRU检测是保障碰撞高发时段行人安全的关键环节，但缺乏大规模数据集与自动标注方法；现有自动标注多以RGB检测为标签源，无法迁移到低光照场景。

Method: 采用LWIR-RGB图像对，以LWIR检测作为标签源（VRU因体温与背景可区分），并改用LWIR-RGB立体相机替代此前复杂对齐硬件，构建端到端自动标注流水线，在芬兰首都区采集数据上评估。

Result: 所生成标签训练的模型在手工标注测试集上于F1、mAP@50、LAMR均超过现成基线和手工标注子集微调模型，结论在两种不同LWIR检测器上均成立。

Conclusion: 低光照VRU自动标注无需专用对齐硬件即可实现，兼具可复现性与有效性，可降低数据构建门槛。

Abstract: VRU detection in low-light conditions is a key component in ensuring VRU safety during the most collision prone time of the day. Currently, advances in low-light detection are limited due to the lack of large scale datasets and automated labeling methods. Most autolabeling methods use RGB detections as their labels source, which cannot be replicated in low-light conditions. To fill this gap in this paper we present an automated pipeline for low-light VRU labeling. We use LWIR-RGB image pairs and source our labels from LWIR as VRUs differentiate from the background due to their body temperature. Unlike our previous work that relied on a complex hardware setup to align the two modalities, we perform this experiment using a LWIR-RGB stereo setup, lowering hardware complexity and cost, making the pipeline easier to reproduce. We evaluate method end-to-end on a dataset collected in the capital region of Finland. Models trained on our generated labels beat both off-the-shelf baselines and models fine tuned on a hand labeled subset on F1, mAP@50 and LAMR metrics when tested on a manually labeled dataset, and the result holds for two different LWIR detectors. This shows that low-light VRU autolabeling can be made both accessible and effective without specialized alignment hardware.

</details>


### [89] [Diversity Without Labels: Task-Agnostic Coreset Selection Across Perception and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23642)
*Hindel, Julia,Ramtoula, Benjamin,Yeon, Kyuhwan,Heppert, Nick,Newman, Paul,De Martini, Daniele,Valada, Abhinav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoreDNA: 一种无监督、任务无关的coreset估计方法，通过池化视觉基础模型各层神经元激活构建图像级与数据集级分布，利用"高质量样本位于最后一层激活统计尾部"的发现进行高效数据选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有coreset方法依赖代理模型训练、大量标注或任务定制；无监督方法局限于成对图像比较或与固定子集的比较，难以兼顾低层视觉结构与高层语义信息，且缺乏任务无关的通用性。

Method: 从视觉基础模型的神经元激活中构建逐图像分布与数据集级分布，跨层比较二者以同时捕获低层结构与高层语义；发现高质量样本集中于最后一层激活统计的尾部，并将其与基于训练的coreset方法建立关联以验证；基于此设计计算高效的选择机制，构建任务无关的高质量coreset用于选择性标注与模型训练。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割、深度估计，以及端到端驾驶和移动操作的模仿学习上，匹配或超越SOTA，展现出跨感知与动作任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 紧凑的激活表示是数据选择的有效信号；任务无关的无监督coreset可在多类感知与具身任务中替代或优于需要代理训练与标注的方法。

Abstract: While data collection for model training is relatively easy, curating high-quality datasets from large collections is inherently challenging. Coreset estimation methods address this problem by identifying the best data subsets to train models on. However, existing approaches often require proxy model training, extensive annotations, or task-specific tailoring. Unsupervised alternatives typically exploit geometric properties in foundation model feature spaces, but are limited to pairwise image-to-image comparisons or comparisons against a fixed subset. We introduce CoreDNA, an unsupervised, task-agnostic coreset estimation approach that pools neuron activations from vision foundation models into per-image and per-dataset distributions. By comparing each image's distribution to the dataset-level distribution, CoreDNA can capture both low-level visual structure and high-level semantic information across layers. We find that high-quality samples concentrate in the tails of the last layer's activation statistics, which we validate by relating them to training-based coreset methods. Building on these findings, we develop a computationally efficient selection mechanism to construct task-agnostic, high-quality coresets for selective annotation and model training. Across image classification, semantic segmentation, depth estimation, and imitation learning for end-to-end driving and mobile manipulation, CoreDNA matches or outperforms the state of the art, demonstrating unprecedented generalization across perception and action tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of compact activation-based representations for data selection.

</details>


### [90] [A Language-Informed Loss for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23653)
*Agarwal, Suchir,Chandrasegaran, Keshigeyan,Chen, Ziyu,Sargent, Kyle,Niebles, Juan Carlos,Fei-Fei, Li,Wu, Jiajun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LILIE：一种可逆图像编码器，通过归一化流transformer与VLM联合训练，使VLM能从加噪特征中恢复caption；由此提取语言监督损失用于文本到图像生成，在GPIC上gFD由66.84降至53.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端像素空间生成已具竞争力，但像素损失监督信号较为贫乏；作者探索语言先验/语言模型监督能否为视觉生成提供更丰富的训练信号。

Method: LILIE是语言知情且无损（可逆）的图像变换：归一化流transformer作为编码器，与视觉语言模型通过下一词元预测联合训练，要求VLM在噪声特征下还原caption；该编码器用于为JiT（Just-in-time）生成模型定义简洁的语言知情重建损失。

Result: GPIC数据集上，使用LILIE损失的JiT模型将gFD从66.84降至53.01（降低14点），相比纯像素损失基线；代码、编码器与生成模型已开源。

Conclusion: 语言模型可充当可逆编码器的语义监督器，构成比像素损失更有结构的生成目标；方法可逆、无损且开源，具备扩展到其他生成架构的潜力。

Abstract: End-to-end visual generation with pixel-space loss has recently achieved competitive performance without the need for learned image tokenizers. In this work, we study whether language-informed supervision can provide a richer training signal than pixel loss for visual generation. We introduce LILIE (pronounced "lily"), a Language-Informed and Lossless Image Encoder. LILIE is an invertible image transform trained such that a vision-language model can recover captions from noised features. The encoder uses a normalizing flow transformer trained jointly with a vision-language model via next token prediction. We then use the encoder to extract a simple language-informed loss for text-to-image generation. We train JiT models with LILIE loss, reducing gFD by 14 points (66.84 $\to$ 53.01) against a pixel-only baseline on the GPIC dataset. Our code, encoders, and generative models are available at https://suchiragarwal.com/lilie.

</details>


### [91] [FlexPart: Text-Controlled Semantics-Aware 3D Part Segmentation at Flexible Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23659)
*Zhao, Yiqun,Xu, Jiachen,Zhi, Yihao,Zhao, Yunfei,Zhao, Zibo,Han, Xintong,Guo, Chunchao,Gao, Shenghua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLEXPART 是一个文本可控的 3D 网格部件分割统一框架：用"部件名+位置/形状子句"的整体控制提示，借助 VLM 自动构建多粒度分区数据集，在原生 3D 空间生成视角无关的部件身份场，直接为面片分配离散部件 ID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 资产存在多种合理部件分解方式，语义分割应同时反映用户期望的粒度与目标部件；但缺少多粒度训练数据和整体控制机制，使"所问即所得"难以实现。

Method: (1) 引入含语义部件名、位置与形状子句的整体控制提示；(2) 用自动化 VLM 流水线构建大规模多粒度分区块与对应提示数据集；(3) 模型输出视角无关的 3D 部件身份场，直接给网格面分配离散部件 ID；(4) 训练时使用随机背景丢弃，迫使模型依赖语义条件而非几何先验；(5) 推理时 VLM planner 从 2D 渲染自动补全描述子句，用户只需给出普通词名。

Result: 在 planner 补全提示下，比最强基线 mIoU 高 15%（即使基线获得真值引导）；在自建 FLEXPARTBENCH 上各粒度均领先，平均 mIoU 提升 22%；仅给定部件名时，选择性分割 IoU 比最强点击驱动基线高 35%。

Conclusion: FLEXPART 以可控提示+多粒度数据+原生 3D 身份场，实现了粒度与目标部件均可指定的"所问即所得"式 3D 分割，并在自动提示补全下保持可用性。

Abstract: A 3D asset admits multiple valid part decompositions, while semantics-aware segmentation should reflect both the desired granularity and the parts a user wants to extract. Ideally, a practical segmentation model should strive for a simple principle: what you ask for is what you get. However, realizing this goal is hindered by two core obstacles: the lack of multi-granularity training data and the absence of a holistic control mechanism. We propose FLEXPART, a unified framework for text-controlled 3D mesh segmentation. To establish flexible control, we introduce a holistic control prompt comprising semantic part names with location and shape clauses. To support this mechanism, we utilize an automated Vision-Language Model (VLM) pipeline to construct a large-scale dataset of multi-granularity partitions and their corresponding prompts. Driven by this data, FLEXPART generates a view-independent part-identity field in native 3D space, directly assigning discrete part IDs to mesh faces. To compel the model to respect semantic conditions rather than rely on geometric priors, we train the model using randomized background dropping. Furthermore, an inference-time VLM planner can automatically complete descriptive clauses from 2D renderings, allowing users to simply name the desired parts in ordinary words. FLEXPART with planner-completed prompts outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline by 15% in mIoU, even when that baseline receives ground-truth guidance. On our held-out FLEXPARTBENCH, FLEXPART leads at each granularity and improves average mIoU by 22% over the strongest baseline. With only the requested part names supplied by the user, it also improves IoU by 35% over the strongest click-driven baseline on selective segmentation.

</details>


### [92] [An Efficient Approach to Beetle Damage Detection of Banana Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23661)
*Naeem, Md. Mehedi Hasan,Araf, Ishtiyak Ahmmad,Mahir, Md. Arefin Haque,Saha, Tushar Kanti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用 YOLOv8n 在孟加拉本地市场图像上做香蕉实例定位与表面健康/不健康二分类，测试集 F1 77.67%、mAP@0.5 87.04%，模型 5.96 MB、T4 上 6.54 ms/图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 香蕉表面可见疤痕会降低市场价值，人工目检主观且难以规模化；需一个轻量、可部署的实例级表面检测基线。

Method: 自建 2,473 张图、5,827 个实例标注数据集（80/20，1982 训练 / 491 测试）；预训练 YOLOv8n，640×640、50 epoch、batch=4；与 YOLOv8s 对比。文中明确"甲虫损害"仅为操作性描述，不识别物种或生物成因。

Result: 测试集 precision 81.36%、recall 74.30%、F1 77.67%、mAP@0.50 87.04%、mAP@0.50:0.95 59.33%；YOLOv8s F1/mAP 略低且权重大得多；YOLOv8n 权重 5.96 MB，Tesla T4 推理 6.54 ms/图。

Conclusion: YOLOv8n 可作为给定采集条件下香蕉实例级表面检测的紧凑基线，但结论局限于该数据域，未涉及生物学成因判别或跨场景泛化验证。

Abstract: Visible surface damage of banana fruits by Basilepta subcostata (the banana fruit scarring beetle) can reduce their market value. Here, manual inspection of Banana fruits is subjective and difficult to scale. This study presents a lightweight framework based on YOLOv8n deep learning model for locating individual bananas and classifying their visible surface condition as healthy or unhealthy. The term beetle damage in the paper title is used operationally for the locally observed scar-like surface condition; the model does not identify a specific beetle species or biological cause. A custom dataset of 2,473 images containing 5,827 annotated banana instances was prepared from local-market imagery in Bangladesh. The reported experiment used an 80/20 protocol with 1,982 training images and 491 test images. A pretrained YOLOv8n model was trained at 640x640 resolution for 50 epochs with a batch size of 4. On the test partition, it achieved 81.36% precision, 74.30% recall, 77.67% F1-score, 87.04% mAP@0.50 and 59.33% mAP@0.50:0.95. A YOLOv8s comparison gave slightly lower F1 and mAP while using a substantially larger checkpoint. The YOLOv8n checkpoint occupied 5.96 MB and required 6.54 ms/image for inference on an NVIDIA Tesla T4. The results support YOLOv8n as a compact baseline for instance-level banana surface inspection within the studied acquisition conditions.

</details>


### [93] [PCDFusion: Proposal-Context-Detail Bayesian Rendering for Infrared-Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23663)
*Liu, Rui,Gu, Lin,Li, Hesong,Fu, Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将红外-可见光图像融合重构为局部贝叶斯后验渲染，提出以可见光为锚的三阶段框架 PCDFusion，用后验动作权重控制融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光与热红外观测物理互补但各自不可靠，现有深度 IVIF 方法侧重特征交互与重建，缺乏"何时以可见光外观为主、何时引入热响应"的显式局部决策规则。

Method: 将 IVIF 形式化为局部贝叶斯后验渲染，融合由后验动作权重而非直接模态混合控制；PCDFusion 含三个渲染阶段：Proposal（保留潜在有用的热响应）、Context（更新过曝/退化可见光区域）、Detail（将可靠的带限红外结构渲染进最终亮度）。

Result: 在公开 IVIF 基准上，融合质量、目标恢复以及挑战性光照下的下游检测与分割性能均有提升；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以后验动作权重驱动的可见光锚定渲染为 IVIF 提供了显式局部决策机制，融合质量与下游任务收益同步改善。

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) aims to combine visible and thermal infrared observations for human perception and downstream vision tasks. Visible and thermal infrared observations are physically complementary but unreliable in different ways. Existing deep IVIF methods mainly improve feature interaction and reconstruction, but often lack an explicit local rule for deciding when visible appearance should dominate and when thermal responses should contribute. To address this issue, we reformulate IVIF as local Bayesian posterior rendering, where fusion is controlled by posterior action weights rather than direct modality mixing. In this paper, we propose Proposal-Context-Detail Fusion (PCDFusion), a visible-anchored framework that implements this formulation through three rendering stages. PCDFusion preserves potentially useful thermal responses, updates exposure-degraded visible regions, and renders reliable band-limited infrared structures into the final luminance. Extensive experiments on public IVIF benchmarks show that PCDFusion improves fusion quality, target recovery, and downstream detection and segmentation performance under challenging illumination. The code is available at https://github.com/BIT-RuiLiu/PCDFusion.git.

</details>


### [94] [Agentic Physics-Grounded Video Generation via Memory-Augmented In-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23677)
*Guan, Xikang,Yang, Shaocong,Bao, Yanqi,Wang, Can,Gu, Zheng,Chen, Zhengqing,Chen, Kai,Huo, Jing,Gao, Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysRepair: an agentic post-hoc video repair framework that uses spatiotemporal causal constraints to locate the first physically invalid state plus experience-augmented in-context learning for localized regeneration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing physically-plausible video generation imposes guidance before synthesis (simulations, refined prompts), which restricts dynamic diversity, cannot fix errors that only surface after generation, and needs repeated full regeneration. A generate-diagnose-repair paradigm is proposed to keep the original generative capability while selectively correcting physical inconsistencies.

Method: Agentic framework: (1) Spatiotemporal Causal Constraints identify the first invalid physical state and its causally affected states, yielding a repair specification that drives physically plausible local regeneration while preserving unaffected content; (2) Experience-augmented In-context Learning combines the current repair context with retrieved experience from prior repairs, enabling adaptive repair decisions across failure types and accumulating trajectories as a memory/experience pool.

Result: On Physics-IQ Verified spanning solid mechanics, fluid dynamics, optics, thermodynamics and magnetism, PhysRepair consistently improves physical plausibility of generated videos over existing methods.

Conclusion: Post-generation diagnosis-and-localized-repair with causal localization plus experience-augmented in-context learning is an effective and reusable route to physically plausible video generation, avoiding over-constraining the initial generation.

Abstract: Physical plausibility is essential for realistic video generation. Existing methods impose physical guidance before synthesis via simulations or refined prompts, but this can limit dynamic diversity, miss errors that emerge only after generation, and require repeated regeneration when inconsistencies remain. We therefore study a generate-diagnose-repair paradigm that preserves the original generation capability and selectively repairs physical inconsistencies afterward, raising two key questions: what to repair and how to repair? To address them, we introduce PhysRepair, an agentic video generation framework that combines causal reasoning over the observed physical failure with prior repair experience to enable localized and adaptive correction. Specifically, Spatiotemporal Causal Constraints identify the first invalid physical state and its causally affected states, producing a repair specification that guides physically plausible local regeneration while preserving unaffected content. Experience-augmented In-context Learning combines the ongoing repair context with relevant experience from previous repairs, allowing the agent to adapt its repair decisions across diverse physical failures and accumulate completed trajectories for future cases. Experiments on Physics-IQ Verified across solid mechanics, fluid dynamics, optics, thermodynamics, and magnetism demonstrate that PhysRepair consistently improves the physical plausibility of generated videos over existing methods. Our project page is available at https://xxkkxxx.github.io/PhysRepair-Website/.

</details>


### [95] [FedRAPP: Federated Test-Time Prompt Tuning for Vision-Language Models with Reliability-Aware Prompt Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23705)
*Hasan, Syed Md. Ahnaf,Iftee, Md Akil Raihan,Mistry, Sajib,Ali, Amin Ahsan,Rahman, AKM Mahbubur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出源自由联邦测试时提示微调任务(SF-FTTPT),并给出FedRAPP框架,通过可靠性感知的客户端评估、服务器端EMA提示记忆与相似度引导的对等聚合实现个性化聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦提示学习方法面向有标签、分布相对稳定的监督设定;测试时客户端收到无标签且非IID异构的目标流,本地适配的提示会朝不同方向漂移,导致常规联邦聚合失效。源自由联邦测试时提示微调尚未被充分探索。

Method: FedRAPP个性化聚合框架,包含三个组件:(1)可靠性感知的客户端评估;(2)服务器端指数移动平均(EMA)提示记忆;(3)相似度引导的对等聚合;选择性地融合可靠且兼容客户端的信息,同时保留本地特化。

Result: 在9个CLIP基准数据集及ImageNet变体上,异构客户端分布下FedRAPP持续优于标准联邦聚合基线,并保持仅提示适配的效率优势。

Conclusion: 个性化、可靠性加权的聚合是应对联邦测试时分布异构的有效手段,提示级适配即可在无源数据条件下实现稳健的协同自适应。

Abstract: Federated prompt learning enables vision-language models like CLIP to collaboratively adapt lightweight soft prompts across distributed clients without sharing raw data. However, existing federated prompt learning methods are designed primarily for supervised settings with labeled client data and assumed relatively stable training distributions. At test time, source-free federated prompt tuning remains largely unexplored. Clients may receive unlabeled target samples with heterogeneous, often non-IID distributions that differ substantially across clients and from the source training distribution, causing locally adapted prompts to drift in different directions and reducing the effectiveness of federated aggregation. In this work, we explore Source-Free Federated Test-Time Prompt Tuning (SF-FTTPT), where distributed clients collaboratively adapt soft prompts on unlabeled test streams without access to source data. Our study reveals that conventional federated aggregation methods struggle under heterogeneous client distributions, as locally adapted prompts become increasingly specialized and difficult to combine effectively. To address this challenge, we propose FedRAPP, a personalized aggregation framework that combines reliability-aware client assessment, server-side exponential moving-average prompt memory, and similarity-guided peer aggregation. FedRAPP selectively incorporates information from reliable and compatible clients while preserving local specialization, enabling more robust adaptation under distribution shift. Extensive experiments across nine CLIP benchmark datasets and ImageNet variants demonstrate that FedRAPP consistently outperforms standard federated aggregation baselines under heterogeneous client distributions while maintaining the efficiency advantages of prompt-only adaptation.

</details>


### [96] [Synthetic Image Oversampling for Rare-Class Representation Learning in Deep Facial Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23730)
*Liao, Miles,Wang, Zhanliang,Chen, Hongzhuo,Wang, Kai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究疾病特异性合成过采样能否提升罕见遗传综合征的面部表型识别，并考察不同增广强度与各疾病获益的差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者面部图像稀缺且在各疾病间分布不均，合成图像可在不额外采集真实照片的情况下补充训练数据；但其价值取决于能否提升对真实患者的识别，需明确增广用量与受益疾病。

Method: 使用表型疾病图像数据库生成的疾病特异性合成图像，对GestaltMatcher数据库子集做分层过采样；固定真实训练集与患者不交叉的评估划分，在图像模型GestaltMatcher-Arc与多模态模型GestaltMML上逐级提高增广比例，重复训练评估整体与疾病级准确率，并用嵌入分析考察类内紧致性与类间分离度变化。

Result: 两种训练设置下平均识别准确率均随增广提升，但GestaltMatcher-Arc增益有限，GestaltMML在测试的各增广水平上持续提升；疾病层面响应不均衡，部分疾病准确率反而下降。嵌入分析显示真实训练样本较少的疾病类间分离度提升更明显，但几何变化与分类性能并不一致对应。

Conclusion: 合成过采样可支撑低数据量的面部表型识别，但需针对具体训练设置评估合适的增广量与逐疾病的实际收益，不能默认整体增益等同于普遍受益。

Abstract: Recent deep facial phenotyping methods can support the recognition of genetic syndromes, but patient images are often scarce and unevenly distributed across disorders. Synthetic facial images offer a way to supplement the training data without collecting additional patient photographs. Their usefulness, however, depends on whether they improve recognition of real patients: generated images may introduce artifacts or repeat features already represented in the training set. It is therefore necessary to examine both how much synthetic augmentation to use and which disorders benefit. We investigate disease-specific synthetic oversampling using images from the Phenotypic Disease Image Database to augment a subset of the GestaltMatcher Database. We compare increasing augmentation levels in an image-based model, GestaltMatcher-Arc, and a multimodal model, GestaltMML, while keeping the real training and patient-disjoint evaluation partitions fixed. Repeated training runs assess overall and disease-specific accuracy, and an exploratory embedding analysis examines changes in class compactness and separation. Augmentation improves mean recognition accuracy in both training setups, although gains remain modest in GestaltMatcher-Arc and continue across the tested levels in GestaltMML. These aggregate improvements are accompanied by uneven disease-level responses, including reduced accuracy for some disorders. In the embedding analysis, disorders with fewer real training images tend to gain more class separation, but geometric changes do not consistently track classification performance. Together, the findings support synthetic oversampling for limited-data facial phenotyping and emphasize the need to assess its amount and benefit for individual disorders within each training setup.

</details>


### [97] [ESC: Escaping 2D Diffusion Ambiguity with Geometric Constraints for Consistent Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23732)
*Luo, Ge Ya,Severo, Daniel,Karrer, Brian,Pal, Christopher,Romero-Soriano, Adriana,Drozdzal, Michal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出联合生成目标视图及其深度图的扩散框架，通过源图像、源深度与目标相机射线条件注入，强制模型在3D空间中推理，并引入逐像素深度一致性约束以保证生成视图间的3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张2D图像在剧烈视角变化下生成3D一致的新视图仍是核心难题：纯2D扩散模型缺乏几何推理能力，生成的多视图在空间上易错位。作者希望通过"视图+深度"联合生成让模型显式在3D中推理。

Method: 以单目源图像、源深度图和目标相机射线作为条件输入到扩散管线，同时生成目标视图与对应深度图；并设计逐像素深度一致性约束（per-pixel depth consistency），用于校验并强制跨视图的三维一致性。

Result: 摘要层面声称在极端视角变化下取得优于当前SOTA的视觉精度与空间对齐效果，形成几何一致的单目新视图合成管线；未给出具体定量指标或数据集细节。

Conclusion: 该工作为几何一致的新视图合成提供了较完整流程，并指向可从单视图理解复杂环境的鲁棒3D世界模型；但其属于视图合成/3D生成邻近方向，缺乏机器人或交互场景验证与具体实验证据。

Abstract: Generating 3D-consistent novel views from 2D diffusion models remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. In this work, we introduce a framework that enhances geometric alignment for monocular view synthesis under drastic camera changes by jointly generating a target view and its corresponding depth map. By conditioning the diffusion pipeline on the single source image, source depth, and target camera rays, our approach forces the model to inherently reason in 3D space even under extreme viewpoint shifts. To guarantee spatial fidelity, we propose a novel per-pixel depth consistency constraint, which verifies and enforces 3D consistency across generated views. Our framework establishes a robust pipeline for geometrically consistent view synthesis, yielding superior visual accuracy and spatial alignment compared to current state-of-the-art methods. With this work, we pave the road towards robust 3D world models capable of understanding complex environments from a single view image.

</details>


### [98] [Material and Defocus Cues for Occluded Object Localization in Single Hyperspectral Images: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23733)
*Zia, Ali,Zhou, Jun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 探索性工作：用高光谱图像结合NMF材质分组、散度光学线索与图拉普拉斯传播来分离部分遮挡物体，并以30个室内+6个室外场景给出定性示例，尚未做系统定量评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分遮挡下可见区域纹理弱或外观相似，导致物体难以区分；高光谱成像提供材质相关光谱与波长相关光学模糊，可作为互补证据。

Method: 流水线式组合：基于非负矩阵分解(NMF)的材质分组、基于散度的光学线索、图拉普拉斯传播；传播后的分数用于区域提取与包围盒定位，推理阶段无需额外深度传感器。

Result: 使用两套相机系统采集30个室内与6个室外高光谱场景，给出跨材质、深度排列与部分遮挡的定性示例；材质信息在光学线索弱时辅助分离，光学线索可区分材质相似的部分表面；材质与深度均相同的物体仍难处理。

Conclusion: 该文确立传感原理与数学表述，属探索性研究，尚未建立定量评测与实现细节，系统检测性能有待验证。

Abstract: Partial occlusion makes object detection difficult when visible regions have weak texture or similar appearance. Hyperspectral imaging provides material-dependent spectra and wavelength-dependent optical blur, which offer complementary evidence for separating objects. We investigate a pipeline that combines nonnegative-matrix-factorization-based material grouping, divergence-based optical cues, and graph Laplacian propagation. The propagated scores support region extraction and bounding-box localization without requiring a separate depth sensor at inference. A collection of 30 indoor and six outdoor hyperspectral scenes acquired using two camera systems provides qualitative examples spanning different materials, depth arrangements, and partial occlusions. The examples illustrate how material information can support separation when optical cues are weak, and how optical cues can distinguish some surfaces with similar material properties. Objects sharing both material and depth remain challenging. This exploratory study establishes the sensing rationale and mathematical formulation, while identifying the quantitative evaluation and implementation details needed to assess detection performance systematically.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Multi-Stage Pipelines: A Label-Correlation Ensemble for Multi-Label Retinal Fundus Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23747)
*Uddin, Md Asif,Ferdous, Jannatul,Islam, Asif,Zisan, Zobaer Mahmud,Ghosh, Promit,Arman, Shah Ahafik,Farhan, Niloy,Rahman, Rafeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在统一39类眼底病变标签空间上，用同一Swin-V2-B骨干与训练流程受控比较三种架构：三级门控级联、39路独立sigmoid扁平模型、以及用39个可学习label query加两层Transformer解码器的标签相关性模型。结果发现分阶段级联无稳定优势，标签相关性建模增益最大，扁平模型与标签相关性模型在四视图TTA下融合为最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼底图像常多病共存，公开数据集异质且长尾；临床分诊暗示"筛查门—病组路由—组内分类器"的分阶段结构，但这种层级是否值得其复杂度尚不清楚，且缺少在统一标签空间下、控制骨干与训练协议的公平比较。

Method: 合并RFMiD 1.0、RFMiD 2.0与ODIR-5K共12,652张图像构建39目标统一标签空间；三模型共享Swin-V2-B骨干、预处理与训练协议：(i) 8模型三级门控级联；(ii) 39路独立sigmoid扁平头；(iii) 以39个可学习标签查询加两层Transformer解码器替换线性头的标签相关性模型。另做误差溯源（逐实例追踪漏检发生在哪一级）、四视图TTA与集成分析、以及按来源的负采样消偏。

Result: 级联中两个粗粒度门丢掉34.2%的真实例，细粒度分类器仅丢6.7%；门的硬零输出将级联macro-AUC压至0.747。标签相关性建模带来最大单项增益，检出率0.621→0.682（TTA后0.689）。扁平+标签相关性融合配四视图TTA在8项指标中6项最优（exact match 0.694、micro-F1 0.678、macro-F1 0.456、macro-AUC 0.972）。定义对齐的子集上macro/micro-F1达到或超过4项近期工作，低样本病种上优势最明显。此外，级联与连续输出模型集成有害；需按来源负采样以消除导致AUC虚高至1.000的数据集捷径。

Conclusion: 分阶段门控层级并未换来稳定收益，硬门控是主要误差来源；用标签相关性解码器建模多标签依赖更有效，且与扁平模型互补；集成应限于连续输出模型之间，并须处理跨数据集捷径偏差。

Abstract: Retinal fundus images frequently carry more than one disease, and public fundus datasets are heterogeneous and severely long-tailed. Clinical triage suggests a staged design, in which a screening gate, a disease-group router and group-specific classifiers operate in sequence, but it is unclear whether this hierarchy earns its complexity. We present a controlled comparison of three architectures on a unified 39-target label space built from RFMiD 1.0, RFMiD 2.0 and ODIR-5K (12,652 images): (i) a three-stage gated cascade of eight models, (ii) a flat model with 39 independent sigmoid outputs, and (iii) a label-correlation model that replaces the flat linear head with 39 learned label queries and a two-layer transformer decoder. All three share one Swin-V2-B backbone, one preprocessing pipeline and one training protocol, so differences are attributable to the architecture. Staging offers no consistent advantage: tracing every missed disease instance to the stage that lost it shows that the two coarse gates discard 34.2% of true instances while the fine-grained classifiers lose only 6.7%, and the gates' hard zeros depress the cascade's macro-AUC to 0.747. Label-correlation modeling gives the largest single gain, raising the detection rate from 0.621 to 0.682 (0.689 with test-time augmentation). Blending the flat and label-correlation models under four-view test-time augmentation is the best configuration on six of eight metrics (exact match 0.694, micro-F1 0.678, macro-F1 0.456, macro-AUC 0.972). On definition-matched class subsets it matches or exceeds four recent studies on macro- and micro-F1, most clearly on low-support pathologies. We also show that ensembling a gated cascade with continuous-output models is harmful, and that per-source negative sampling is needed to remove a dataset shortcut that otherwise yields a spurious AUC of 1.000.

</details>


### [100] [MTEdit: Packed Trajectories Beat Rollouts for Multi-Turn Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23751)
*Chen, Houyuan,Wang, Fanzhou,Fan, Xiangyu,Yang, Songlin,Yang, Zhitao,Zhang, Lvmin,Yang, Pei,Zhao, Hao,Yang, Lei,Rao, Anyi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTEdit多轮图像编辑数据集，通过packed trajectory canvas（PTC）一次性在统一视觉上下文中生成多个编辑状态，缓解顺序rollout的误差累积与视觉质量漂移；含500K轨迹（≤8轮，均值5.38），并配套MTEdit-Bench评测基准，微调Qwen-Image-Edit-Causal得到Qwen-Image-MTEdit，在开源模型中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮图像编辑更契合人机协同创作，但高质量多轮训练数据难以构建：传统顺序rollout会跨轮累积误差并导致视觉质量漂移。

Method: 以packed trajectory canvas（PTC）生成为核心的数据构建流水线：在统一视觉上下文内联合生成多个编辑状态，而非逐轮串行生成；覆盖step-wise与context-aware两类编辑场景；用MTEdit对Qwen-Image-Edit-Causal进行微调。

Result: MTEdit含500K条多轮轨迹（最多8轮，平均5.38轮），为规模最大的多轮编辑数据集之一；MTEdit-Bench从指令遵循、内容一致性、抗质量漂移、历史上下文利用四维评估；Qwen-Image-MTEdit在参与评测的开源模型中取得最优。

Conclusion: 一次性联合生成多状态的数据构建方式可有效抑制多轮质量漂移，并显著提升多轮图像编辑模型能力。

Abstract: Multi-turn image editing aims to modify an image according to a sequence of instructions, making it better aligned with human-AI collaborative creation than single-turn editing. However, constructing high-quality multi-turn training data remains challenging, as conventional sequential rollout accumulates errors and introduces visual-quality drift across editing turns. To address this challenge, we introduce MTEdit, a multi-turn image editing dataset built through a pipeline centered on packed trajectory canvas (PTC) generation. By jointly generating multiple editing states within a unified visual context, this pipeline reduces visual-quality drift across turns. Covering both step-wise and context-aware editing scenarios, MTEdit comprises 500K multi-turn image editing trajectories, with up to 8 turns and an average of 5.38 turns per trajectory, making it one of the most extensive datasets for multi-turn image editing. We further introduce MTEdit-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate both scenarios by assessing models' ability to follow editing instructions, maintain content consistency, resist visual-quality drift, and effectively use historical context across turns. Using MTEdit, we fine-tune Qwen-Image-Edit-Causal to obtain Qwen-Image-MTEdit. On MTEdit-Bench, we evaluate a broad range of closed-source and open-source image editing models, including Qwen-Image-MTEdit. Experimental results show that Qwen-Image-MTEdit achieves the best performance among the evaluated open-source models, demonstrating the effectiveness of MTEdit in improving models' multi-turn image editing capabilities.

</details>


### [101] [A Paired RGB-Sonar Dataset for Fish Monitoring with Consumer Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23759)
*Derbedrosian, Mourad,Hobley, Michael,Perona, Pietro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 推出 HaCKRS 数据集：将消费级前视声呐（Garmin LiveScope）记录与俯视 RGB 视频时间对齐，提供 10.5 万+ 帧对、约 3.3 小时、4 种量程/朝向配置，含逐帧元数据、声呐几何与坐标、同步数据及三种水平配置的平面配准，但不提供任务级鱼类标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态监测用成像声呐昂贵、可及性低；消费级前视声呐价格低廉但图像噪声大，缺乏配对的干净参照与标定信息，导致标注困难且自动化方法难以开发。

Method: 构建配对采集系统：同步采集 LiveScope 声呐录像与俯视 RGB 视频，覆盖四种量程/朝向配置；发布时间对齐帧对、帧级元数据、声呐几何与坐标系、同步信息，并对三种水平配置完成平面配准，使声呐帧可映射到水面平面图像。

Result: 发布 105,000+ 时间匹配帧对、约 3.3 小时记录、四配置同步与几何标定数据及三配置平面配准；数据集定位为配对观测与标定资源，而非任务标注集。

Conclusion: HaCKRS 为鱼群检测、跟踪、计数方法的标注与开发，以及消费级声呐用于生态监测的可行性研究提供基础数据平台；作者自述贡献限于数据与标定，不含具体方法或性能结果。

Abstract: Imaging sonar enables underwater observation in conditions where optical imaging is ineffective due to darkness and turbid water. However, imaging-sonar systems previously used for ecological monitoring have typically been expensive, limiting their accessibility. Consumer forward-looking sonar has the potential to make fish monitoring more accessible, but its noisy imagery makes annotation and the development of automated methods challenging. We introduce the Huntington and Caltech Koi RGB and Sonar dataset (HaCKRS), which pairs Garmin LiveScope imaging-sonar recordings with top-down RGB video of koi carp. The dataset contains more than 105,000 temporally matched frame pairs, spanning approximately 3.3 hours of recordings across four range and orientation configurations. We provide frame-wise metadata, sonar geometry and coordinate information, synchronization data for all four configurations, and planar registration for the three horizontal configurations. The release focuses on paired observations and calibration rather than task-specific fish annotations. We intend HaCKRS to support annotation and the development of fish detection, tracking, and counting methods, and investigations into the potential of consumer sonar for ecological monitoring.

</details>
