### [102] [Video Diffusion Avatars: Taming Video Diffusion Models into Controllable 3D Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23772)
*Giebenhain, Simon,Kirschstein, Tobias,Schoneveld, Liam,Davoli, Davide,Chen, Zhe,Nießner, Matthias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VDAs fine-tune a video diffusion model to render a single-image avatar with independent control over camera view, learned expression codes, and upper-body pose (via proxy-mesh renderings); a DISTRACT benchmark exposes and a disentanglement step removes the viewpoint bias of learned expression codes, enabling multi-view-consistent, photo-realistic reenactment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DMM 表情空间表达能力有限（舌头、口腔内部），而显式渲染与 3D 采集成本高；视频扩散模型自带外观、运动、物理先验，但缺少对视角、表情、姿态的精确解耦控制。

Method: 在公开多视图数据上微调 VDM；(1) 用学习式表情空间替代 3DMM 提升表达力；(2) 提出 DISTRACT 基准揭示学习表情码的强视角偏置，并通过解耦训练消除该偏置，使其可作为多视图一致驱动的控制信号；(3) 相机与上半身姿态通过 proxy-mesh 渲染联合控制，实现表情/姿态/视角三者独立可控；(4) 相机三元组（camera-triplet）训练策略提升效率。

Result: 新视角重演上优于 SOTA 单图 3D avatar：BecomingLit 上人脸 LPIPS 降低 29%、JOD 感知质量提升 66%；静态 3DGS 重建探针证实生成结果多视图一致性最佳；并产生显式渲染难以实现的物理合理发丝动力学、阴影、反射与折射效果；单张 A100 训练 34 小时。

Conclusion: 以视频扩散先验替代显式 3DMM+渲染管线，可获得更高表达力与物理真实感的单图可驱动 avatar，关键在于表情码的视角偏置解耦与代理网格联合控制；局限在于依赖多视图人脸数据、未涉及机器人/具身场景。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) trained on internet-scale data encode a rich prior on appearance, motion, and physics that is difficult to replicate with explicit 3D rendering and costly 3D data capture. We introduce Video Diffusion Avatars (VDAs), a method that creates photo-realistic, multi-view consistent avatars from a single image by fine-tuning a VDM for precise control over camera viewpoint, facial expression, and upper-body pose. Departing from 3D morphable models (3DMMs), VDAs use a learned expression space that lifts the expressiveness ceiling of 3DMMs, e.g., for the tongue and mouth interior. However, learned expression codes exhibit heavy viewpoint bias, as our DISTRACT benchmark reveals. We show that this bias can be removed through disentanglement, enabling their use as driving signals for multi-view consistent avatars. Camera and upper-body pose are controlled jointly via proxy-mesh renderings, giving independent control over expression, pose, and viewpoint. A camera-triplet training strategy makes the approach efficient: our models train for 34 hours on a single A100 using only public multi-view data. VDAs outperform state-of-the-art single-image 3D avatars on novel-view reenactment, reducing face LPIPS by 29% and improving perceptual quality (JOD) by 66% on BecomingLit. A static 3DGS reconstruction probe further confirms that our generations are the most multi-view consistent. Built on a video prior, VDAs produce effects beyond the reach of explicit renderers, including physically plausible hair dynamics, shadows, reflections, and refractions.

</details>


### [103] [The Collection of a Real-World, Large-Scale Dataset for Automated Skill Evaluation in Central Line Dressing Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23789)
*Tanin, Ummey,Hamlin, Shannan K.,Chen, Hsin-Mei,Peizhu,Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向中心静脉导管敷料更换（CLDC）技能评估的大规模视频数据集（365段注册护士录制，含17个子任务级时序标注与临床评分），并给出数据采集/标注流水线与时空深度学习基准实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLABSI是可预防的院感死亡来源，CLDC操作规范性至关重要；但现有能力评估依赖临床教育者人工观察，耗时、难扩展、一致性差、粒度粗，亟需AI辅助的客观可复现评估，而瓶颈在于缺乏大规模、临床可落地的数据集。

Method: 在Houston Methodist Hospital 2026年度能力考核中采集365段护士操作视频；设计录制配置预实验、标注协议（17个子任务时序定位+临床表现评分）与后处理流水线；用时空深度模型做基准实验验证可行性。

Result: 构建了带子任务级时序标注与临床评分的CLDC视频数据集，并通过时空模型基准实验初步验证视频技能评估的可行性。

Conclusion: 该数据集为自动化临床技能评估、反馈生成与AI辅助护理培训提供基础，属数据集/基准性质工作。

Abstract: Central line-associated bloodstream infections (CLABSIs) remain a major source of preventable morbidity and mortality in healthcare settings. Proper execution of the central line dressing change (CLDC) procedure is essential for infection prevention, yet current approaches to competency assessment rely primarily on manual ob- servation by clinical educators, making evaluation time-intensive, difficult to scale, and limited in consistency and granularity. These challenges highlight the need for AI-assisted skill evaluation methods that can support more objective, reproducible, and scalable assessment of clinical performance. However, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of broad-scale, clinically grounded datasets for developing and evaluating such methods. In this work, we present the collection process of a large-scale video dataset for CLDC skill evaluation, comprising 365 recordings of registered nurses collected during the 2026 annual competency exam at Houston Methodist Hospital. Each video is paired with subtask-level temporal annotations and clinical performance assessments spanning 17 subtasks of the procedure. We describe the data collection and curation pipeline, including pilot testing of record- ing configurations, annotation protocol design, and post-collection processing. We further establish the feasibility of video-based skill assessment through benchmark experiments with spatiotemporal deep learning models. This dataset provides a foundation for future research on automated clinical skill assessment, feedback generation, and AI-assisted training in nursing education.

</details>


### [104] [Persistent Planar Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23795)
*He, Yuze,Tan, Bin,Gao, Zelin,Shen, Yujun,Liu, Yong-Jin,Xue, Nan,Xu, Yinghao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PPMem用定向平面基元作为视频世界模型的持久3D记忆，在视频扩散Transformer上增加平面预测头与Depth Token Fusion模块，逐块联合生成RGB与平面几何并合并为可渲染深度的持久平面记忆，记忆体积比逐像素点云小三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需要长时程、跨视角一致的3D记忆，而逐像素点云记忆冗余且庞大。平面基元紧凑（每基元7参数）、几何显式，能刻画墙、地面、立面等主导结构，因此适合作为持久3D记忆表示。

Method: 在视频扩散Transformer上增加平面预测头与Depth Token Fusion (DTF)模块；每个自回归chunk同时输出RGB视频与平面几何，经合并流程形成持久平面记忆，再由渲染深度作为后续chunk的条件。同一骨干联合预测保证跨视角一致性，合并过程随生成推进自适应精化几何。

Result: DL3DV与RealEstate10K上视频质量优于先前3D-grounded世界模型，同时输出度量尺度深度、表面法向量与可导出平面几何；全场景约100K基元、5MB，较逐像素点云紧凑三个数量级；在数百帧、大视角变化下保持相干性。

Conclusion: 以平面基元为持久3D记忆是可行且高效的视频世界模型方案，兼顾紧凑性、几何显式性与长时程一致性，为可交互、可探索的生成式环境提供基础。

Abstract: Video world models that generate and explore environments at scale need a persistent 3D memory of what they have produced. Plane primitives offer a natural candidate: a compact set of oriented planes captures the dominant structural elements (e.g., walls, floors, facades) of indoor and outdoor scenes, while requiring only 7 parameters per primitive. We present PPMem, a video world model that adopts plane primitives as its persistent 3D memory. PPMem augments a video diffusion transformer with a planar prediction head and a Depth Token Fusion (DTF) module: each autoregressive chunk jointly produces RGB video and planar geometry, which merges into a persistent plane memory that conditions subsequent chunks via rendered depth. The resulting memory is three orders of magnitude more compact than per-pixel point clouds (~100K primitives, 5MB for a full scene), geometrically explicit (metric depth, normals, exportable surfaces), cross-view consistent (jointly predicted by the shared backbone), and adaptive (refined by the merging procedure as generation proceeds). On DL3DV and RealEstate10K, PPMem outperforms prior 3D-grounded world models in video quality while jointly producing metric-scale depth, surface normals, and exportable planar geometry, sustaining coherence across hundreds of frames under large viewpoint changes. Project page: https://hyzcluster.github.io/ppmem.

</details>


### [105] [StopWatch: Goal Grounding for Object-goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23841)
*Liu, Xinhao,Tian, Yunsheng,Qiu, Yuheng,Abbeel, Pieter,Chen, Xi,Shi, Guanya,Feng, Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文诊断出VLN类目标导航微调策略的主要失败原因是"目标接地"（goal grounding），并显式引入目标接地以修复该问题，在三个场景集合的六个基准上取得SOTA，同时修正了基准标注缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLM微调为导航策略已成常规做法，但成功率仍低，且不清楚这些策略缺少哪种能力。作者通过诊断发现失败主因是目标接地：策略能很好探索，却常常没注意到目标已在视野中，或无法估计其距离。

Method: 对微调VLM导航策略进行失败归因分析，识别出感知层面目标接地缺失是主导瓶颈；随后在策略中显式引入目标接地模块（判断目标是否可见并估计距离/位置），并修正现有基准中的标注缺陷以进行更可信的评估。

Result: 显式接地显著提升成功率，在三个场景集合的六个基准上达到SOTA；在干净基准上，若给予真值接地，策略几乎追平生成示范数据的专家策略，说明剩余提升空间在感知而非策略学习。

Conclusion: 目标接地是目标导航中缺失的关键一环，简单有效的显式接地方案可显著提升性能；后续研究应聚焦感知层面的接地能力。

Abstract: Pretrained VLMs bring rich world knowledge and semantic priors to object-goal navigation, and fine-tuning a VLM into a navigation policy has become a common recipe. Despite this progress, success rates remain low, and it is unclear which capability these fine-tuned policies lack. Our diagnosis identifies goal grounding as the dominant failure. These policies explore well, yet often fail to notice that the goal is already in view or to estimate how far away it is. To address this failure, we ground the goal explicitly. Explicit grounding substantially improves success and reaches state-of-the-art results on six benchmarks across three scene collections. We also correct annotation defects in these benchmarks, so that future methods are evaluated more faithfully. Our analysis on the clean benchmarks shows that, with ground-truth grounding, our policy nearly matches the expert that generated its demonstrations, leaving the headroom in perception rather than policy learning. Our work points to goal grounding as a missing piece of object-goal navigation and offers a simple and effective solution to the task.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking the design space of Vanilla Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23854)
*Mukhopadhyay, Soumik,Mai, Long,Shrivastava, Abhinav,Mahapatra, Aniruddha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MFlow: 可学习地在 x0-预测与 u-预测之间沿数据主方向自适应插值的像素空间扩散参数化；并提出 ConvRefiner 事后对抗训练卷积模块，弥补像素扩散 transformer 的高频缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散已成为规避 VAE 有损潜空间瓶颈的可行路线，JiT 表明在像素空间用 x0-预测替代 u-预测即可让朴素 DiT 直接生成图像。但该选择（x0 vs u）此前被视为固定超参，缺乏对其与数据表示、模型容量关系的系统理解，且像素扩散输出存在明显高频缺陷。

Method: 1) MFlow：在数据主方向（PCA-like 子空间）上学习一个自适应的 x0/u 预测插值系数，与扩散训练端到端联合优化，取代全局统一的参数化选择。2) ConvRefiner：冻结扩散主干，仅以对抗损失事后训练一个轻量卷积模块，用于恢复生成图像的高频统计。

Result: 实验发现参数化偏好随数据表示与模型容量系统性变化——容量增大时偏好向 u-预测迁移。在 ImageNet、UCF-101 视频及大规模文生图上取得有竞争力或更优的生成性能；ConvRefiner 以少量额外训练恢复高频统计且保持 FID。

Conclusion: 扩散参数化不应是固定超参，而应按方向、按容量自适应学习；像素扩散的残余高频缺陷可通过轻量事后对抗精修低成本修复。属于生成模型/扩散训练方法论改进，非具身或 3D 核心。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models have recently re-emerged as a compelling alternative to latent diffusion, avoiding the lossy latent bottleneck and separately trained autoencoder. JiT shows that a vanilla diffusion transformer can generate directly in pixel space by replacing the commonly used velocity prediction ($\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction) with clean image prediction ($\boldsymbol{x_0}$-prediction). In this work, we revisit this choice and introduce MFlow, an adaptive parameterization that learns to interpolate between $\boldsymbol{x_0}$- and $\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction across principal directions of the data, learned end-to-end with diffusion training. We find that the preferred parameterization varies systematically with the data representation and model capacity -- notably, increasing model capacity shifts preference toward velocity prediction. We evaluate MFlow across ImageNet, UCF-101 video, and large-scale text-to-image generation, demonstrating competitive or improved generative performance across diverse settings. In addition, we find that vanilla pixel diffusion transformers, irrespective of parameterization, retain a persistent high-frequency deficit relative to real images. To address this, we introduce ConvRefiner, a lightweight convolutional module trained post-hoc with an adversarial loss while keeping the diffusion backbone frozen. With only a small amount of additional training, ConvRefiner restores the high-frequency statistics of generated images while preserving FID. Project webpage: https://soumik-kanad.github.io/mflow-diffusion/.

</details>


### [107] [Learning to Correct Geometry in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23856)
*Lyu, Weijie,Shen, Tiancheng,Li, Jason,Wang, Yujing,Wang, Xiaoyuan,Yuan, Haobo,Zhao, Yizhou,Yang, Ming-Hsuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGC 是一个后处理式 video-to-video 几何校正模型，用合成配对数据（真实视频作 target，经轨迹自适应关键帧 + 图生视频 inpainting 生成几何失真的 source）训练，以光流为辅助线索解耦相机运动与物体运动，无需改动源生成器即可提升相机运动下的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络规模视频语料中，可见运动主要来自静态或弱运动相机拍摄的动态物体，对视角变化引起的表观变化监督稀疏，导致生成视频在相机运动下出现结构扭曲、视差不一致、平滑运镜退化为镜头切换式跳变。作者据此把该失效归因为数据问题。

Method: 构建 VG-Pairs 合成配对监督数据：以多相机运动的真实视频（含位姿标注）为几何一致 target，用预训练 image-to-video inpainting 模型配合轨迹自适应关键帧合成失真 counterpart；训练 VGC 学习从失真输入映射回一致 target，作为 post-hoc 模块不修改源生成器，并以光流作为辅助监督/线索用于分离相机运动与物体运动。

Result: 在位姿条件与提示词驱动两类基准上，VGC 对多个源生成器均提升了几何一致性；将骨干模型规模扩大后，框架可进一步走向更通用的视频精修。

Conclusion: 几何一致性不足可被有效作为数据构造问题来处理，合成配对监督 + 光流辅助的后处理校正是一种不侵入源模型的通用精修范式，并具备随骨干规模扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Generated videos often lose geometric consistency under camera motion: structures warp under viewpoint change, parallax becomes inconsistent, and smooth camera movement may collapse into shot-like transitions. We argue that this failure is largely a data problem. In web-scale video corpora, most visible motion comes from dynamic objects captured by static or weakly moving cameras, leaving only sparse supervision for viewpoint-induced appearance changes. To address this, we propose Video Geometry Corrector (VGC), a post-hoc video-to-video model trained on synthetic paired supervision. Starting from real videos with diverse camera motion and pose annotations, we construct VG-Pairs with a synthetic paired-supervision data generation pipeline. Each video clip is treated as a geometry-consistent target and paired with a distorted counterpart synthesized by a pretrained image-to-video inpainting model using trajectory-adaptive keyframes. VGC learns to map the distorted input to the geometry-consistent target without modifying the source generator, and uses optical flow as an auxiliary cue to help disentangle camera motion from object motion. Experiments on pose-conditioned and prompt-driven benchmarks show that VGC improves geometric consistency across multiple source generators, and scaling to a larger backbone further extends the framework toward more general video refinement.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [An Agentic ENF Framework for Forensic Video Authentication Against Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22440)
*Wyton, Ryan,Rehman, Bacha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于电网频率（ENF）的音视频统一双通道取证框架：用共享谐波估计器与网络从音频与卷帘视频中提取ENF，以Ornstein-Uhlenbeck漂移模型合成注入训练缓解标注稀缺，并通过参考匹配与两轴agentic级联编排实现录制真实性与内容篡改的双重验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频已难与真实拍摄区分，依赖生成器伪影的学习型检测器在未见模型上泛化性差；需要寻找生成过程无法控制的物理侧信道（ENF音调嗡鸣与视频闪烁）作为判别依据。

Method: 1) 统一双通道检测器：单一二阶谐波估计器+共享网络，从音频和卷帘快门视频中提取ENF；2) 合成注入训练：以Ornstein-Uhlenbeck漂移模型生成ENF信号并锚定真实录音，解决标注ENF数据稀缺；3) 参考匹配通道：验证声称的录制时间并可恢复未知时间；4) 两轴agentic编排器：在非对称规则下融合录制真实性与内容篡改证据，仅在必要时升级调用昂贵检测器。

Result: 关键发现：生成视频中同样存在ENF频段闪烁且显著性与真实素材无法区分，故"存在性"不能作为真实性证明，认证需依赖与独立参考的一致性。在留出真实录音上，对真实音频/视频的接受率为93%/100%（朴素基线53%/15%）；声称时间认证AUC达1.0；agentic级联在无精度损失下匹配平坦集成，墙钟时间减少54%、解码帧数减少64%。

Conclusion: 将视频鉴伪从"伪影检测"重构为"与独立物理参考的一致性验证"，并提出可行的高效级联取证系统；局限性在于依赖录制时段的ENF参考数据库可得性。

Abstract: Generative models now synthesise video that is hard to distinguish from genuine footage, and learned detectors that key on a generator's artefacts degrade sharply on unseen models. This paper develops a forensic framework that authenticates video by a signal that generation does not control: the electric network frequency (ENF), the mains-driven hum in audio and flicker in video. A unified two-channel detector extracts the ENF from audio and from rolling-shutter video through one second harmonic estimator and a shared network; a synthetic-injection training method, driven by an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck drift model and anchored to real recordings, overcomes the scarcity of labelled ENF data; a reference-matching channel verifies a claimed time of recording and recovers an unknown one; and a two-axis agentic orchestrator fuses recording authenticity with content-manipulation evidence under an asymmetric rule, escalating expensive detectors only when needed. A central finding reframes the task: an ENF-band flicker is present in generated video at a prominence indistinguishable from real footage, so its presence is not proof of authenticity, and authentication must rest on consistency with an independent reference rather than on presence. On held-out real recordings the detector accepts genuine audio and video at 93% and 100% against naive baselines of 53% and 15%; claimed-time authentication reaches an area under the curve of 1.0; and the agentic cascade matches a flat ensemble while cutting wall-clock time by 54% and frames decoded by 64%, without loss of accuracy.

</details>


### [109] [Erase the Mark, Preserve the Motion: Temporally Consistent Removal of Video Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22457)
*Liao, Yifan,Sun, Zhen,Huang, Zhicong,He, Jiaming,Zhang, Zongmin,Ai, Xi,Hong, Cheng,Zheng, Xinhu,He, Xinlei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PoseRegen：用"时序共享视角变换扰动 + 位姿条件视频DiT联合重建"去除视频水印，在11种水印方案上把平均比特准确率压到0.505（接近随机），且运动一致性优于基线9.34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成普及带来版权保护需求，但视频水印对抗去除攻击的鲁棒性研究不足。图像域的两类去除方法迁移到视频均有缺陷：信号优化注入的像素扰动在时间维上不一致，产生可见闪烁；扩散重合成逐帧生成细粒度结构不一致，造成结构-运动不一致。因此需要一个既能去水印又能保持内容与运动的重建式攻击框架。

Method: 1) 对整段视频施加时序共享的视角/位姿变换，打乱承载水印的空间模式；2) 冻结的视频DiT以参考视频latent处理整条序列，并用全序列Flow Matching监督，保证身份与背景跨帧一致；3) 引入轻量级 Pose-Conditioned Motion Consistency Adapter，使冻结主干适配按时间排序的位姿引导，并在重建过程中约束身体部位轨迹以保持运动结构。

Result: 在11种视频水印方案上，平均比特准确率降至0.505，接近随机猜测；在VBench运动相关指标上比最强基线高9.34%。

Conclusion: 当前视频水印存在实际可被去除的脆弱性，水印鲁棒性评测应同时考察时间与运动质量，而非仅报比特准确率。该工作本身属于水印攻防/内容保护方向，对人形视频的位姿条件序列重建有一定借鉴意义，但对具身智能/3D重建主线价值有限。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have increased the demand for reliable watermarking for copyright protection, yet the robustness of video watermarks against removal attacks remains underexplored. Most watermark-removal methods originate from the image domain: signal optimization injects imperceptible pixel-domain perturbations, whereas generative resynthesis produces a visually similar instance through diffusion synthesis. When extended to videos, the former introduces temporally varying noise that causes visible flickering, while the latter regenerates fine-grained structures inconsistently across frames, leading to structural motion inconsistency. To evaluate video watermark robustness, we propose PoseRegen, a pose-consistent reconstruction framework that removes watermarks while preserving the original content and motion. PoseRegen first applies a temporally shared viewpoint transformation to disrupt watermark-bearing spatial patterns and then jointly reconstructs the transformed sequence with a pose-conditioned video model. A frozen video DiT processes the complete sequence with its reference-video latent, and full-sequence Flow Matching supervision keeps identity and background consistent across frames. To further preserve motion structure, we introduce a lightweight Pose-Conditioned Motion Consistency Adapter that adapts the frozen backbone to temporally ordered pose guidance and constrains body-part trajectories throughout reconstruction. Experiments on eleven video watermarking schemes show that PoseRegen reduces the average bit accuracy to 0.505, near random guessing, while outperforming the strongest baseline by 9.34% on motion-related VBench metrics. These results reveal a practical vulnerability of current video watermarks and highlight the need to evaluate robustness jointly with temporal and motion quality.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Is a Linear Probe Evidence of a Linear Representation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22655)
*Kutluozen, Nadir,Internò, Christian,Klindt, David*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文指出视觉模型的"线性表征假说"常用验证方法（线性探针高准确率）过于宽松：任何足够丰富的特征映射（包括随机特征）都能获得线性读出。作者提出三个非对称诊断（反向可预测性、谱集中度、OOD泛化）来区分"真正的线性世界模型"与"核机器式曲线流形"，并在仿真与六个预训练ViT上验证：预训练确实产生了超出随机核的结构，但远未达到线性世界模型的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表征假说认为预训练视觉模型把生成因子编码为特征空间中的线性可解码方向，主流方法只是训练线性探针、以高准确率判定"已表征"。作者认为该检验过于宽松：理论上渐近地任何足够丰富的特征映射（含随机映射）都能做到线性读出，因此高探针准确率同时兼容两种截然不同的几何结构——潜在对齐子空间主导的真正线性世界模型，或潜在因子仅嵌于更大弯曲流形中的核机器。二者差异对分布外泛化、鲁棒性与组合迁移至关重要，现有评测却无法区分。

Method: 提出三个非对称诊断指标以区分两种几何：(1) 反向可预测性（reverse predictivity，从特征预测因子的逆向可解性）；(2) 谱集中度（spectral concentration，检查潜在对齐子空间是否主导表征能量）；(3) 分布外泛化（OOD generalization）。在一个完全可观测、ground truth 已知的模拟环境中先做验证，再应用于六个预训练视觉 Transformer；使用自建的 SVG-World 确定性生成基准（成对视觉风格），并在 CelebA 上测试其中两个模型；另在 dSprites、3D Shapes、MPI3D 上做交叉分析。

Result: 模拟实验验证了三项诊断的有效性。在 SVG-World 与 CelebA 上，预训练模型的诊断结果显示其表征结构优于随机核，但明显弱于线性世界模型；在 dSprites、3D Shapes、MPI3D 上，前向可解码性与潜在对齐方差出现解离（dissociation），说明前向解码精度高并不代表被探测因子与表征的主导几何对齐。

Conclusion: 仅凭前向线性探针精度不足以证明因子被"线性表征"；作者提醒应警惕将线性探针的成功等同于真正线性且低维的表征，应结合反向可预测性、谱集中度与 OOD 泛化等更严格的几何诊断来评估表征质量。

Abstract: The linear representation hypothesis holds that pretrained vision models encode generative factors as linearly decodable directions in feature space, and comes with a standard methodology: train a linear probe, declare a factor represented if accuracy is high. We show this test is too permissive. Asymptotically, any sufficiently rich feature map, including a random one, admits a linear readout, so high probe accuracy is consistent with two very different geometries: a true linear world model, where the latent-aligned subspace dominates the representation, or a kernel machine, where the latents lie linearly inside a much larger curved manifold. This distinction matters because the curved structure orthogonal to the probe is relevant for out-of-distribution generalization, robustness, and compositional transfer. We propose three asymmetric diagnostics that distinguish the two cases: reverse predictivity, spectral concentration, and out-of-distribution generalization. We validate them in a fully observed simulation where ground truth is known. Applied to six pretrained vision transformers on SVG-World, our deterministic generative benchmark with paired visual styles, and to two of them on CelebA, they show pretraining produces structure beyond a random kernel but well short of a linear world model. On dSprites, 3D Shapes and MPI3D, forward decodability and latent-aligned variance dissociate. Thus, forward decoding alone does not show that the probed factors align with the dominant geometry of the representation. In summary, our work cautions about the success of linear probes as opposed to truly linear and low dimensional representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [ECHOES: Seeing Beyond the Current Image via Recursive History Compression and Privileged On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22408)
*Guo, Tengda,Hu, Jiawang,Tao, Yifei,Wang, Yin,Hu, Haotian,Wang, Fanyi,Tang, Liujuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ECHOES 用训练时特权信息（多图）蒸馏来引导文本历史压缩，提出 CTA 格式与 PMF-Gap 引导的 OPD-RL 框架，部署时无需历史截图即可维持长程 GUI 任务状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 GUI 智能体依赖视觉历史维持任务状态，但保留历史截图会显著增加视觉 token 与推理成本；纯文本摘要又会丢失关键状态信息，损害长程可靠性。因此需要在不牺牲状态保真度的前提下压缩历史。

Method: 1) Compression-Think-Action (CTA) 格式，递归更新文本历史状态并推理；2) 同一模型在多图特权输入下作为 teacher，对学生生成的 CTA rollout 做密集 token 级 on-policy distillation (OPD)；3) Perception-Memory Fidelity (PMF) Gap 引导的 OPD-RL 框架，动态平衡特权蒸馏与任务奖励优化，并周期性更新 teacher；4) 构建 ECHOESBMK 细粒度诊断基准，源自真实 GUI agent 失败轨迹，联合评测视觉感知与跨模态状态保持。

Result: 在四个 GUI 基准与 ECHOESBMK 上任务性能与记忆保真度均有显著提升；部署时不保留历史截图仍具竞争力，取得良好的性能—推理成本权衡，并对未见视觉规划任务呈现正向零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 训练期特权信息蒸馏 + 自适应 RL 平衡是压缩智能体历史状态的有效范式，可在去掉部署期视觉历史的同时保持长程可靠性；零样本迁移表明该记忆压缩具备一定泛化性。

Abstract: Long-horizon GUI agents rely on visual history to maintain task states, but retaining historical screenshots increases visual-token usage and inference costs, while textual summaries can discard critical state information, undermining long-horizon reliability. We propose ECHOES, which leverages training-time privileged information to guide textual history compression and strengthen long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, ECHOES adopts a Compression-Think-Action (CTA) format to recursively update a textual history state and reason. The same model, given privileged multi-image inputs, serves as a teacher that provides dense token-level on-policy distillation on student-generated CTA rollouts. Our Perception-Memory Fidelity (PMF) Gap-guided OPD-RL framework dynamically balances privileged distillation and task-reward optimization, with periodic teacher updates supporting continued improvement. We further introduce ECHOESBMK, a fine-grained diagnostic benchmark derived from real GUI agent failure trajectories to jointly evaluate visual perception and cross-modal state retention. Experiments on four GUI benchmarks and ECHOESBMK demonstrate substantial gains in task performance and memory fidelity. Without retaining historical screenshots at deployment, ECHOES achieves competitive performance with a favorable performance-inference-cost trade-off and exhibits positive zero-shot transfer to unseen visual planning tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Self-Building Labs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23826)
*Bai, Jiaru,Zhao, Yuchi,Hao, Han,Bogdanovic, Miroslav,Cao, Yang,Bernales, Varinia,Aspuru-Guzik, Alán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“自建实验室”(SBL)：给定高层科学目标，系统能自主设计实验室布局、构建数字孪生、生成机器人技能与实验流程，并跨不同机器人与多机器人设置运行，最终完成真实量子点合成实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体已能提出新实验，但现有自驱动实验室(SDL)只能执行其被建造时预设的流程，适配新实验需大量人工工程，实验室柔性成为科学发现规模化的瓶颈。

Method: SBL 以既有条件（空房间到已运行 SDL）为起点，自动补全缺失能力：实验室布局设计、数字孪生构建、机器人技能合成、实验工作流编排；在多种机器人与多机器人协同场景中验证。

Result: 系统在不同机器人、不同初始设置上运行成功，可从单工作站扩展到多机器人协同实验室；以真实世界量子点合成实验验证端到端能力。

Conclusion: 将人类研究者角色从手工配置与编程设备自动化转变为指定科学目标，使科学自动化更具可达性、柔性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) accelerate scientific discovery by automating experimental execution and optimization. AI agents can increasingly propose new experiments, but existing SDLs can only run the procedures they were built for. Adapting them to a new experiment still takes extensive manual engineering, which makes laboratory flexibility the bottleneck for scaling discovery. We introduce the self-building laboratory (SBL), which designs, configures, and operates its own laboratory setup given a high-level scientific goal. SBL starts from whatever already exists, from an empty room to a working SDL, and builds the missing capabilities: the lab layout, its digital twin, robot skills, and experimental workflows. We demonstrate the system working across different robots and setups, scaling from a single workstation to laboratories with several robots working together. We validate SBL's end-to-end capabilities through a real-world quantum-dot synthesis experiment. By shifting the role of human researchers from manually configuring and programming device automation to specifying scientific objectives, SBL makes scientific automation more accessible, flexible, and scalable. Project website: https://self-building-labs.github.io/

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Alignment of visual brain encoding models with individual perceptual similarity judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22569)
*Chong, Daniel,Chen, Haomiao,Jamison, Keith W,Marjieh, Raja,Sucholutsky, Ilia,Jacoby, Nori,Kuceyeski, Amy*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该工作用NSD数据对脑编码模型做微调，使其内部表征对齐被试个性化感知相似性判断，发现个体化相似性微调能同时对齐人类判断与个体间相关结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脑编码模型以预测观测fMRI为唯一目标，其内部表征未必反映个体人类的感知结构；作者希望检验能否将人类相似性判断数据"引导"进编码模型。

Method: 基于NSD中8名被试对100张图像的相似性判断，先用DreamSim为每位被试适配个性化感知相似度模型；再据此为各被试训练集构造被试特异的相似性结构；在相同初始化与目标下，将基线编码器分别对齐到通用（群体）相似性与个性化相似性进行微调。

Result: 基线编码器对个体内感知相似性的反映强于实测fMRI，并保留了实测fMRI的个体间相关；但对齐通用相似性后仍无法复现人类判断的个体间相关；对齐个性化相似性后与人类判断最一致，并能复现其个体间相关结构。

Conclusion: 将人类相似性判断数据注入编码模型的训练目标，可得到更具个体化、更贴近人类感知的视觉编码模型。

Abstract: Encoding models that predict brain activation responses to images have traditionally been trained to maximize accuracy when predicting observed brain data. However, their internal representations may not reflect individual human perception. In this work, we investigate whether we can fine-tune brain encoding models to better align their representations with individuals' similarity judgments. Using similarity judgments of 100 images from 8 participants in the Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD), we adapt a generic human similarity judgment model (DreamSim) separately for each participant to obtain personalized perceptual similarity models. These models are then used to obtain subject-specific similarity structure across images in their encoding model training sets. We fine-tune each participant's baseline encoder in two conditions, aligning its representations with either generic or personalized similarity judgments, with the same initialization and objective. We find that baseline brain encoders reflect within-subject perceptual similarity more strongly than measured fMRI responses and preserve intersubject correlations present in measured fMRI, but fail to reflect those of human perceptual similarity judgments. Fine-tuning with generic, population-level perceptual similarity information results in better alignment with human judgments than baseline encoders, but these models still fail to capture intersubject correlations of human judgments. However, fine-tuning with personalized perceptual similarity information results in models that are the most aligned with human perceptual similarity judgments and also reflect intersubject correlations of human judgments. Our framework provides an approach for bootstrapping human similarity judgment data into more individualized, human-aligned brain encoding models of vision.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [CF-NODE: Counterfactual Spatial Node Intervention for Test-Time Adaptation Under Domain Shift, Evaluated Across Five Diabetic Retinopathy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22437)
*G, Sabarish,Parashar, Deepak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CF-NODE: 冻结模型的反事实测试时自适应框架，通过逐步抑制高注意力空间节点并观察期望等级响应的稳定性来校准DR分级预测，无需批统计或模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼底DR自动分级可扩展筛查能力，但源域模型在跨相机、跨诊所、跨人群时性能骤降；现有TTA依赖熵最小化或批量/流式目标信息，易强化"自信错误"且在单张独立到达的临床图像上失效。

Method: 以模型注意力、特征幅值、节点集相似度对内部空间节点排序，渐进式衰减最高排名节点，用期望等级响应的稳定性对预测等级分布做有界校准。全冻结、逐图独立自适应，仅用几何视图与固定源域先验，无目标信息、无参数更新。

Result: 在五个外部DR数据集上重复种子评测，相对TENT、EATA、SAR、T3A均取得统计显著提升；每个目标域AUC均有提高，平均AUC与macro-F1最高，严重度各级识别更均衡。

Conclusion: 反事实干预敏感性可作为单图临床推理的一致、可部署的域鲁棒性信号；但其价值主要限于医学影像域偏移场景，缺乏可迁移到通用视觉/具身任务的机制洞察。

Abstract: Automated fundus-based diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading can substantially expand screening capacity, but source-trained models degrade sharply across different cameras, clinics, and patient populations. Existing test-time adaptation methods depend on entropy minimization or target information accumulated from image batches and streams. These mechanisms can reinforce confidently incorrect predictions and break down when clinical images arrive independently. We introduce CF-NODE, a novel counterfactual test-time adaptation framework for cross- domain DR grading. Instead of trusting prediction confidence or estimating a target distribution, CF-NODE asks how each prediction responds under a controlled intervention on the evidence supporting it. CF-NODE ranks internal spatial nodes using model-derived attention, feature magnitude, and node-set similarity, progressively attenuates the highest-ranked nodes, and uses the stability of the resulting expected-grade response to apply a bounded calibration of the predicted grade distribution. The framework is fully frozen and adapts every image independently using only geometric views and a fixed source-derived prior, requiring no target-derived information or model updates. We evaluate CF-NODE on five external DR datasets under repeated-seed evaluation against TENT, EATA, SAR, and T3A. CF-NODE outperforms all four methods with statistically significant improvements over the second-best method, improves AUC on every target domain, and attains the highest average AUC and macro-F1, delivering stronger grade discrimination together with more balanced recognition across DR severity levels. These results demonstrate that counterfactual intervention sensitivity provides consistent and deployment-ready domain robustness for single-image clinical inference.

</details>


### [115] [Domain-specific language alignment of multimodal embeddings towards explainable predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23110)
*Tijūnaitytė, Gabrielė,van der Plas, Thijs L.,Belaid, Mohamed-Bachir,Knapen, Rob,Soja, Maciej J.,Athanasiadis, Ioannis N.*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个可对任意嵌入空间进行语言对齐的框架：用辅助数据生成部分字幕，用依赖属性相似度的软对比损失训练，并用基于排名的概念检索评估；在地球多模态嵌入（AlphaEarth）与物种分布建模上验证可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合得到的潜在表示虽提升下游性能但不可解释，限制其在需可解释决策领域的应用；图像-文本对齐需大量成对数据，而嵌入本身不可直接观测标注，因此缺乏配字幕数据。

Method: 三项设计：(1) 字幕函数将可解释的辅助数据转换为部分字幕（partial captions）；(2) 软对比损失按每对样本在字幕所描述属性上的相似度加权；(3) 基于排名的概念检索任务，以辅助数据而非字幕为参照评估对齐质量。应用于地球观测多模态嵌入与 S2BMS、SatBird 物种分布建模。

Result: AlphaEarth 嵌入在 6/7 指标上超过此前 SOTA；该框架将领域特定概念与嵌入空间语言对齐，在两数据集平均概念检索上优于先前地球观测视觉-语言模型；对齐后概念可恢复物种-生境关联（ρ=0.44），并将生境描述与物种预测相联系。

Conclusion: 表明抽象潜在嵌入可被领域专家语言"审问"，为无成对字幕的多模态嵌入提供通用语言对齐与可解释性路径；但其证据限于遥感/生态领域，无机器人或物理AI相关验证。

Abstract: Multimodal data are commonly fused into latent representations that improve downstream task performance but are not interpretable, limiting their application in domains that require explainable model decisions. Vision-language alignment has made image embeddings interpretable through language, but requires vast image-caption training data. However, captioned data are typically not available for multimodal embeddings, as relevant features cannot directly be observed and annotated from embeddings alone. Here, we propose a framework to language-align any embedding space that can be linked to interpretable auxiliary data. Captioning functions convert auxiliary data into partial captions; a soft contrastive loss weights pairs by the similarity of their properties that each caption describes; and a rank-based concept retrieval task evaluates alignment against the auxiliary data rather than the captions. We apply this framework to multimodal Earth embeddings to facilitate explainable predictions for two species distribution modelling tasks: S2BMS and SatBird. AlphaEarth embeddings beat previous state-of-the-art models on 6/7 metrics, while our framework language-aligns domain-specific concepts of the embedding space, outperforming previous Earth observation vision-language models on average concept retrieval for both datasets. Finally, aligned concepts recover species-habitat associations ($\rho$=0.44) and relate habitat descriptions to species predictions, showing that abstract embeddings can be interrogated in the language of domain experts.

</details>


### [116] [Structural Affinity: An Algorithm for Coherent Cage-Based Shape Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23173)
*Newatia, Aviraj,Yang, Shengan,Zhong, Hantao,Antonova, Rika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Structural Affinity 显式正则化，用 mean-value coordinates 预计算内外两层 cage 之间的亲和矩阵，把 cage handle 位移耦合到邻居，使 CBD 变形更平滑、少自交、低畸变，并在机器人协同设计、神经变形迁移和翼型优化上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 cage-based deformation (CBD) 的优化多在目标任务上叠加软正则损失（如平滑/结构损失）来抑制网格畸变，这类损失与任务目标相互竞争，且不改变变形参数化本身；作者希望从参数化层面显式约束 cage 变形。

Method: 引入两层 cage 结构（外层粗 cage + 内层控制 cage），用 mean-value coordinates 预计算亲和矩阵 A，把内层 handle 位移映射为经外层平滑的位移（v' = A v），从而直接耦合相邻 handle 运动；该矩阵仅离线计算一次，变形时仅增加线性开销。

Result: 在机器人控制-形态协同设计、source-to-target 神经变形迁移、机翼气动外形优化三个任务上，产生更规则的变形并显著减少对手工结构损失项的依赖；在 cotangent Laplacian（表面光滑度代理）、cage distortion（handle 运动一致性）、surface roughness（机翼几何抖动）三项指标上均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过将正则化内嵌进 CBD 的变形参数化（而非作为软损失项），Structural Affinity 以低成本实现了更稳定、更可控的 cage 变形，为下游优化与设计任务提供可替代结构损失的显式机制。

Abstract: Cage-based deformation (CBD) parametrises 3D geometry through a small set of control handles, effectively enabling a variety of tasks from animation and interactive modelling to tool-based co-design, neural deformation transfer, and aerodynamic shape optimization. Many optimization approaches relying on CBD employ regularisation strategies to reduce the distortion of the deformed mesh by encouraging smooth, regular cage deformations. These techniques offer soft guidance to an optimization algorithm towards desirable deformations, but compete with the task objective rather than changing the deformation parameterization itself. In this work we introduce Structural Affinity for CBD, an explicit regularisation method which enables smooth deformations through control cages by exploiting a two-cage structure to couple cage-handle motion directly. Structural Affinity precomputes an affinity matrix using mean-value coordinates, binding the inner control-cage to a coarser outer-cage. This matrix defines how much a cage handle displacement propagates to neighbouring handles which substantially decreases and often eliminates cage self-intersections, reduces mesh distortion, and adds linear overhead at deformation time. Across robotic control--morphology co-design, source-to-target neural deformation transfer, and aerodynamic wing shape optimization, Structural Affinity produces more regular deformations and substantially reduces reliance on hand-designed structural loss terms. We report consistent gains on three complementary diagnostics: the cotangent Laplacian, a proxy for surface smoothness; cage distortion, capturing whether control handles move coherently; and surface roughness, which measures undesirable oscillation in wing geometry.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [The Optimal Ranking Tradeoffs Between Two Ranking Scores in Two-Class Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23294)
*Piérard, Sébastien,Deliège, Adrien,Van Droogenbroeck, Marc*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 论文提出"ranking scores"统一框架：所有常用分类评价指标（accuracy、TPR、TNR、PPV、NPV、IoU、F-score、kappa、balanced accuracy）都是对TN/FP/FN/TP四类结果赋予相对重要性权重的特例；并在类先验相同时给出精确解析解，求出任意两项初始指标之间关于Kendall距离Fréchet方差最优的权衡排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发机器学习方法时人们希望同时最大化多个常用指标，但这些指标会给出不同的方法排名，存在"该选哪个排名"的困境。若有多个合理排名，需要在其构成流形上找到一个"最优折中"排名，使与各初始排名的Kendall距离的Fréchet方差最小（即位于测地线中心），但该折中点一般不等于直接平均重要性权重。

Method: 将各指标统一为以TN/FP/FN/TP的相对重要性值为参数的四维权重空间中的ranking score连续统；将每个分数在每个分类器集合上诱导的排名视为流形上的点，最优折中定义为使Kendall（bubble-sort）距离Fréchet方差最小的点，即测地路径中心；对任意两项初始分数，在各类先验相同的常见情形下推导最优折中分数权重的精确解析解；并实验考察在固定先验下性能服从若干分布时折中排名的行为。

Result: 给出了类先验相同时任意初始分数对的最优折中Ranking Score的精确闭式解析解，指出可通过计算四类混淆结果重要性参数的最优组合得到，而非简单平均；实验分析了在固定先验、性能服从特定分布时相关指标与折中排名的关系。

Conclusion: 评价指标排名之间的折中可以形式化、并解析求解，为在多个合理指标之间做原则性排名选择提供了理论基础与实用工具；但结论限于类先验相同或固定的场景。

Abstract: The accuracy, true negative rate (negative class recall), true positive rate (positive class recall), negative predictive value (negative class precision), positive predictive value (positive class precision), class-specific intersections over unions, class-specific F-scores, Cohen's kappa, and balanced accuracy are scores that one intuitively wants to maximize when developing machine learning methods. However, they lead to different rankings of such methods. So, how can we establish a ranking that is an optimal tradeoff between any pair of these scores? It turns out that all these scores are special cases of ranking scores: a continuum of scores parameterized by the relative importance values assigned to the true negative, false positive, false negative, and true positive outcomes. Since all these scores are known to induce meaningful rankings, we seek the optimal tradeoffs among them. We consider the tradeoff between two scores to be optimal when it minimizes the Fr\'echet variance regarding the Kendall distance (also known as the bubble-sort distance) between rankings. For any given set of classifiers to rank, the optimal tradeoff lies at the center of the shortest (geodesic) path on the manifold of all rankings induced by the ranking scores. However, in the parameter space of the ranking scores, it is rarely achieved by averaging the importance values corresponding to the initial scores. In this paper, we present an exact analytical solution for determining a ranking score that is an optimal tradeoff for any given pair of initial scores, in the common case where the class priors are the same for all compared performances. We also experimentally investigate what happens when performance follows some distributions with fixed priors.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Online Constraint Optimization-Based Indirect-Adaptive Robust Control for Autonomous Vehicles with Unknown Friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22675)
*Zhou, Yupeng,Chang, I-Chia,Wang, Ziran,Yao, Bin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出双层控制框架：上层用约束最优控制生成时间最优可行轨迹，下层用间接自适应鲁棒控制（IARC）在线辨识摩擦相关参数并结合鲁棒反馈保证跟踪性能，配估计监控与平滑参数重置，参数回馈规划器实现摩擦变化下的重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦不确定性会破坏自动驾驶车辆控制器的可行性与性能，需在规划与控制之间建立参数自适应闭环。

Method: 分层架构：上层求解带约束的最优控制问题，在期望路径上生成时间最优且满足约束的轨迹；下层IARC自适应未知摩擦参数，模型补偿+鲁棒反馈保证瞬态与稳态跟踪；设计估计监控器在数据提示模型失配时抑制不良学习，并用平滑参数重置降低估计轮胎容量、避免约束骤紧；更新参数反馈至规划器模型与约束中实现轨迹重规划。

Result: 仿真结果显示在较大摩擦不确定性下可实现对期望轨迹的精确跟踪与在线摩擦自适应，同时保持时间最优运动，并提供演示代码链接。

Conclusion: 优化规划与自适应鲁棒控制的紧耦合能有效应对摩擦不确定性，但仅在仿真中验证，缺乏实车实验与复杂工况。属车辆控制领域，与具身智能/3D重建主线关联较弱。

Abstract: Friction uncertainty is a significant challenge for autonomous vehicle control, undermining feasibility and performance. This paper proposes a novel two-layer control framework to achieve vehicle motion control under large friction uncertainty by combining optimization-based planning with indirect adaptive robust control (IARC). The upper layer solves a constrained optimal control problem to generate a feasible trajectory that satisfies all the constraints in a time-optimal manner along the desired path. The lower layer, IARC, adapts the unknown parameters related to friction uncertainty and combines model compensation with robust feedback to ensure transient and steady-state tracking performance. An estimation monitor is designed to prevent poor learning when data indicate a model mismatch, while a smooth parameter reset reduces the estimated tire capacity and limits abrupt tightening of constraints. The updated parameters are fed back into the model and constraints in the planner, enabling trajectory replanning as friction conditions change. Simulation results indicate that the proposed method achieves accurate tracking of the desired trajectory and online friction adaptation under large friction uncertainty while ensuring time-optimal vehicle motion. The simulation results demos are available at https://github.com/TedZhou0603/CO-IARC.

</details>


### [119] [SparseMax-dSTL: Sound Robustness with Horizon-Independent Gradient Allocation for Temporal Logic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23837)
*Damera, Sai Sandeep,Matheu, Ryan,Baras, John S.,Belta, Calin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出基于sparsemax投影的min/max下近似，构造的STL鲁棒性既sound（非负仅在满足规范时）又在任意时域长度下对违反样本的梯度权重有正下界；在独轮车与七关节机械臂轨迹优化中优于log-sum-exp类近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键轨迹优化中用光滑近似STL定量语义（robustness）并求梯度。现有近似（log-sum-exp、其sound变体、广义均值鲁棒性）无法同时满足两点：sound性（仅满足规范时非负）与长度无关的违反样本梯度权重下界；长时域下log-sum-exp对违反样本的梯度权重趋于消失，导致优化器忽略约束。

Method: 引入最小/最大算子的下近似，其梯度恰为sparsemax投影；由此构造的鲁棒性严格低于精确鲁棒性（故sound），误差界与样本数无关且优化前已知。梯度在超过数据相关阈值的样本上精确为零，故对超出其他样本一个间隔的违反样本，其梯度权重在任意时域下均有正下界。

Result: 平面独轮车与七关节机械臂（旁有人）仿真：log-sum-exp近似在违反轨迹上仍为正；机械臂任务时域10→80 s时sound变体在违反样本上的梯度权重由0.22降至0.04，sparsemax保持为1；独轮车约束优化中log-sum-exp鲁棒性约束返回违反轨迹，sparsemax鲁棒性约束则返回满足规范且留有裕度的轨迹。

Conclusion: sparsemax型下近似可同时提供sound性与时域无关的梯度加权保证，是安全关键STL约束轨迹优化的稳健选择。

Abstract: A gradient-based solver that enforces a Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specification uses a smooth approximation of its quantitative semantics, the robustness. In safety-critical trajectory optimization, this approximation must have two properties. First, it must be sound, that is, nonnegative only on trajectories that satisfy the specification. Second, at each temporal operator, the weight of its gradient on the violating samples must stay bounded away from zero, however long the horizon. The approximations in use, including the log-sum-exp, its sound variant, and the generalized-mean robustness, do not have both. We introduce lower approximations of the minimum and the maximum whose gradients are sparsemax projections, and we prove that the robustness built from them has both. It lies below the exact robustness and is therefore sound, and its error has a bound that is independent of the number of samples and known before optimization. The gradient of each approximation is exactly zero on every sample beyond a data-dependent threshold, so the weight on violating samples a gap beyond the others has a positive lower bound at every horizon. On a planar unicycle and on a seven-joint manipulator beside a person in simulation, the log-sum-exp approximation is positive on a violating trajectory. As the horizon of the manipulator's task grows from 10 to 80 s, the gradient weight of the sound variant on the violating samples falls from 0.22 to 0.04, while that of sparsemax stays at 1. In a constrained trajectory optimization of the unicycle, the constraint on the log-sum-exp robustness returns a violating trajectory, whereas the constraint on the sparsemax robustness returns a trajectory that satisfies the specification with the required margin.

</details>

### [120] [Generative AI Produces and Transmits Beauty Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22553)
*Alvero, AJ,Dennison, Deepak Varuvel,Crespin, Alexandra,Kim, Heeyon,Regla-Vargas, Alejandra,Akerkar, Avani,McNealy, Jasmine,Kizilcec, René*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 对2022–2026年发布的27个文生图模型进行审计（51,300张生成人脸、160,196张真实照片、3种自动指标+550名人工评分），发现生成人脸普遍被评判为更具吸引力，女性面孔中37%超过职业模特中位数，深肤色群体得分偏低；预注册招聘实验（N=570）显示高吸引力AI人脸使同一组候选人的可雇佣性与可信度评价下降、面试推进率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会依据与性别、种族交织的审美标准奖励"有吸引力"的人；作者希望检验生成式AI是否以及如何放大这类社会动力学偏见。

Method: 审计27个文生图生成器，使用不含吸引力/美貌词汇的中性提示词；生成51,300张人脸并与160,196张真实照片对比；采用3种自动化度量与550名人类评分者；按性别与种族/民族/国别分层分析；并进行预注册招聘模拟实验（N=570）。

Result: 几乎所有生成器在自动指标上得分高于真实照片，23个生成器的人工吸引力评分显著高于真实照片（多重比较校正后仍成立）；37%的女性提示生成面孔超过职业时装模特中位数；深肤色相关群体得分系统性地低于浅肤色群体；在招聘模拟中，高吸引力AI人脸条件下，同一测试集候选人的可雇佣性、可信度评分下降，面试推进率降低，而修图照片无此效应。

Conclusion: 生成式AI可复现种族等级与性别化的审美模式，并改变真实个体被评判的社会语境；提示生成模型输出与社会评价体系存在系统性耦合。

Abstract: Societies reward people judged to be attractive by standards intertwined with gender and race. In this paper, we examine if and how generative AI may contribute to these dynamics. We audited 27 text-to-image generators released between 2022 and 2026 using simple prompts that never mention attractiveness or beauty, analyzing 51,300 generated faces and 160,196 photographs of real people across primary and supplementary comparisons. We used three automated measures and collected ratings from 550 human raters on a subset of images. Nearly all generators produced faces scoring above photographed faces under automated measures, and human raters judged the outputs of all 23 generators as significantly more attractive than photographed faces, even after correction for multiple comparisons. When focusing our analysis on gender, we find that 37% of female-prompted generated faces outscored the median professional fashion model. Across racial, ethnic, and national categories, groups associated with darker skin tones tended to receive lower attractiveness scores than groups associated with lighter skin tones. In a preregistered hiring simulation experiment (N = 570), exposure to AI-generated faces selected for high human-rated attractiveness lowered hireability and trustworthiness ratings and reduced interview advancement for an identical test set of candidates paired with photographs of real people; exposure to retouched photographs produced no such effect. Generative AI can reproduce racial hierarchies and gendered patterns of beauty and alter the context in which real people are judged.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [DriveWorld3D Evidence Constrained 3D World Modeling and Generative Simulation for Real-World Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22450)
*Wang, Wei,Wang, Yiru Veronika,Wang, Yihan,Li, Zihao,Liang, Xiaohui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveWorld3D 提出融合时序视频学习、多传感器测量约束与离线生成式仿真的自动驾驶三维建模框架：在线维护统一世界状态（路面、体素占据、动态实体、道路关系），并显式分离物理占据、可观测性与证据来源；离线用 NeRF/3DGS 重建录制场景并以生成模型扩展长尾条件，但禁止生成内容充当几何真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重建方法虽能合成逼真新视角，但视觉保真度不足以刻画驾驶所需的几何可靠性；生成式世界模型虽可扩展场景与预测未来，却可能引入缺乏测量支撑的内容。因此需要在有限算力下显式管理证据与不确定性。

Method: 框架含在线与离线两部分。在线：时序视频学习 + 多传感器测量约束，维护统一世界状态，分别表示物理占据、可观测性与证据溯源；以动作条件未来预测与风险敏感规划连接表征与驾驶决策。离线：用 NeRF 或 3D Gaussian Splatting 重建录制场景，再用生成模型扩展长尾条件，并限制生成内容作为几何真值的用途。实验设计包括数据划分、传感器消融、故障注入、成对闭环测试与实到虚一致性评估。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果，仅提出核心假设：在同等计算预算下，显式证据管理可降低危险的虚假自由空间预测与遮挡风险，同时保持可接受的驾驶效率；所有性能声明均待实验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将测量驱动的占据/不确定性建模与生成式仿真结合，强调证据溯源与生成内容的使用边界，为自动驾驶世界模型提供了一个可验证的研究框架，但目前仍属提案式描述，缺乏实证支撑。

Abstract: Three dimensional modeling for autonomous driving must continuously estimate road geometry, obstacle occupancy, traffic participant behavior, and observational uncertainty under limited computational resources. Existing reconstruction methods can synthesize realistic novel views, but visual fidelity does not sufficiently characterize the geometric reliability required for driving. Generative world models can expand scenarios and predict future observations, yet may introduce content unsupported by measurements. We propose DriveWorld3D, a framework combining temporal video learning, measurement constraints from multiple sensors, and offline generative simulation. Its online component maintains a unified world state comprising road surfaces, volumetric occupancy, dynamic entities, and road relations, while separately representing physical occupancy, observability, and evidence provenance. Action conditioned future prediction and risk sensitive planning connect this representation to driving decisions. Its offline component reconstructs recorded scenes using neural radiance fields or 3D Gaussian Splatting, then expands long tail conditions using generative models while restricting the use of generated content as geometric ground truth. We specify data partitioning, sensor ablations, fault injection, paired closed loop testing, and real to simulation consistency assessment. The central hypothesis is that explicit evidence management can reduce dangerous false free space predictions and occlusion risk within a matched computational budget while retaining acceptable driving efficiency. All performance claims remain subject to experimental validation. Keywords: autonomous driving; 3D occupancy; sensor fusion; uncertainty; generative world models; NeRF; 3D Gaussian Splatting; closed loop simulation

</details>


### [122] [Mothership: A Two Tier Autonomous Refuse Collection System for Pedestrian Campuses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22461)
*Wang, Wei,Wang, Yiru Veronika,Wang, Yihan,Li, Zihao,Liang, Xiaohui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文明提出Mothership两级自主垃圾收集系统：电动压缩车作为"母船"搭载两台低速人行道机器人(RoboCart)，RoboCart负责取送标准两轮垃圾桶并回舱充电；通过按尺度分工把举升倾倒机构留在车上，放宽对接容差至±100 mm；离散事件仿真给出吞吐量与桶密度、小车数量、停靠半径的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园步行区、公园与混合用地的小巷、庭院、楼栋服务门处垃圾桶无法被压缩车触及，只能依赖人工收集，自动化存在空白。

Method: 两级架构+尺度分工（母船负责道路行驶、压缩、举升与供能；小车仅做人行道运输与抱桶）+桶处理与对接接口设计+把路线分解为卡车停靠点与小车任务的mission-planning形式化+合成校园布局（2–16 bins/ha）上的离散事件仿真，扫描桶密度、小车数与停靠半径。

Result: 两车配置吞吐20–29 bins/h；吞吐由小车数与停靠半径交互决定：单车主打密集近距停靠点，三/四车需≈80 m宽停靠半径才能保持忙碌，中高密度下达35–49 bins/h；最优停靠半径须与车队规模联合选择。

Conclusion: 通过"车做重活、小车做轻活"的尺度分工与放宽对接容差，可实现步行区垃圾收集自动化；但本文证据仅为仿真，缺乏实机验证，系统设计参数需与车队规模协同优化。

Abstract: Refuse collection in pedestrian campuses, parks and mixed-use districts remains manual because compaction trucks cannot reach bins located on walkways, courtyards and building service doors. This paper presents Mothership, a two-tier autonomous refuse collection system in which an electric compaction truck (the mothership) carries two low-speed sidewalk robots (RoboCarts) that fetch standard two-wheel bins, deliver them to the truck, return the empty bins to their original positions and re-dock on the truck for charging. The architecture separates responsibilities by scale: the truck performs road driving, compaction, lifting and energy supply, while the carts perform only sidewalk transport and bin grasping. This split removes the lift-and-tip mechanism from the sidewalk vehicle, keeping it light and low, and relaxes cart-to-truck docking tolerance to the +/-100 mm capture window of a standard automated lifter. We describe the system architecture, the bin-handling and docking interfaces, and a mission-planning formulation that decomposes a route into truck stop points and cart tasks. A discrete-event simulation over synthetic campus layouts with 2-16 bins/ha quantifies throughput as a function of bin density, cart count and stop-point radius. A two-cart configuration serves 20-29 bins/h. Throughput is governed by an interaction between cart count and stop-point radius: a single cart performs best with many close stops, whereas three or four carts need wide stops (80 m) to stay busy, reaching 35-49 bins/h at moderate-to-high densities. The best stop radius therefore has to be chosen together with the fleet size, not fixed in advance.

</details>


### [123] [Has GPT-6-Astra Achieved Embodied Intelligence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22471)
*SenseNova,Team, Joint Research,:,Cai, Zhongang,Cao, Ziang,Chen, Tailai,Chua, Han Chong,Dai, Linjun,Deng, Hanming,Deng, Hokin,Diao, Haiwen,Ding, Chenjing,Fan, Weichen,Fu, Xiao,Gong, Zerui,Gu, Chenyang,He, Zexin,Hou, Zhi,Li, Bo,Li, Qinyue,Li, Quanzhou,Li, Xiaowan,Liao, Zhichao,Lin, Dahua,Lin, Zilong,Liu, Bo,Liu, Chunxiao,Liu, Pengfei,Liu, Tianqi,Liu, Ziwei,Loy, Chen Change,Lu, Lewei,Luo, Jiapeng,Ma, Yuexiao,Pan, Liang,Pan, Xingang,Qian, Oscar,Shang, Ziyi,Shao, Yuyao,Su, Dawei,Tao, Dacheng,Tao, Qingyi,Wang, Quan,Wang, Ruisi,Wang, Xiaogang,Wang, Ziyi,Wen, Beichen,Wu, Donghao,Wu, Jiaxu,Wu, Silei,Wu, Xiaoming,Wu, Zhonghua,Xiao, Hongcan,Xu, Weiye,Yamashita, Atsushi,Yang, Lei,Yang, Zhe,Yin, Baiqiao,Yin, Wanqi,Zhao, Hanfei,Zhong, Tianbo,Zhuo, Long,Zuo, Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告大规模评测 GPT-6-Astra 在机器人操作与导航上的能力边界，分析 System 2/System 1 智能体框架、真实部署差距，并提出下一代具身基准原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验通用大模型是否已可取代按任务/构型定制的专用机器人模型，并厘清其能力上限、系统集成代价与真实部署中的实际瓶颈。

Method: 在 12 个代表性基准（桌面操作、人形、移动平台、灵巧手）上以统一协议评测单模型，允许 ICL 并显著增加执行步数；构建高层 System 2 推理器与低层 System 1 控制器（如 π0.5）的闭环协同框架；在 8 种真实机器人构型上部署验证；设计原型基准（任务源于真实需求、无域内示范、按世界时钟持续计时评判）。

Result: 统一协议下取得前所未有的评测表现；System 2–System 1 闭环有效但代价高昂；真实部署暴露两大差距——ICL 所需机器人示范采集昂贵、物理约束下无法用更长执行时间换取成功率。

Conclusion: GPT-6-Astra 已基本跨越具身能力门槛，但尚未达到实用具身智能；后续需改进 ICL 泛化性、成本与执行时间、物理状态校验、不可逆失败恢复及长程进度保持。

Abstract: GPT-6-Astra demonstrates a level of competence in robotic manipulation and navigation that challenges the conventional paradigm of building specialized models for individual tasks and embodiments. In this report, we ask not only how capable GPT-6-Astra is, but what its capabilities imply for the field. First, we probe the limits of standalone GPT-6-Astra. We conduct a large-scale evaluation across 12 representative benchmarks spanning tabletop manipulation, humanoids, mobile platforms, and dexterous hands. Under a unified protocol that allows in-context learning (ICL) from training-set demonstrations and substantially increases the number of execution steps, GPT-6-Astra achieves unprecedented performance (Fig. 1). Future research may focus on improving 1) the generality of ICL 2) cost and execution time, 3) physical-state verification, 4) recovery from irreversible failures, and 5) long-horizon preservation of progress. Second, we study the agentic harness for embodied control. We identify that close-loop coordination between a high-level "System 2" reasoner (i.e., GPT-6-Astra) and a low-level "System 1" controller (e.g., {\pi}0.5) is costly but effective. Third, we examine GPT-6-Astra on real-world deployment. Our experiments on 8 robot configurations reveal substantial gaps: 1) robot demonstrations required for ICL are expensive to collect, and 2) it is implausible to trade longer execution for higher success rates with physical constraints. Fourth, we envision the next generation of embodied benchmarks. We develop a prototype that 1) derives tasks from real-world needs, 2) provides no in-domain demonstrations, and 3) judges agents on a world clock that keeps running while they deliberate. Taken together, our results suggest that GPT-6-Astra has largely crossed the threshold of embodied competence, but not yet that of practical embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [124] [Scaling Online Exploration for Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22496)
*Huang, Runhan,Zhang, Xiangcheng,Chen, Haoyu,Li, Yitong,Wang, Yen-Jen,Lyu, Bohan,Ye, Haotian,Hong, Chuye,Wang, Yixuan,Su, Chloe,Xiong, Haoyu,Wu, Yangzhen,Xie, Enze,Manderson, Travis,Ermon, Stefano,Abbeel, Pieter,Torralba, Antonio,Song, Dawn,Ye, Weirui,Du, Yilun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAX：以通用编码智能体生成机器人控制程序、完全依赖在线探索合成可执行程序的开源机器人智能体框架，无需遥操作示教数据，在多构型（单臂/双臂/灵巧手/移动/人形）仿真与真机上验证探索规模化带来泛化与持续自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习依赖大规模示教数据与专用遥操作语料，换构型/换任务即需重新采集；策略被模仿分布所限，难以泛化与持续自提升。作者希望把"规模化"的重心从示教数据转向在线探索经验。

Method: RAX 框架由通用编码代理直接生成机器人控制代码，不使用动作示教、手工动作原语或价值函数引导；代理在在线探索中发现达成任务目标的决策序列，并将其编译为模块化程序（封装感知-规划-控制闭环）；先前会话验证过的技能可复用组合。以统一 API 接入新构型，跨单臂、双臂、灵巧、移动、人形系统在仿真与真机做多构型系统研究。

Result: 未见环境的泛化性能随探索规模稳定提升；仿真中平均超过最强基线 +37.4%；真机上零人类示教自主学会依赖记忆、关节物体操作、高精度、可变形物体及长程移动操作任务，并通过组合已验证技能持续改进；探索规模化后涌现主动感知、错误恢复、安全护栏与人机协作等能力。

Conclusion: RAX 将机器人智能体的扩展维度从示教数据转向在线探索与程序合成，展示了跨构型、跨仿真/真机的一致收益，并作为仅需 API 接入、无需遥操作采集与策略训练的通用机器人智能体平台开源。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced by scaling demonstration datasets and model capacity; however, deploying a new robot embodiment or task typically necessitates a dedicated teleoperation corpus. The resulting policies remain bounded by their imitation distributions, limiting generalization and continual self-improvement. We introduce RAX (Robot Agent eXploration), an open-source framework for robot agents centered on scaling exploration: enabling agents to improve by acquiring online experience and synthesizing executable programs. In RAX, a general-purpose coding agent generates robot control code, operating without action demonstration data, hand-engineered action primitives, or value-function guidance. Through online exploration, the agent identifies decision sequences that achieve task goals and compiles them into modular programs encapsulating the full perception-planning-control loop. Using RAX, we present a systematic multi-embodiment study on scaling online exploration across precision and long-horizon manipulation tasks using single-arm, bimanual, dexterous, mobile, and humanoid systems in simulation and on real robots. Generalization performance on unseen environments improves consistently as exploration scales. In simulation, RAX outperforms top baseline models by +37.4% on average. On real robots, RAX autonomously learns memory-dependent, articulated, high-precision, deformable, and long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks without human demonstrations, continually improving performance by composing verified skills from prior exploration sessions. As exploration scales, we observe emergent capabilities including active perception, error recovery, safety guardrails, and human-robot collaboration. We open-source RAX as a generalist robot-agent platform in which new robot embodiments are integrated through their APIs alone, without teleoperated data collection or policy training.

</details>


### [125] [RTISSLAM-3D: Full 6-DoF SLAM With an In-Air 3D Imaging Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22552)
*Steckel, Jan,Katter, Vincent,Jansen, Wouter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RTISSLAM-3D：搭载空中3D成像声呐+IMU+轮速计的轮式机器人6-DoF声呐SLAM系统，用球面CFAR稀疏化、光束形状建模的声学里程计、极坐标子图描述子回环检测与3D配准验证，构建带姿态因子的6-DoF位姿图，在545m森林行驶中将水平误差从1.74m降至0.28m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声呐SLAM均为平面化（2D轨迹），丢弃了声呐图像中的高度/俯仰信息，该假设在粗糙、有坡度的森林地面等场景失效；同时空中3D成像声呐已能逐脉冲覆盖整个前半球，具备做真正3D声呐SLAM的硬件条件。

Method: (1) 在球面上运行CFAR检测器，将每帧声呐图像压缩为稀疏3D检测点；(2) 基于3D检测构建声学里程计，并显式建模声呐波束形状；(3) 用极坐标子图描述子做地点识别；(4) 以3D配准验证回环闭合；(5) 全部融合进含姿态（attitude）因子的6-DoF位姿图优化，并融合IMU与轮式编码器。

Result: 在545m森林行驶数据上、以3D激光SLAM为参考评估：回环闭合使声学里程计水平误差由1.74m降至0.28m，高度误差0.12m；最终3D声呐地图94.6%的点位于激光地图0.3m范围内。

Conclusion: 首次实现面向空中3D成像声呐的6-DoF SLAM，证明利用俯仰信息可显著提升粗糙地形下的轨迹与建图精度；为声呐在GPS拒止、视觉退化环境中的3D导航提供了可行链路。

Abstract: Echolocating bats navigate dense forests using sonar alone, and in-air 3D imaging sonars now capture the entire frontal hemisphere with every pulse. Sonar SLAM, however, has so far been planar: current systems estimate a 2D trajectory and discard the elevation information in the sonar images, an assumption that does not hold on rough and sloped terrain such as a forest floor. In this paper, we introduce RTISSLAM-3D, a novel 6-DoF SLAM system for a wheeled robot that carries an in-air 3D imaging sonar, an IMU and wheel encoders. A CFAR detector on the sphere reduces every sonar image to a sparse set of 3D detections, which we use for a 3D acoustic odometry that models the shape of the sonar beam, for place recognition with polar submap descriptors, and for the verification of loop closures by 3D registration, all combined in a 6-DoF pose graph with attitude factors. We evaluated RTISSLAM-3D on a 545m drive through a forest, with a 3D lidar SLAM as reference. The loop closures reduced the horizontal error of the acoustic odometry from 1.74m to 0.28m, with a height error of 0.12m, and 94.6% of the resulting 3D sonar map lies within 0.3 m of the lidar map.

</details>


### [126] [When Plans Change, Remember: Replanning-Guided Visual Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22556)
*Smelova, Victoria,Kazachkov, Daniil,Kazantsev, Georgiy,Tsvetkova, Iuliia,Cherepanov, Egor,Kachaev, Nikita,Latyshev, Artem,Panov, Aleksandr I.,Kovalev, Alexey K.*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RePlanMem-VLA利用VLA策略自身receding-horizon下相邻action chunk的不一致性作为事件信号，在线触发有界视觉记忆写入，实现选择性关键帧记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序多阶段VLA任务常需要当前观测中已不存在的视觉信息，保留完整历史代价高且冗余，因此需要选择性视觉记忆。但"何时写入记忆"缺乏可靠信号。

Method: 在receding-horizon执行下，相邻action chunk对重叠未来动作的预测出现异常大的分歧，被视为任务相关事件的证据；比较35种分歧度量，在单任务自回归decoder-only与Flow-Matching Transformer（从头训练）以及预训练OpenVLA-OFT、π0.5上筛选信号；用选定信号触发有界视觉记忆更新，并加机器人掩码写过滤器抑制冗余帧。

Result: 事件检测器在MIKASA-Robo-VLA任务上ROC-AUC达0.981–0.997；记忆保持实验中100%的episode将任务相关观测保留至后续决策点；真实机器人回放数据表明事件选择与冗余抑制可超出仿真。

Conclusion: 将plan revision确立为策略内部信号，用于控制VLA记住什么，提供了低成本、无需额外监督的记忆写入机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon, multi-stage Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies often require visual information that is no longer present in the current observation. Retaining the complete observation history is computationally expensive and highly redundant, motivating selective visual memory. We introduce RePlanMem-VLA (Replanning-Guided Keyframe Memory for VLA), which turns a policy's own plan revisions into an online memory-write signal. Under receding-horizon execution, consecutive action chunks predict some of the same future actions. RePlanMem-VLA treats unusually large disagreement between these temporally aligned predictions as evidence of a task-relevant event. We first compare 35 disagreement measures across single-task autoregressive decoder-only and Flow-Matching Transformers trained from scratch, then validate the selected signals on pretrained OpenVLA-OFT and $\pi_{0.5}$ policies over MIKASA-Robo-VLA tasks. The resulting detectors achieve ROC-AUC of 0.981-0.997. We then use the selected signals in RePlanMem-VLA to trigger updates to a bounded visual memory, with a robot-masked write filter suppressing redundant frames. In the evaluated memory-retention experiments, RePlanMem-VLA preserves task-relevant observations until the later decision point in 100% of episodes. Real-robot replay data further show that event selection and redundancy suppression extend beyond simulation. These results establish plan revision as a policy-internal signal for controlling what a VLA remembers. Website: https://vika787.github.io/RePlanMem/.

</details>


### [127] [Micro-Localization: High-Precision Mobile Robot Localization Using Microscopic Ground Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22563)
*Wilhelm, Aaron,Marcon, Marina de Albuquerque,Uyanwatte, Emith,Napp, Nils*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用显微镜光学观测自然地面纹理，实现数十微米级的高精度全局定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动机器人定位精度通常停留在毫米至厘米级，而固定式精密定位系统虽精度高但缺乏机动性。作者希望借助高倍显微成像挖掘自然表面微纹理信息，把移动定位推进到微米级精度区间。

Method: 建立光学放大倍率、图像空间定位不确定性与物理定位精度之间的解析关系；基于该关系设计显微视觉定位系统，解决高倍成像下的标定、相机建模（含景深/畸变）与对焦难题；用标定好的精密龙门平台在多种表面纹理上采集地图与评测，并配合独立的光度配准（photometric registration）交叉验证特征法估计。

Result: 每种表面 1,007 条留出查询全部成功定位，平移 RMSE 28–73 μm；光度配准与特征定位结果吻合在约 9 μm RMSE 内；在低成本移动机器人上使用龙门采集的地图实现定位；并可分辨热膨胀引起的微米级位移。

Conclusion: 自然表面微纹理可作为高精度定位信源，使移动平台达到通常只有固定式精密定位系统才具备的精度水平，为微米级移动操作/装配提供可行路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate high-precision global localization using natural ground texture viewed through microscope optics, achieving localization in the tens-of-micrometers regime. For the planar ground texture localization problem considered here, we relate optical magnification and image-space localization uncertainty to physical localization precision. Leveraging this relationship, we then develop a microscope-based localization system that addresses the calibration, camera-modeling, and focus challenges introduced by high-magnification imaging. We evaluate this system across several surface textures using a calibrated precision gantry. Across 1,007 held-out queries per surface, the system localizes every query and achieves translation RMSEs of 28$-$73 $\mu$m. Independent photometric registration agrees with the feature-based localization estimates to within approximately 9 $\mu$m RMSE. We further demonstrate localization on a low-cost mobile robot using the gantry-collected maps, and also show that the system can resolve micrometer-scale displacement caused by thermal expansion. These results show that microscopic natural surface texture can bring mobile localization into a precision regime typically associated with fixed positioning systems.

</details>


### [128] [Embodiment-Classifier Guidance: Transferring Frozen VLAs Across Robot Dynamics without Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22576)
*Han, Devin,Niranjani, Hershraj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 训练轻量"本体分类器"区分源本体与目标本体上的成功动作块，在冻结π₀的去噪每一步加入分类器梯度引导，实现免演示、免微调的新本体迁移，LIBERO上平均成功率72%→80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略（如π₀）在固定本体集合上训练，迁移到新本体通常需要目标本体的演示或参数微调，代价高。作者希望在冻结源策略、无额外示教、无参数更新的前提下完成跨本体适配。

Method: 用少量校准任务训练二分类器，判别动作块"来自源本体成功分布"还是"目标本体成功分布"，其梯度可估计从源成功动作分布指向目标成功动作分布的方向；在π₀每步去噪时以log p_target梯度扰动采样（embodiment-classifier guidance），本质是分布层面的奖励/似然引导，不触碰策略参数。

Result: LIBERO仿真中4种修改控制器、12个suite-controller组合上，平均成功率由72%升至80%，失败率相对降低31%（11/12提升，5个经Holm校正显著）；每任务仅需少量目标本体rollout，具备数据效率。局限：弱腕/锁腕本体上引导效果不稳定甚至有害（分类器数据不足或偏好不可行动作）。用引导π₀采集的成功数据重新训练分类器，可把某弱腕手臂任务成功率从52.5%提升到70.7%。

Conclusion: 提出一种无需微调、无需目标演示的冻结流策略跨本体迁移方法，验证了"分类器梯度作为本体对齐方向"的有效性与数据效率，并提出引导采样—重训分类器的自增强闭环，为sim-to-real降低rollout成本提供思路；但在动力学受限（弱腕/锁腕）本体上需额外数据覆盖。

Abstract: Flow-based policies such as $\pi_0$ are trained on a fixed set of embodiments, and transferring them to new ones is an open problem. To adapt a frozen $\pi_0$ to a new embodiment without additional demonstrations or parameter updates, we train a lightweight embodiment classifier to distinguish successful action chunks on the source body from those on the target body, using a few calibration tasks. On transfer tasks that $\pi_0$ saw on its source body but the classifier was never trained on, we nudge each denoising step of $\pi_0$ with the classifier's gradient, which estimates the direction from the distribution of successful actions on the source body toward that on the target body. On the LIBERO simulation benchmark with four modified controllers, embodiment-classifier guidance raises $\pi_0$'s average success on the target bodies from 72% to 80% across 12 suite-controller pairs, with a 31% relative reduction in failures (11 of 12 gain; 5 significant after Holm correction). In simulation, guidance is data-efficient, needing few target-body rollouts per task, which makes it promising for sim-to-real transfer, where rollouts are costly. Guidance is mixed on weak wrists and harmful on a locked wrist, when the classifier lacks data or favors infeasible motions. Collecting more target-body data with guided $\pi_0$ can fill such gaps: on one weak-wrist arm, guided $\pi_0$ solved a task on which unguided $\pi_0$ had zero successes in the original data, and retraining the classifier on the guided successes raised success from 52.5% to 70.7%.

</details>


### [129] [FCTS: Failure-Guided Curriculum Tree Search Over Generated Terrain for Legged Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22619)
*Parameswaran, Narayanan Palghat,Kim, Dong-Ki,Patrikar, Jay,Gutierrez, Santiago,Zade, Harshad,Omidshafiei, Shayegan,Khambhaita, Harmish*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FCTS：用局部通行失败（位置、终止模式、机器人状态、周边地形几何）条件化自动生成下一轮训练地形，并以MCTS在固定训练预算下搜索课程轨迹，显著提升四足机器人在真实扫描工地地形上的通行成功率与真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业与灾后场地的障碍呈不规则、复合几何，与标准障碍差异大；直接在实际重建几何上训练也难以泛化。手工课程只覆盖少量地形族，LLM生成课程对"针对哪类策略弱点"缺乏可控性。

Method: 失败条件化地形课程框架：先用定位到的通行失败信号（失败位置、终止模式、机器人在失败时刻状态、周边地形几何）作为生成条件，自动合成针对性的新地形；再用蒙特卡洛树搜索在固定训练预算下探索不同课程演进轨迹，选择最优课程序列。

Result: 在扫描真实建筑工地构建的地形上，成功率相对最强parkour与LLM课程基线在中等难度从28.4%提升至85.8%，高难度从14.9%提升至44.7%；在完整通行罕见的极端地形上保持更高进度；四足真机实验验证策略可迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: 以真实失败为信号驱动地形生成、并用搜索优化课程序列，可有效弥合"重建几何直接训练"与"真实不规则复合地形通行"之间的泛化差距，且具备实机可用性。

Abstract: Quadruped parkour policies handle canonical obstacles well, but their training curricula often miss the irregular, compound geometry of industrial and disaster-response sites, where agile locomotion matters most. Such environments pose a hard generalization problem: even training directly on reconstructed geometry from the target site does not reliably yield policies that traverse it. Hand-designed curricula span only a few terrain families, and recent LLM-generated curricula offer little control over which policy weaknesses they target. We propose FCTS, a failure-conditioned terrain curriculum framework that uses localized traversal failures to automatically generate the terrains a policy should train on next. Each failure's location, termination mode, robot state, and surrounding terrain geometry condition this generation, and Monte Carlo Tree Search explores alternative curriculum trajectories under a fixed training budget. On terrains built from scanned real-world construction sites, FCTS raises success rate from 28.4% to 85.8% on a medium-difficulty course and from 14.9% to 44.7% on a hard course relative to the strongest parkour or LLM-curriculum baselines, and maintains higher progress on extreme terrains where full traversal is rare. Hardware experiments on a quadruped robot confirm that the learned FCTS policies transfer to the real world.

</details>


### [130] [Task Structure and Scalability in Multi-Robot Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22670)
*Marowski, Christian,Min, Byung-Cheol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文把绘画表示为几何笔画图，比较“整组件单令牌分配”与“共享令牌（组件内分解）分配”两种多机器人绘制流水线的扩展性，发现后者平均加速更明显、大队规模时闲置时间占比更低，但并非所有图形都提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同绘制虽可通过分散工作量缩短完成时间，但加速幅度取决于任务结构与分配-路径规划流水线，作者希望量化这种结构与流水线设计之间的关系。

Method: 将图形建模为几何笔画图，对比组件级单令牌分配与允许同一连通组件内多机协作的共享令牌分解分配；对385幅图形、1–8台机器人的团队做仿真研究，按各自单机完成时间归一化加速比，并按结构属性分组分析扩展行为。

Result: 单令牌分配出现加速收益递减；共享令牌流水线平均加速更大（各自单机基线归一化），大队规模下闲置时间占比更低，但不能为每一幅图形降低完成时间；结构描述子与扩展行为存在关联，大队规模下若干属性组间差异更明显。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了所评估流水线的经验扩展规律，说明除组件数量外，还必须考虑组件内工作负载与图结构；属仿真层面的多机器人任务分配研究，与Physical AI/3D重建核心方向关联有限。

Abstract: Multi-robot authoring can reduce completion time by distributing drawing work, but the resulting speedup depends on task structure and the allocation-routing pipeline. We investigate these relationships by representing drawings as geometric stroke graphs and comparing component-level (single-token) allocation with shared-token (decomposed) allocation, which permits multiple robots to work within a connected component. A simulation study of 385 drawings and teams of one to eight robots shows diminishing speedup gains under single-token allocation and larger mean speedups under the shared-token pipeline, each normalized by its own single-robot completion time. Shared-token execution also exhibits lower idle-time shares at larger team sizes, although it does not reduce completion time for every drawing. Comparisons across structural attribute groups reveal associations between structural descriptors and differences in scaling behavior, with greater separation between several attribute groups at larger team sizes. These findings characterize empirical scaling in the evaluated pipelines and highlight the importance of considering within-component workload and graph structure alongside component count. Additional materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/multirobotauthoring/home.

</details>


### [131] [Spatial Memory by Agents, for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22736)
*Wang, Qianwei,Zhang, Ce,Luo, Zhanpeng,Su, Pengpeng,Yang, Shuo,Xu, Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用编码智能体（Designer Agent）自动化设计机器人空间记忆的"构建+检索"代码，在固定预算下迭代实现并用固定 Query Agent 的反馈调优；冻结后在留出观测史上构建记忆并在五类任务上评测。Codex/GPT-6 Astra 平均 44.23，比最强全覆盖基线 ReMEmbR（29.32）相对提升 50.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人空间记忆需要同时决定"存什么"和"怎么取"，且要兼顾语义、度量与时序多种需求，人工设计成本高、耦合强。作者希望验证编码智能体能否通过迭代实现与评测把这一设计过程自动化，并在固定预算下与人类设计基线比较。

Method: 固定开发预算下，Designer Agent 依据固定 Query Agent 的反馈编写与修改记忆构建/检索代码；实现冻结后在未见过的观测历史上离线构建记忆（不接触下游问题），再用同一 Query Agent 在五个互补任务轨道上评测；共进行三次独立的设计搜索，并与 ReMEmbR 等支持全部五轨的基线对比，辅以定性分析。

Result: 三次搜索平均五轨等权得分 44.23，ReMEmbR 为 29.32，相对提升 50.9%；定性分析将差异归因于证据保留与检索方式，同时发现 ground、证据使用类失败，并揭示任务相关的性能—计算成本权衡。

Conclusion: 编码智能体可有效自动化空间记忆设计，但存在任务依赖的权衡与证据使用缺陷；工作偏基准/流程研究，缺乏真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Spatial memory enables robots to reuse past observations for localization, navigation, and question answering. Designing such memory involves jointly deciding what to retain and how to retrieve it for diverse semantic, metric, and temporal needs. We investigate whether coding agents can automate this design process through iterative implementation and evaluation. Under a fixed development budget, a Designer Agent writes and revises code for memory construction and retrieval using feedback from a fixed Query Agent. The resulting implementations are frozen before building memories from held-out observation histories, without access to downstream questions, and evaluated across five complementary task tracks using the same Query Agent. Across three independent design searches, Codex / GPT-6 Astra averages 44.23 on the equally weighted five-track score, versus 29.32 for ReMEmbR, the strongest evaluated baseline supporting all five tracks, a 50.9% relative improvement. Qualitative analysis relates performance differences to evidence retention and retrieval, while identifying grounding and evidence-use failures. These results demonstrate the potential of coding agents to automate spatial memory design, while revealing task-dependent trade-offs in performance and computational cost. Project page: https://ba-fa-3344.github.io/.

</details>


### [132] [Shape-Aware Neural Time Fields for Rigid-Body Motion and Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22762)
*Zhou, Yanming,Liu, Yuchen,Qureshi, Ahmed H.*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Shape-Aware Neural Time Fields: 学习可复用的刚体运动 travel-time/cost-to-go 场，分别建模平移与旋转的局部运动难度，并与采样规划器结合实现2D/3D运动规划与非抓取式推动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体规划的难点在于平移与旋转被环境几何以不同方式约束，两者耦合使搜索困难；现有物理约束神经规划方法对平移/旋转几何限制建模不足，且规划效率与成功率的平衡不佳。

Method: 构建物理信息神经网络学习形状感知的到达时间（cost-to-go）场，显式区分平移与旋转的局部几何约束；将该场与采样规划器耦合，在向目标推进与到达邻近位姿的代价之间做权衡；通过消融验证表示与规划策略各自贡献。

Result: 在2D与3D刚体规划任务及多环境推动式非抓取操作中，规划成功率与推理效率显著优于物理信息神经规划基线；规划时间显著低于经典采样方法而保持竞争力。闭环推动实验在执行偏差和外部扰动下通过快速重规划实现反应式自适应执行。

Conclusion: 显式的平移-旋转分离式代价表示能以低成本泛化到多种刚体规划与推动任务，重规划速度支持扰动下的闭环自适应操作。

Abstract: Rigid-body motion planning is fundamental to robotic transport and manipulation, where both object position and orientation determine whether a body can move through cluttered environments. This coupling makes planning chal- lenging because translation and rotation can be constrained very differently by surrounding geometry. We propose Shape- Aware Neural Time Fields, a physics-informed neural motion planning framework that learns a reusable travel-time, or cost- to-go, representation for rigid-body motion while explicitly accounting for these distinct geometric restrictions. The learned field captures local motion difficulty in translation and rotation and is paired with a sampling-based planner that balances progress toward the goal with the cost of reaching nearby poses. We evaluate the framework across 2D and 3D rigid- body planning tasks and non-prehensile manipulation through object pushing in diverse environments. Results show substantial improvements in planning success and inference efficiency over physics-informed neural planning baselines, while remaining competitive with classical sampling-based planners at significantly lower planning times. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of both the learned representation and the proposed planning strategy. Finally, closed-loop pushing experiments under execution deviations and external disturbances demonstrate that fast replanning enables reactive and adaptive task execution.

</details>


### [133] [Pedicel-Targeted Strawberry Harvesting via Imitation Learning on a Low-Cost Teleoperated Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22765)
*Rai, Nitin,Lee, Won Suk,Leon, Hongyoung,Zhu, Heping*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于人类遥操作示教，训练 ACT(ResNet-50) 视觉-运动模仿学习策略，实现草莓果梗精准抓取与无损伤分离，并在 Jetson AGX Orin 上以 15 Hz 部署，同时开源数据、权重与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草莓果梗直径仅 1.3–3 mm、与叶片颜色相近，且需要精确力控才能在离层无损伤脱落；直接抓果实会损伤商品外观。经典方法难以在遮挡冠层中统一视觉模型、运动规划与接触操作，因此需要端到端视觉-运动策略。

Method: 采集人类遥操作示教数据（130 条演示），训练 Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) 策略，视觉主干为 ResNet-50；在 Jetson AGX Orin 边缘平台以 15 Hz 闭环部署；开源数据集、模型权重与部署代码。

Result: 130 条演示、100K 梯度步训练下损失从 2.84 收敛至 0.091；策略在边缘平台 15 Hz 实时运行。摘要未给出抓取成功率、损伤率等实机定量评估指标。

Conclusion: 工作证明模仿学习可用于细分作物器官（果梗）的接触式操作流程构建，并提供了可复现的农业机器人开源基线；但缺乏成功率/力控质量等任务级实机证据，方法上为 ACT 在农业场景的应用而非新算法。

Abstract: Precise pedicel-targeted grasping in strawberry harvesting remains a critical bottleneck due to the pedicel's small diameter (1.3-3 mm), color similarity with surrounding foliage, and precise force control required for clean abscission zone detachment. As a market-grade crop, strawberry aesthetic value makes bruise-free pedicel detachment essential over direct fruit grasping. Classical approaches struggle to unify vision models, motion planning, and contact manipulation into a robust framework, particularly in occluded canopy environments. Therefore, this work presents a visuomotor imitation learning pipeline for pedicel-targeted strawberry harvesting that unifies perception, motion planning, and contact manipulation from collecting human teleoperation demonstrations, training an Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) policy with a ResNet-50 visual backbone, and deploying the learned policy autonomously on a Jetson AGX Orin edge platform at 15 Hz. The trained policy achieved consistent loss convergence from 2.84 to 0.091 over 100K gradient steps on 130 demonstrations. We have also established a reproducible open-source pipeline with publicly released dataset and model weights for the agricultural robotics community. Full dataset is available at, https://huggingface.co/datasets/nitindominicrai/strawberry_pedicel_grasp_teleoperation and trained policy at, https://huggingface.co/nitindominicrai/act_strawberry_pedicel. To replicate this work, all code, configurations, and deployment instructions are publicly available at, https://github.com/nitin-dominic/autonomous_strawberry_pedicel_harvesting.

</details>
