If a point lies on a known plane, such as the table or the top of a block, it lies where its ray meets that plane. When the head camera is not enough, the robot moves a wrist camera to get a second view. We build the tools on only ten layouts of one seed per task, then freeze them and test them on ten new layouts of the same seed. We use Codex with GPT-6-Astra at medium reasoning effort. On all 42 RoboDojo tasks, HexaShell reaches a success rate of 39.7% and a progress score of 48.0, averaged in the same way as the leaderboard. This is a new state of the art on RoboDojo. The limit is contact, not reasoning: pushing reaches 92.5% and memory tasks 65.0%, but tight insertion only 8.3%. When the agent fails, it usually sees the failure but cannot make the motion work. We open source the tools evolved so far at https://github.com/HexaFuture/HexaShell.

</details>


### [169] [FLoTS-SLAM: Optical Flow-Derived Uncertainty for Monocular Dynamic SLAM with Two-Stage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23496)
*Du, Haorui,Zhou, Lipu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单目 RGB 动态 SLAM：用光流与相机运动诱导流之间的残差直接估计物体运动不确定性，生成像素级动态掩码，并设计分层 BA（高置信像素定姿 + 动态像素重投影联合优化位姿与深度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的单目 SLAM 需要实时识别运动物体；已有方法依赖渲染残差或多视图特征一致性来估计逐像素不确定性，缺乏直接运动证据，重建不准或特征歧义时易失效；且该不确定性仅在 BA 中降权，估计偏差仍会污染位姿与深度。

Method: 1) 运动不确定性估计：以光流与相机运动诱导流之差的残差作为直接运动证据，估计随帧对齐精度自适应变化的像素级不确定性，得到动态区域掩码；2) 鲁棒 BA：先用高置信像素估计相机位姿，再用该位姿重投影动态像素，在联合优化中精细化位姿与深度，降低运动物体对位姿/深度更新的干扰；3) 对动态掩码做定量评测并分析其对下游跟踪与重建的影响。

Result: DAVIS 上动态掩码平均 IoU 较 SOTA 基线提升 35%；Bonn 上平均 ATE 降低 59%，TUM 上降低 19%（相对于其所用 SLAM 主干）。

Conclusion: 以光流残差为直接运动证据的不确定性建模可得到更可靠的动态掩码，配合分层 BA 策略显著提升动态场景下单目 SLAM 的位姿与重建精度；掩码质量与下游跟踪/建图性能的定量关联也得到验证。

Abstract: Monocular simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) remains challenging in dynamic environments. Real-time dynamic object identification in such environments is a critical problem. Recent approaches estimate per-pixel uncertainty from rendering residuals or multiview feature inconsistency to identify dynamic regions. These methods lack direct motion evidence, and their accuracy may degrade when reconstruction is inaccurate or features are ambiguous. In addition, such uncertainty merely downweights pixels in bundle adjustment (BA), so inaccurate uncertainty estimates can still impair camera pose estimation and 3D reconstruction. Accordingly, we present a monocular RGB SLAM system for dynamic scenes. Specifically, a motion uncertainty estimation method is proposed to derive uncertainty from the residual between optical flow and camera motion induced flow, directly reflecting object motion. The estimation varies with frame alignment accuracy, reducing false detections and yielding accurate pixel-level masks of dynamic regions. To make BA more robust, we estimate camera poses from high confidence pixels. These poses are then used to reproject dynamic pixels during joint refinement of poses and depth, reducing the influence of object motion on both pose and depth updates. Moreover, we provide a quantitative evaluation of dynamic masks and analyze how these masks affect the downstream tracking and reconstruction of dynamic objects. Our approach improves the mean dynamic mask IoU by 35\% over a state-of-the-art baseline on DAVIS, and reduces the average ATE by 59\% on Bonn and 19\% on TUM relative to the SLAM backbone used in our system. The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [170] [Reinforcement Learning Based Multi-Terrain Adaptive Whole-Body Control for a Quadruped Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23548)
*Jin, Yanzhou,Suo, Fei,Liu, Chang,Hasegawa, Yasuhisa,Chen, Liming,Iwasawa, Yusuke,Matsuo, Yutaka,Zhu, Yaonan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的四足机械臂全身控制框架，结合混合任务表示与任务优先级全身学习，在小尺度四足平台上实现复杂地形下的任务一致性末端行为，并在30°斜坡、楼梯、可变形地形上完成实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂在非结构化环境中具备移动操作潜力，但地形扰动与移动-操作强耦合导致运动过程中末端执行器难以保持任务一致行为，小尺度四足平台尤为困难。

Method: 强化学习全身控制框架：混合任务表示（hybrid task representation）+ 任务优先级全身学习（task-prioritized whole-body learning）以协调移动与操作；同时自研轻量级四足机械臂硬件平台用于验证。

Result: 实机部署成功，可在最高30°斜坡、楼梯及可变形地形上完成移动操作任务，验证了学习型全身控制策略在真实环境中的可行性。

Conclusion: 学习型全身控制策略可实机部署于四足移动操作；任务一致的全身协调对地形自适应移动操作至关重要。

Abstract: Legged robots equipped with manipulators offer promising capabilities for loco-manipulation tasks in unstructured environments. However, achieving task-consistent end-effector behavior during locomotion remains challenging due to terrain-induced disturbances and the strong coupling between locomotion and manipulation, particularly for small-scale quadruped platforms. A reinforcement-learning based whole-body control framework is proposed for quadruped loco-manipulation on complex terrain. The proposed framework combines a hybrid task representation with task-prioritized whole-body learning to coordinate locomotion and manipulation while preserving task-consistent end-effector behavior. A lightweight quadruped manipulator platform is further developed to validate the proposed framework on a small-scale quadruped system. Real-world experiments demonstrate successful deployment on complex terrains such as slopes up to 30 degrees, stairs, and deformable terrain. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying learned whole-body control policy for quadruped loco-manipulation in real-world environments and highlight the importance of task-consistent whole-body coordination for terrain-adaptive loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [171] [Reason-guided Human Reward Modeling for Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23550)
*Nayak, Anshul,Shaik, Shahil,Smereka, Jonathan,Wang, Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出理由引导的偏好强化学习(PbRL)框架：在轨迹级偏好标签之外加入解释"为何偏好"的理由标签，联合训练人类奖励模型，并与环境奖励结合用于策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用任务奖励优化的RL策略虽高效，但可能与人类期望、安全偏好或意图不一致；仅靠偏好标签的PbRL缺乏对偏好成因的监督，奖励学习信号稀疏且可解释性不足。

Method: 基于轨迹级人类反馈学习人类奖励模型；除成对偏好标签外，额外引入理由标签（reason tags）提供额外监督；将学到的人类奖励与环境任务奖励在策略优化中组合。

Result: 摘要未给出量化结果，仅声称相较纯偏好奖励学习，理由引导形式提供更强监督、提升奖励与策略的可解释性，并使策略更符合人类偏好且对偏好更敏感，同时兼顾任务性能。

Conclusion: 理由标签是一种低成本、可提升人类对齐奖励学习质量与可解释性的辅助监督信号；但缺少实验细节、基准与真实场景验证，方法贡献偏增量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies optimized for task rewards may produce behaviors that are efficient but misaligned with human expectations, safety preferences, or intent. This paper presents a reason-guided preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) framework that learns a human reward model from trajectory-level feedback. In addition to preference labels, the framework incorporates reason tags explaining why a behavior is preferred, enabling the learned reward to capture both human choices and their underlying rationale. Compared with preference-only reward learning, the reason-guided formulation provides additional supervision for learning human-aligned rewards and improves the interpretability of the resulting policy. The learned human reward is combined with the environment reward during policy optimization, allowing the agent to improve task performance while producing more interpretable, human- aligned, and preference-sensitive behavior

</details>


### [172] [Embodiment-Aware Planning for Heterogeneous Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23551)
*Jain, Sukhvansh,Maurya, Anurag,Vats, Shivam,Dhar, Narendra Kumar,Prakash, Ravi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 探讨异构双臂（吸盘+平行夹爪）协同是否能完成单一夹爪无法完成的操作任务：以单次人类演示为输入，将执行问题建模为有限时域下的"手臂-轨迹分配"优化，逐段决策单臂操作或双臂协同交接，联合考虑异构抓取可行性、运动约束、可操作度与跨夹爪兼容性，并通过显式切换正则获得全局一致分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业平台常用吸盘或平行夹爪等任务专用末端执行器，但任何单一模式都难以覆盖取放、重定向、插入、受限放置等多阶段任务的多样约束。作者希望验证：把互补的简单夹爪组合成异构双臂系统，能否以"系统级"能力超越任何单一夹爪。

Method: 输入单条人类演示给出期望物体运动；将"具身感知执行"表述为对手臂-轨迹分配的有限时域优化。分段规划器判断每段需单臂操作还是双臂协同交接，代价/约束综合了异构抓取可行性、运动学约束、可操作度及跨夹爪交接兼容性，并加入切换正则项，求全局一致的分配。硬件为双 Franka FR3，分别装配吸盘与平行夹爪。

Result: 在双 Franka FR3（吸盘+平行夹爪）平台上实验，任务在单夹爪执行下不可行，采用异构双臂分配后可行性与可靠性均提升。

Conclusion: 异构简单夹爪的协同规划可在不升级末端硬件的前提下扩展任务可行域；单次人类演示+分段优化即可自动决定单臂/交接策略。抽象中未给出量化指标、成功率统计、与基线（如单一夹爪、同构双臂）的对比及失败模式分析。

Abstract: Many manipulation tasks that are effortless for dexterous human hands remain challenging for robotic systems equipped with simple, task-specialized grippers. Industrial platforms commonly rely on end-effectors such as suction cups or parallel-jaw grippers, yet no single mode can reliably satisfy the diverse constraints encountered across different phases of complex manipulation tasks, including pickup, reorientation, insertion, and constrained placement. In this work, we investigate whether complementary simple grippers within a heterogeneous bimanual setup can collectively realize tasks that exceed the capability of any individual gripper alone. Given a single human demonstration specifying the desired object motion, we formulate embodiment-aware execution as a finite-horizon optimization over arm-trajectory assignments. At each segment, the planner determines whether single-arm manipulation or a coordinated dual arm handover is required, jointly reasoning over heterogeneous grasp feasibility, motion constraints, manipulability, and cross-gripper compatibility. Exploiting this formulation, we compute globally consistent assignments with explicit switching regularization. Experiments on a dual Franka FR3 platform equipped with suction and parallel-jaw grippers demonstrate improved feasibility and reliability in tasks that are infeasible under single-gripper execution.

</details>


### [173] [Enhancing Visual Task-Redirection Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models via Closed-Loop Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23566)
*Ye, Chunjin,Chen, Guanying,Huang, Jianjie,Chen, Jianhao,Chen, Quanlin,Zhao, Hanjie,Liang, Haozhe,Qian, Tieyun,Cao, Xiaochun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACO：针对VLA模型的闭环对抗视觉攻击优化框架，用执行驱动的监督重建与进展引导的更新选择提升任务重定向成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLA模型的定向视觉攻击采用固定的目标监督优化扰动，但扰动会改变早期动作并进而改变后续到达的物理状态，固定目标与实际到达状态失配；且降低平均匹配误差并不保证任务持续推进，机器人在关键阶段仍可能失败，后续更新还会破坏已成功的早期行为。

Method: 提出闭环优化的攻击框架TRACO：(1) Execution-Driven Supervision——针对攻击下实际到达的状态重新构建监督信号以指导后续更新；(2) Progress-Guided Selection——将更新集中在进展停滞的阶段，同时保留到达该阶段所需的早期行为监督。

Result: 在多种VLA模型与仿真环境中，TRACO相比现有定向视觉攻击获得更高的任务重定向成功率。

Conclusion: 将攻击建模为闭环优化可显著提升视觉攻击对VLA机器人操作行为的重定向能力，凸显了闭环攻击优化的安全风险。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable language-guided robot manipulation, but remain vulnerable to visual attacks that undermine user control over robot behavior. Prior studies optimize visual perturbations using fixed target supervision. However, the perturbation can change earlier actions and, in turn, the physical states reached later. As the attacked trajectory evolves, this supervision may no longer match the behavior needed at the states actually reached. Accurately matching these targets can therefore fail to advance the attacker-specified task. Even with suitable supervision, reducing average matching error does not ensure sustained task progress. The robot may still fail at a critical stage, and further updates can disrupt previously successful behavior. To address these limitations, we propose TRACO, a framework that enhances existing targeted visual attacks through closed-loop optimization. Its Execution-Driven Supervision rebuilds supervision for the states reached under attack to guide subsequent updates. Within this framework, Progress-Guided Selection focuses updates where progress stalls while maintaining supervision on the earlier behavior needed to reach that point. Experiments across VLA models and simulation environments show improved task-redirection success with TRACO, highlighting the security implications of closed-loop attack optimization.

</details>


### [174] [F-LIVO: A Feature-Level Geometric Measurement Framework for LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23595)
*Dai, Xinye,Wang, Yapeng,Zhou, Shujie,Wang, Zihao,Zheng, Shiqi,Hu, Jiayu,Wang, Yusheng,Song, Weiwei,Tang, Weiming*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: F-LIVO：基于滤波器的激光-惯性-视觉里程计，用稀疏视觉点与LiDAR平面的点到面约束实现跨模态紧耦合，并用LiDAR地图恢复弱三角化视觉特征的深度，按局部平面可靠性自适应缩放残差协方差，保持紧凑地图表示并降低CPU/RSS开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量级LIVO精度高，但需持续为地图点附着稠密光度块或外观属性，带来额外存储、关联与更新代价；需要一种既保持跨模态耦合又维持紧凑地图、资源友好的方案。

Method: 滤波器框架下耦合视觉-惯性与激光-惯性子系统，构造视觉点-LiDAR平面的特征级点到面约束；通过局部平面拟合与射线-平面求交从LiDAR地图恢复视觉特征深度，以在视觉三角化失效时保持耦合；LiDAR更新中依据局部平面可靠性自适应缩放点到面残差协方差，抑制病态表面上的过度自信更新。

Result: 多个公开数据集上精度具有竞争力且随序列而异；在评估的退化场景中保持连续估计且端到端漂移累积较低；在给定资源配置下取得最低平均/峰值CPU占用、最低峰值RSS与最低RSS增长率（R2LIVE平均RSS更低）。

Conclusion: 以稀疏几何特征替代持久稠密光度地图属性，可在保持紧密跨模态耦合与合理精度的同时显著降低存储与计算开销，适合资源受限平台的多传感器里程计。

Abstract: Measurement-level LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) can achieve high accuracy, and its practical resource requirements depend on the adopted association and map-maintenance strategy. Persistently attaching dense photometric patches or appearance attributes to mapped LiDAR points can introduce additional storage, association, and update costs. This paper presents F-LIVO, a filter-based LIVO framework that couples a visual-inertial subsystem and a LiDAR-inertial subsystem through feature-level point-to-plane constraints between visual points and LiDAR planes. To preserve cross-modal coupling when visual triangulation is weak, we further recover visual feature depth from the LiDAR map through local plane fitting and ray-plane intersection. In the LiDAR update, the covariance of each point-to-plane residual is adaptively scaled according to local planar reliability, thereby reducing overconfident updates on poorly conditioned surfaces. Because the proposed method relies on sparse geometric features rather than persistent dense photometric map attributes, it retains a compact map representation. Experiments on multiple public datasets show competitive, sequence-dependent accuracy. Across the evaluated degradation cases, F-LIVO maintains continuous estimation with comparatively low accumulated end-to-end drift. Under the evaluated resource configuration, it achieves the lowest mean and peak central processing unit (CPU) usage, the lowest peak resident set size (RSS), and the lowest RSS growth rate among the evaluated implementations, while R2LIVE retains a lower mean RSS. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance to facilitate reproducible research.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Action Prediction: Learning Search-Free Planning in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23615)
*Du, Guanchen,Du, Jinhao,Sun, Yanpeng,Zhang, Shan,Zhao, Na,Li, Zechao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIJP 用"后见视角逆动力学"替代测试时在线搜索：离线轨迹中把未来观测重标记为目标，训练目标条件逆模型直接输出动作，并让预测动作经潜空间预测器回推的下一状态与后继观测对齐，实现免搜索的多步视觉控制规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型（如 JEPA）虽能提供动作条件的状态转移预测，但视觉规划需在测试时对动作序列做昂贵的在线优化。作者希望用目标条件逆模型直接回归动作，以避免在线搜索。难点在于：仅监督动作不能保证目标表征携带动作相关信息，也不能保证预测动作与已学潜预测器一致。

Method: 一类免搜索框架：(1) 沿离线轨迹将未来观测重标记为目标（多时间跨度），提供动作监督；梯度穿过目标表征以直接塑造视觉表征；(2) 将预测动作送入潜预测器，把结果下一状态表征与后继观测编码对齐，强制动作-转移一致性；(3) 推理时交替使用逆动力学模型与潜预测器，自回归生成多步动作序列，无需在线搜索。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务上，总体成功率 86.8%，相对基于搜索的规划取得 1.9–16.5 倍的规划加速。

Conclusion: 通过在训练阶段同时约束表征的目标条件性与动作-转移一致性，可在保持性能的同时把世界模型规划从测试时搜索转为前馈推理，显著降低规划开销；为潜世界模型的免搜索目标条件控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: Latent world models learn action-conditioned state transitions in a compact representation space, providing a predictive model for visual planning and control. However, planning with these models typically requires costly test-time optimization over action sequences. We propose to replace this online search with a goal-conditioned inverse model that directly predicts actions from current and goal observations. A key challenge is that action prediction alone neither ensures that goal representations encode action-relevant information nor that predicted actions are consistent with the learned latent prediction model. We introduce Hindsight Inverse JEPA Planning (HIJP), a search-free framework that addresses both aspects during training. HIJP relabels future observations along offline trajectories as goals, providing action supervision across multiple temporal horizons. By retaining gradients through the goal representations, this supervision directly shapes the visual representation. HIJP further feeds predicted actions into the latent predictor model and aligns the resulting next-state representations with encoded successor observations, enforcing action-transition consistency. At inference time, the inverse dynamics model and latent predictor models are applied alternately to generate multi-step action sequences without online search. Across four visual control tasks, HIJP achieves 86.8% overall success and a 1.9-16.5x planning speedup over search-based planning.

</details>


### [176] [Foundation-Model-Driven 3D Scene Graphs for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23622)
*Maggio, Dominic,Shaikewitz, Lorenzo,Naveen, Aryan,Hauser, Kris,Carlone, Luca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将 FOUND-IT 单目基础模型驱动的 3D 场景图集成到移动操作机器人自主流水线，实现物体变化检测、多次建图会话、可通行路径判定与抓取支持，并给出系统性局限分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的几何/语义基础模型虽已被用于从单张未标定相机实时构建任务驱动的开放集 3D 场景图（如 FOUND-IT），但缺乏将此类机器人空间记忆整合进完整可用自主流水线的系统级工作，也缺乏其在真实机器人上的效果与局限评估。

Method: 将 FOUND-IT 部署到移动操作平台上，围绕多种下游目标进行集成与实验：物体变化检测、多会话建图融合、可导航路径提取、以及直接支撑抓取以完成场景重排；并通过严格的真机实验对单目、基础模型驱动的 3D 场景图构建进行定性/定量分析。

Result: 真机实验表明 FOUND-IT 可支撑上述多类任务目标，是可行且可用的空间记忆模块；同时暴露出与几何/语义基础模型特性相关的具体失效模式与局限（如尺度、开放集语义、单目几何误差等）。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了新型 3D 空间记忆与完整自主流水线之间的系统级空白，并提供了对单目基础模型驱动场景图方法有效性边界的细致经验性洞察。

Abstract: Geometric and Semantic Foundation models are becoming increasingly popular tools for generating spatial maps for robots. Recent work (e.g., FOUND-IT) has leveraged these tools to construct real-time, task-driven, and open-set 3D scene graphs from a single uncalibrated camera. However, while classical robot mapping techniques have undergone extensive system demonstrations, limited work has been done to develop both the fundamental and the systems wide advancements needed to integrate these new types of robot spatial memory into a full, useable autonomy pipeline. In this paper, we bridge this gap, and in doing so we also evaluate the effectiveness and limitations of this type of 3D memory. Towards this end, we integrate FOUND-IT into a mobile manipulator platform and demonstrate it is well-suited to support multiple objectives such as object change detection, support for multiple mapping sessions, determination of navigable paths, and directly support object grasping for the task of scene re-arrangement. Furthermore, we provide nuanced insights into the effectiveness and limitations of a monocular, foundation-model-driven 3D scene graph construction system using observations from rigorous on-robot experiments. Code is available at https://github. com/MIT-SPARK/FOUND-IT.

</details>


### [177] [TransNav: Transformer-Based End-to-End Navigation Method for Static and Dynamic Quadrotor Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23635)
*Zhu, Jianghan,Li, Zhuo,Zhang, Yanjun,Sun, Jian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TransNav：基于Transformer的端到端四旋翼导航方法，用patch级点云编码融合局部特征与全局上下文，直接预测有限时域动作序列；结合轻量质点动力学模型的数值梯度反传实现无监督轨迹级代价优化，免专家示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四旋翼避障方法多专用于静态或动态单一场景，难以同时应对密集静态障碍与移动障碍，缺乏跨环境适应性。

Method: patch-based点云编码（局部特征提取+全局上下文建模）；感知与导航统一于单一Transformer架构，输出有限时域动作序列并执行首动作；引入轻量质点动力学模型，将预测序列上的轨迹级代价通过数值梯度传播反传，实现无监督训练，无需专家示范。

Result: 仿真与真机实验表明：在密集静态环境中表现强，动态场景中避障能力具竞争力，且推理开销低，适合机载部署。

Conclusion: 统一的端到端Transformer框架可同时覆盖静态与动态避障，无监督轨迹级训练有效替代示范学习，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Obstacle avoidance for autonomous quadrotors in cluttered environments remains challenging, especially when both dense static obstacles and moving obstacles are present. Existing methods are often tailored to either static or dynamic settings, which always limit their adaptability across diverse environments. This paper presents TransNav, a transformer-based end-to-end navigation method for quadrotor obstacle avoidance in both static and dynamic environments. TransNav employs a patch-based point-cloud encoding scheme that combines local feature extraction with global contextual modeling, enabling compact yet informative environment representation. The method integrates perception and navigation in a single architecture and directly predicts a finite-horizon action sequence, from which the first action is executed. Moreover, we develop an unsupervised learning strategy by incorporating a lightweight point-mass dynamics model, which directly optimizes trajectory-level costs over the predicted sequence through numerical gradient propagation. Thus, the policy can be efficiently trained without expert demonstrations. Simulation and real-world experiments show that the proposed method achieves strong performance in dense static environments and competitive obstacle-avoidance capability in dynamic scenarios, while maintaining low inference cost for onboard deployment.

</details>


### [178] [Learning Robot Metareasoning: Adaptive Computation Budgets for Real-Time Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23662)
*Magnino, Lorenzo,Marino, Antonio,Amir, Michael,Shishika, Daigo,Prorok, Amanda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将"每次规划的计算预算（动作承诺时长）"建模为SMDP，用RL策略依状态自适应选择预算，在导航、四旋翼飞行、接触丰富操作上超过固定预算基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发规划存在"思考深度 vs 反馈频率"的权衡：机器人在计算期间必须执行开环动作块，承诺越长规划越好但反馈越少。现有控制器在部署前固定该承诺时长与计算预算，而最优计算时间高度依赖状态（未建模动力学、安全约束、边际优化收益），离线静态调参会导致关键机动时"失明"或无法求得全局有效解。

Method: 把最优计算预算的识别表述为半马尔可夫决策过程（SMDP），单次决策同时确定机器人动作承诺与分配给求解器的时间。训练强化学习策略，条件输入为当前状态、正在执行的规划以及低成本的规划器统计量，从而实现元推理（metareasoning）式的动态预算分配。

Result: 在地面机器人导航、敏捷四旋翼飞行、接触丰富操作三类任务中，所学策略随场景需求改变预算，并发现优于固定预算基线的推理模式。摘要中未给出具体量化指标与真机实验规模。

Conclusion: 将计算预算分配作为可学习决策（SMDP + RL 元推理）是处理在线规划延迟-反馈权衡的有效且通用框架，跨多种机器人形态均有效；未来可扩展到更复杂的安全约束与真实部署验证。

Abstract: Planning while moving faces an historical trade-off between deliberation and reactivity. A planner running concurrently with execution needs time to compute, so the robot must commit to an open-loop action chunk for that duration: a longer commitment buys a better plan at the cost of feedback frequency. Existing controllers fix this action commitment, and consequently the computation budget, before deployment. However, the optimal computation time is highly state-dependent (due to factors such as unmodeled dynamics, safety constraints, and marginal optimisation gains), i.e. dynamic, therefore offline static tuning risks leaving systems blind during critical maneuvers or unable to find global effective solutions. We cast the identification of the optimal computation budget as a semi-Markov decision process (SMDP) in which a single decision defines both the robot action commitments and the time allocated to the solver. To solve this, we train a reinforcement-learning policy conditioned on the current state, the executing plan, and inexpensive planner statistics. Across ground-robot navigation, agile quadrotor flight, and contact-rich manipulation, the learned policy changes its budget with the demands of the scenario, discovering efficient reasoning patterns that outperform fixed-budget baselines. https://lorenzomagnino.github.io/learning-robot-metareasoning-review/

</details>


### [179] [Not All Actions Contribute Equally: Learning Precise Progress Estimation from Imprecise Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23769)
*Wang, Huanqian,Tang, Boshi,Xiao, Botong,Zhang, Jie,Yue, Yang,Tao, Danhua,Song, Shiji,Huang, Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIME: 通过聚合多条非完美演示轨迹构建状态加权图，用最短路近似到成功的测地距离，从而在含人类失误的演示中学习精确的进度估计器，无需单调性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作强化学习的进度估计器依赖专家数据，并假设演示中所有动作都有助于任务成功；在高精度任务中细微几何误差即可致失败，人类失误会被误判为有效进展，污染进度学习。核心缺陷是缺少关于任务评价标准的全局先验，难以区分真实进展与失误。

Method: 在观测状态上构建跨轨迹加权图：按空间近邻与视觉一致性连接不同轨迹的状态，边权编码空间距离作为转移代价；通过最短路搜索近似到成功状态的测地距离；以该距离的变化自然刻画前进、停滞与倒退，无需时间单调性假设。

Result: 仿真与真实世界高精度操作任务上一致有效，对不同次优程度的数据鲁棒；精度要求越高优势越明显，charger insertion 上相对 SFT 基线策略成功率最高提升 5 倍。

Conclusion: 将进度估计转化为跨轨迹状态图上的测地距离问题，可有效利用非完美演示提供稠密奖励，是高精度操作中可落地的进度估计方案。

Abstract: Progress estimators play an essential role in providing dense reward signals for reinforcement learning in robotic manipulation, but accurately assessing task progress can be much challenging. Existing methods heavily rely on expert data and naively assume that all actions within a demonstration contribute to task success. This assumption often breaks down in real-world scenarios, especially in high-precision manipulation, where subtle geometric errors can lead to task failure. Therefore, these methods can erroneously interpret human mistakes as meaningful progress, further corrupting progress learning. We argue that a key limitation is the lack of a global prior regarding task evaluation criteria, which is crucial for distinguishing meaningful progress from human mistakes. To this end, we propose PRIME, a novel progress estimator capable of learning precise progress estimation from imprecise demonstrations by aggregating experience across trajectories. PRIME constructs a weighted graph over observed states, connecting states across trajectories based on spatial proximity and visual consistency. Edge weights encode spatial distances as transition costs, and shortest-path search approximates the geodesic distance to success. Changes in this distance naturally distinguish advancement, stagnation, and setbacks without assuming monotonic progress over time. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real world on precise manipulation tasks show that PRIME is consistently effective across diverse tasks, and robust to data with varying degrees of suboptimality. Notably, the advantage of PRIME gets more pronounced in higher-precision scenarios, yielding up to a \(5\times\) improvement in policy success rate over the SFT baselines on charger insertion.

</details>


### [180] [MOSS: Multimodal Origami Swamp Robot System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23813)
*Lan, T.,Babgei, A.,Romanello, L.,Kovac, M.,Armanini, S. F.*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSS：采用3D打印折纸轮的紧凑两栖机器人平台，通过曲柄机构同时改变轮宽、体径与鳍片朝向，在压实地面收缩滚动、在软湿地/水面展开增大接触，实现跨陆-泥-水连续运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿地与沼泽环境监测需在陆地与水域间切换，固定几何轮无法自适应调整接触几何，导致软地沉陷、打滑与高能耗。

Method: 设计紧凑曲柄连杆式折纸轮变形机构（收缩/展开双模态，同步改变宽度、体径和鳍片方向）；在土壤、不同含水率泥地、沙地和水面做牵引/能耗评测，测试动态变形速度、陆水过渡、打滑与轮毂沉陷，并进行户外自然地形试验。

Result: 最优构型依地形而定：收缩轮在硬土与较干泥地运输代价更低，展开轮在湿泥、沙、水中降低运输代价最多29%，并减少湿泥打滑及沙地打滑与轮毂沉陷；构型切换耗时1.95±0.11 s，可连续跨越土壤-泥-水过渡带，户外试验验证有效。

Conclusion: 自适应轮体几何可带来地形相关的运动收益，使单一紧凑平台胜任差异显著的陆域与水域工况。

Abstract: Robotic systems for environmental monitoring and ecological surveying in wetlands and swamps must operate across diverse terrestrial and aquatic conditions. Conventional fixed-geometry wheels cannot adapt their contact geometry to these varying conditions. This work presents MOSS (Multimodal Origami Swamp Robot System), a compact amphibious robotic platform with 3D-printed origami wheels. The compact crank mechanism switches the wheel between contracted and expanded configurations, changing its width, body diameter, and fin orientation simultaneously. The contracted configuration is better suited to efficient rolling on firmer ground, whereas the expanded configuration provides greater contact and terrain engagement on softer surfaces. MOSS was evaluated on soil, sand, mud with varying water content, and water, in addition to in-motion morphing and terrestrial-aquatic transition tests. The more energy-efficient wheel configuration depended on terrain. The contracted configuration achieved a lower cost of transport on firmer soil and mud, whereas the expanded configuration performed better on wetter mud, sand, and water, reducing the cost of transport by up to 29%. Expanded wheels also reduced slip in wet mud and reduced both slip and hub sinkage in sand. MOSS successfully completed configuration changes in $1.95 \pm 0.11$ s and demonstrated continuous locomotion across soil-mud-water transitions. Outdoor trials further demonstrated operation across natural deformable terrain and water. These results show that adaptive wheel geometry provides terrain-dependent locomotion benefits and enables a single compact platform to traverse substantially different terrestrial and aquatic conditions.

</details>


### [181] [Blind Precise Assembly: Proprioceptive Sim-to-Real Peg-in-Hole for Diverse Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23849)
*Bronars, Antonia,Park, Younghyo,Kedia, Kushal,Agrawal, Pulkit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Training blind assembly policies that use only proprioceptive joint history (no vision/force/tactile) to perform peg insertion, gear meshing, and nut threading; 73% zero-shot real-world peg insertion success, with a procedural MuJoCo assembly generator released.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Legged robots use joint sensing for contact-rich locomotion, but it is unclear whether proprioception alone suffices for millimeter-precision contact-rich assembly (finding openings, meshing teeth, threading nuts) where contacts are light and small. The paper asks whether joint-position/velocity histories plus actions can substitute for visual, force/torque, or tactile sensing under unknown fixture/part poses and randomized grasps.

Method: Reinforcement learning in simulation over procedurally generated, randomized parts, grasps, and contacts; policies observe only a short history of joint positions, velocities, and their own actions. Controlled simulation ablations compare blind policies against matched visual policies. An open-source generator builds thousands of exact-convex-piece assemblies for MuJoCo with clearance preserved, claimed more accurate and faster than convex decomposition or MuJoCo signed-distance collision.

Result: Blind policies learn peg insertion, gear meshing, and nut threading in simulation. Peg insertion transfers zero-shot to hardware with 73% success across varied peg shapes/sizes. Simulation comparisons show blind policies generalize substantially better to unseen part shapes and sizes than visual policies, though they are slower.

Conclusion: Proprioception-only joint sensing is a viable route to precise contact-rich assembly, with better shape/size generalization and real-world zero-shot transfer, at some cost in speed; the released procedural generator enables training at the scale of thousands of clearance-preserving assemblies.

Abstract: Every robot already carries high-resolution contact sensors: its own joints. Legged robots and humanoids exploit them to walk, run, and track whole-body motions without vision, but there the contacts are large and forceful. Is joint sensing also enough for precise, contact-rich assembly, where the robot must find a millimeter-scale opening, mesh gear teeth, or catch a thread through light contact? We study this question by training blind assembly policies that observe only a short history of joint positions, velocities, and their own actions, without visual, force, torque, or tactile sensing. The poses of both the fixture and the part are unknown to the policy, and the grasp is randomized substantially. The policy must therefore assemble the parts through contact, sensed exclusively through its own proprioception. Trained with reinforcement learning entirely in simulation over randomized parts, grasps, and contacts, such policies learn peg insertion, gear meshing, and nut threading. The peg insertion policy transfers zero-shot to the real world and inserts pegs of different shapes and sizes in 73% of trials. Controlled comparisons with matched visual policies in simulation show that blind policies generalize far better to unseen part shapes and sizes, though they can be slower. Training such policies needs thousands of different assemblies, each simulated with its clearance intact, so we also release an open-source generator that builds them procedurally from exact convex pieces for MuJoCo; it is more accurate and faster than convex decomposition or MuJoCo's signed-distance collision. Videos and interactive simulations are at https://younghyopark.me/blind-proprioceptive-assembly/.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [mmPencil: Toward Writing-Style-Independent In-Air Handwriting Recognition via mmWave Radar and Large Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23419)
*Guo, Yifan,Wang, Zhu,Qin, Qian,Lei, Yangqian,Gan, Qiwen,Sun, Zhuo,Chen, Chao,Guo, Bin,Yu, Zhiwen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: mmPencil用毫米波雷达重建3D空中手写轨迹，并通过空间到视觉映射让视觉语言模型识别200类词，见用户97.6%、未见用户零样本92.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RF信号（如WiFi、毫米波）的空中手写识别受限于预设书写平面、受限书写风格和小词汇量，难以支撑自然的人机交互。

Method: 提出mm²VLMs模态映射架构：多阶段空间轨迹重建算法从毫米波信号恢复3D手写轨迹，再经空间到视觉映射算法渲染为视觉表示，输入大视觉语言模型进行词级识别；同时构建并公开含12名用户、22种书写场景、200词、7,664样本的3D毫米波手写数据集。

Result: 在书写位置、距离、角度、速度、尺寸、方向和用户移动等变化下保持鲁棒；200类已见用户准确率97.60%，50类未见用户零样本准确率92.50%，优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: RF感知与VLM之间的模态映射可有效实现免接触、无书写平面约束的3D空中手写识别，具有较强泛化能力。

Abstract: In-air handwriting recognition based on radio frequency (RF) signals offers a promising solution for human-computer interaction, yet existing methods are often limited by predefined writing planes, constrained writing styles, and small recognition vocabularies. To address these limitations, we propose m^2VLMs, a modality mapping architecture that bridges millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar sensing and large vision-language models (VLMs), and develop mmPencil, a 3D in-air handwriting word recognition system. mmPencil reconstructs 3D handwriting trajectories from mmWave signals using a multi-stage spatial trajectory reconstruction algorithm and maps them into visual representations through a spatial-to-visual mapping algorithm, enabling VLMs to recognize handwriting while reducing sensitivity to writing styles and environmental factors. We collect and release a 3D mmWave handwriting dataset containing 7,664 samples of 200 words from 12 users across 22 writing scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that mmPencil achieves robust word recognition under variations in writing position, range, angle, speed, size, direction, and user movement. It achieves 97.60% accuracy on 200 word classes for seen users and 92.50% zero-shot accuracy on 50 word classes for unseen users, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [SpikeQ: Spike-Guided Post-Training Quantization for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23046)
*Sun, Kai,Duan, Peibo,Yan, Zhanglu,Zhang, Guowei,Huang, Yongsheng,Kuhlmann, Levin*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: SpikeQ提出脉冲引导的PTQ框架：权重按下游神经元脉冲误差逐层选级，膜电位针对芯片实际的"预发放"量化构建将阈值置于舍入边界的reset-aware网格；7个基准上2-bit预发放膜电位下现有网格全部崩溃而SpikeQ保持大部分精度，4/4-bit下内存缩小7.8-7.9×、能耗降低6.4-6.9×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态芯片内存受限，SNN需低比特权重与膜电位；现有PTQ沿用ANN思路最小化MSE等数值误差，但SNN中量化误差仅通过被翻转的脉冲影响后续层，等MSE误差可造成截然不同的精度损失，而翻转相同比例脉冲的误差损失相当。

Method: 1) 权重量化：模拟下游神经元、以最小化脉冲误差为目标为每个权重选择量化级；2) 膜电位量化：针对芯片实际存储的pre-fire（发放前）膜电位而非reset后状态，构造reset-aware量化网格，使发放阈值落在舍入边界上，从而当前时间步的脉冲永不被舍入翻转。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割、目标检测共7个基准上评估：2-bit pre-fire膜电位下所有现有网格失效，SpikeQ保留大部分全精度精度；权重与膜电位均为4-bit时精度损失在2.59点内，内存缩小7.8–7.9倍，能耗降低6.4–6.9倍。

Conclusion: 对SNN的PTQ应以"脉冲翻转比例"而非MSE作为误差度量，并需匹配硬件的pre-fire比较机理，可实现免重训练的低比特神经形态部署。

Abstract: On neuromorphic chips, spiking neural networks (SNNs) consume far less energy than artificial neural networks (ANNs), but the limited memory of these chips requires low-bit synaptic weights and membrane potentials. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods provide them without retraining and, like those for ANNs, minimize numerical errors such as the mean squared error (MSE). In an SNN, however, a quantization error reaches later layers only through the spikes it flips. We find that errors of equal MSE can cause very different accuracy losses, whereas errors that flip the same fraction of spikes cause the same loss. We therefore propose SpikeQ, a spike-guided PTQ framework. For weights, we select the level of each weight by simulating the downstream neurons and minimizing their spike errors. For membrane potentials, most existing methods quantize the state after the reset, whereas neuromorphic chips compare the threshold with the membrane potential stored at a fixed bit width. We target this pre-fire quantization and build a reset-aware grid that places the threshold on a rounding boundary, so rounding never flips the spike at the current time step. We evaluate SpikeQ on seven benchmarks of image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection. With 2-bit pre-fire membrane potentials, every existing grid collapses, whereas SpikeQ retains most of the full-precision accuracy. With both at 4 bits, the models stay within 2.59 points of full precision, shrink their memory footprint 7.8--7.9 times, and use 6.4--6.9 times less energy.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [A Unified Theory of Image Scaling: Point Spread Functions, Discrete Kernels, and Scale-Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22472)
*Shocher, Assaf*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文从相机点扩散函数(PSF)出发，推导出满足Huygens式一致性原理（每像素为次级光源）的"光学对齐"图像缩放算子族K_s，闭式给出、可复合，且上下采样互为Moore-Penrose伪逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出常规图像缩放既非光学忠实也非内部一致：抗锯齿规则通常不对应任何真实相机，两次1/2缩放≠一次1/4缩放，高斯尺度空间核也无法成为有限孔径PSF。因此需要一个由相机光学严格导出、双向可逆的缩放理论框架。

Method: 把PSF视为算子K_0，通过Huygens式一致性原理推导缩放算子K_s的闭式表达；证明复合律K_{s'}∘K_s=K_{ss'}（下-上采样需带通投影）以及K_{1/s}=K_s^†；由此得到采样图像的广义尺度空间（非限于高斯），并推广到可复合可逆的亚像素几何变换；用真实拍摄与含噪光学仿真进行验证。

Result: 实验验证了模型的光学预测与真实相机、噪声光学仿真一致；并显示该缩放方案能提升超分辨率与分类等下游任务的稳定性。

Conclusion: 图像缩放应当由相机光学严格定义：所提出的广义（非高斯、双向、可逆）尺度空间是经典高斯尺度空间的推广，统一了缩放与可逆亚像素几何变换，对超分、分类等任务具有实际价值。

Abstract: We derive the optically aligned system of image-scaling operators $\mathbf{K}_s$ that reproduces optical zoom (exact zoom-out, canonical zoom-in), by identifying a Huygens-like consistency principle in which each pixel acts as a secondary source. The system is determined in closed form by the camera's point spread function (PSF), itself the operator $\mathbf{K}_0$. The operators compose, $\mathbf{K}_{s'} \circ \mathbf{K}_s = \mathbf{K}_{ss'}$ (with band projection for down-up), and account for both upscaling and downscaling, which form a Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse pair, $\mathbf{K}_{1/s} = \mathbf{K}_s^\dagger$. We show that image scaling, as routinely practiced, is neither optically faithful nor internally consistent; for example, common anti-aliasing rules generally correspond to no camera, and two resizes by a factor of $1/2$ generally do not equal one resize by a factor of $1/4$. Gaussian scale-space kernels cannot be exact finite-aperture PSFs. Gaussian scale-space is an idealized special case of what emerges here: a generalized scale-space for sampled images, derived entirely from camera optics, bidirectional, and not limited to Gaussian kernels. The system goes beyond scaling and supports composable and invertible subpixel geometric transformations. We empirically validate its optical predictions with real captures and noisy optics simulations, and demonstrate its practical impact on the stability of tasks such as super-resolution and classification.

</details>


### [185] [LGGS: Longitudinal MRI Reconstruction via Guided Group Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22643)
*Janjušević, Nikola,Chandarana, Hersh,Wang, Yao,Feng, Li*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出纵向引导组稀疏（LGGS）框架，用相位不变循环注意力把既往扫描信息注入MRI重建的正则项，实现可解释、少参数的快速纵向MRI重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列MRI扫描时间长、成本高；同一患者既往扫描与当前检查高度相关却很少被利用，难点在于跨访视配准误差、病灶变化和相位差异。

Method: 从经典组稀疏出发，构建可解释的深度学习重建框架：以学习到的相位不变循环注意力在既往图像与当前k空间/图像间匹配对应特征，将先验扫描嵌入重建正则项，从而对平面内/平面外局部错配及访视间相位变化具鲁棒性；并分析多引导层与相位不变相似度贡献。

Result: 在纵向T1加权多线圈脑MRI各加速率下达到SOTA，优于无引导及纵向引导基线，且可学习参数显著更少；加速采集的既往图像同样可作为有效引导，质量退化极小。

Conclusion: 将组稀疏先验与纵向引导结合是提升加速MRI重建的有效且可解释途径，能稳健处理配准与相位变化，并降低参数需求。

Abstract: Serial MRI is routinely performed in current clinical practice for disease surveillance and applications such as MR-guided radiotherapy. However, long MRI scan times increase costs and limit patient access, making accelerated image reconstruction of broad clinical importance. Previous scans of the same patient contain image information highly correlated with the current exam, yet existing reconstruction methods rarely exploit this information. Major challenges in the effective use of prior scans include inter-visit misregistration, pathological changes, and image phase variation. In this work, we propose Longitudinal Guided Group Sparsity (LGGS), an interpretable deep-learning framework for longitudinally informed MRI reconstruction derived from classical group sparsity. The prior scan is incorporated into the reconstruction regularizer through learned phase-invariant circulant attention that identifies corresponding features across exams. This formulation provides robustness to local in-plane and through-plane misregistration as well as inter-visit phase variation. Across acceleration rates, LGGS achieves state-of-the-art performance on longitudinal T1-weighted multi-coil brain MRI data, outperforming the evaluated unguided and longitudinally informed reconstruction methods, with substantially fewer learned parameters. We further evaluated the contributions of multiple guide slices and phase-invariant similarity and demonstrated that accelerated prior images can also serve as effective guides with minimal degradation in reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [186] [Measurement-Adaptive Light-Cone Transform for Robust Non-Line-of-Sight Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22847)
*Wang, Lianfang,Liu, Xueying,Dong, Junnan,Zhang, Qinghua,Liu, Jun,Wang, Yong,Duan, Yuping*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: MALCT：物理引导的测量自适应光锥变换框架，在保留LCT傅里叶域反演结构的同时，用测量条件加权融合解析响应与学习响应，并预测频率相关正则化图，提升NLOS重建鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际NLOS测量受噪声和偏离理想光传输的影响，传统光锥变换（LCT）重建质量下降，需要兼顾物理可解释性与数据驱动适应性的方法。

Method: 统一变分框架下，将LCT解析响应与共享学习响应通过测量条件加权结合；预测测量条件依赖的频率相关正则化图；引入注意力增强的时空特征提取模块捕捉长程时间依赖与局部空间相关性，以支持低信号条件重建。

Result: 合成数据与多种真实NLOS系统测量上重建质量提升；受控实验验证对时间响应展宽和采集时间缩短的鲁棒性；在低至32×32空间采样网格下仍能保持场景结构。

Conclusion: MALCT以物理引导的自适应方式改进非视域成像重建，在有限采集预算下具备实用潜力，但属特定成像模态的方法改进。

Abstract: Practical non-line-of-sight (NLOS) measurements are affected by noise and deviations from idealized light transport, which can degrade reconstruction with conventional light-cone transform (LCT) methods. We propose the Measurement-Adaptive Light-Cone Transform (MALCT), a physics-guided framework that retains the efficient Fourier-domain inversion structure of LCT. {{Within a unified variational formulation, MALCT combines the analytical response with a shared learned response through measurement-conditioned weighting and predicts a measurement-conditioned, frequency-dependent regularization map.}} An attention-enhanced spatio-temporal feature extraction module captures long-range temporal dependencies and local spatial correlations to support reconstruction under low-signal conditions. Experiments on synthetic data and real measurements from different NLOS systems demonstrate improved reconstruction quality. Controlled evaluations further assess robustness to temporal-response broadening and reduced acquisition time. MALCT also preserves scene structures with spatial sampling grids down to $32\times32$, supporting its potential for NLOS imaging under limited acquisition budgets.

</details>


### [187] [Stabilizing Illumination Conditioning in SSM-Based Low-Light Image Enhancement with Bottleneck Prompt Sidecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22958)
*Ng, Wei Chen,Xie, Qiyi,Tan, Jian Ding,Lee, Yan Kang,Abdullah, Abdulrab Hakim Qaid,Tee, Wei Hown*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 在SSM/Mamba类低光照增强骨干的瓶颈处插入轻量PromptAdapter，将单通道光照图转为提示令牌并以残差旁路注入，LOL三数据集PSNR提升约0.9–1.2 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实低光照图像存在空间变化光照、噪声放大、色彩失真与细节丢失，仅靠亮度增强不足；SSM/Mamba类恢复器虽有高效长程建模，但如何把光照先验安全地引入此类骨干仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 轻量光照估计器预测单通道光照图，映射为prompt token，经瓶颈侧残差旁路适配器（sidecar）注入，不改动状态空间核心；在LOL-v1、LOLv2-Real、LOLv2-Synthetic上采用锁定三随机种子评估协议，并设置Learned-Fixed对照消融。

Result: PSNR相对Base提升0.94/1.24/0.93 dB，SSIM、LPIPS及暗区掩码指标同步改善，LOLv2-Real真实数据证据最清晰；Learned-Fixed对照显示增益更多源于旁路条件化通路本身，而非严格意义上的光照条件化优越性，LOLv2-Synthetic结论受稳定性条件限制。

Conclusion: 在SSM类低光照增强中，条件化机制的设计可能与条件信号本身同等重要；负责任的消融与"稳定性条件化"表述值得肯定，但任务域距核心关注方向较远。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) requires more than brightness amplification because real low-light images often exhibit spatially varying illumination, amplified noise, color distortion, and local detail loss. Recent SSM- and Mamba-based restorers provide efficient long-range modeling, yet the route for introducing illumination priors into such backbones remains insufficiently studied. This study addresses safe illumination conditioning for SSM-based LLIE through a bottleneck-side PromptAdapter. A lightweight illumination estimator first predicts a single-channel illumination map, which is then converted into prompt tokens and injected through a residual sidecar adapter at the bottleneck, thereby avoiding direct modification of the state-space core. Under a locked three-seed evaluation protocol on LOL-v1, LOLv2-Real, and LOLv2-Synthetic, the PromptAdapter improves over the Base model by 0.94 dB, 1.24 dB, and 0.93 dB in PSNR, respectively, while also improving SSIM, LPIPS, and masked dark-region metrics. The clearest real-data evidence appears on LOLv2-Real. A Learned-Fixed control on LOL-v1 indicates that the present results support the utility of the sidecar conditioning pathway more strongly than a strict claim of illumination-conditioned superiority, and the LOLv2-Synthetic result remains stability-conditioned. These findings suggest that, in SSM-based LLIE, conditioning design can be as important as the conditioning signal itself.

</details>


### [188] [TransNSNet: A Transformer-Enhanced Multi-Scale Adversarial Framework for CT-to-PET Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23325)
*Li, Shaojun,Deng, Xiaoyu,Keshi, Ikuo*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出TransNSNet（Transformer增强的多尺度对抗框架）实现CT→PET医学图像合成，在NSCLC配对数据上对比Pix2Pix/NSNet-Base/NSNet-D，10项指标中9项均值最优但统计上未显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索用跨模态生成网络从CT合成类PET图像，以降低PET采集成本与辐射暴露；并引入Transformer上下文交互提升多尺度对抗生成质量，同时控制参数量。

Method: 基于公开NSCLC放射基因组数据构建配对CT-PET数据集（患者筛选、层面匹配、预处理、患者级划分）；多空间尺度生成PET样预测并施加粗到细重建监督；TransPatchGAN在判别器中引入上下文特征交互；采用渐进式消融比较Pix2Pix、NSNet-Base、NSNet-D与TransNSNet，固定收敛窗口（epoch 80–101）汇总指标，并做患者级自适应配对分析与Holm校正。

Result: 10项图像级指标中9项均值最优，NSNet-Base在视觉显著度诱导指标上最高；固定比例高强度区域上NSNet-D的层面召回与平衡准确率略高，但等权重患者级指标几乎一致，最高Dice转向TransNSNet；差异均小于患者间变异；n=10自适应患者级分析经Holm校正后无显著成对差异；参数量显著小于原始next-scale模型。

Conclusion: 结果显示有潜力的图像质量趋势，但不足以证明统计优越性或临床有效性，合成PET样图像不能替代临床采集PET；属医学影像域内方法学改进，无机器人与具身智能相关可迁移洞见。

Abstract: This study investigates CT-to-PET image synthesis using TransNSNet, a Transformer-enhanced multi-scale adversarial framework. A paired CT-PET dataset was curated from the public non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) Radiogenomics collection through patient screening, slice-level matching, preprocessing, and patient-level train-test separation. The proposed framework generates PET-like predictions at multiple spatial scales and applies coarse-to-fine reconstruction supervision, while TransPatchGAN introduces contextual feature interaction into adversarial evaluation. A progressive ablation study compares Pix2Pix, NSNet-Base, NSNet-D, and TransNSNet. Image-level performance was summarized over a fixed convergence window (epochs 80-101) from one training run per model. TransNSNet achieved the best mean value in nine of ten metrics, whereas NSNet-Base achieved the highest visual saliency-induced index. For fixed-fraction high-intensity regions, NSNet-D was marginally higher in slice-level recall and balanced accuracy, but the equal-weight patient-level values were nearly identical and the highest Dice values shifted to TransNSNet. These differences were small relative to between-patient variability. In the separate adaptive patient-level analysis (n=10), no pairwise model difference remained statistically significant after Holm correction. TransNSNet also had a substantially smaller parameter footprint than the original next-scale models. These findings indicate promising image-quality trends, but they do not establish statistical superiority or clinical validity; synthesized PET-like images should not be interpreted as substitutes for clinically acquired PET.

</details>
